== 5. MIDDLEWARES
============================================================

--- 5.1 Fichiers ---
AuthToken.php
CheckRole.php

--- 5.2 Contenu de chaque middleware ---
>>> app/Http/Middleware/AuthToken.php
     1	<?php
     2	
     3	namespace App\Http\Middleware;
     4	
     5	use App\Models\User;
     6	use Closure;
     7	use Illuminate\Http\Request;
     8	
     9	class AuthToken
    10	{
    11	    public function handle(Request $request, Closure $next)
    12	    {
    13	        $token = $request->bearerToken();
    14	
    15	        if (!$token) {
    16	            return response()->json(['error' => 'Non authentifie'], 401);
    17	        }
    18	
    19	        $user = User::where('token', $token)
    20	            ->where('token_expires_at', '>', now())
    21	            ->first();
    22	
    23	        if (!$user) {
    24	            return response()->json(['error' => 'Session expiree'], 401);
    25	        }
    26	
    27	        $request->setUserResolver(fn() => $user);
    28	
    29	        return $next($request);
    30	    }
    31	}
>>> app/Http/Middleware/CheckRole.php
     1	<?php
     2	
     3	namespace App\Http\Middleware;
     4	
     5	use Closure;
     6	use Illuminate\Http\Request;
     7	
     8	class CheckRole
     9	{
    10	    public function handle(Request $request, Closure $next, ...$roles)
    11	    {
    12	        if (!$request->user() || !in_array($request->user()->role, $roles)) {
    13	            return response()->json(['error' => 'Accès refusé. Rôle requis : ' . implode(' ou ', $roles)], 403);
    14	        }
    15	
    16	        return $next($request);
    17	    }
    18	}

>>> app/Http/Controllers/Api/AgentController.php
     1	<?php
     2	namespace App\Http\Controllers\Api;
     3	use App\Http\Controllers\Controller;
     4	use App\Models\User;
     5	use App\Models\Debiteur;
     6	use App\Models\Vente;
     7	use App\Models\AuditLog;
     8	use Illuminate\Http\Request;
     9	use Illuminate\Support\Str;
    10	use Illuminate\Support\Facades\DB;
    11	
    12	class AgentController extends Controller
    13	{
    14	    public function index()
    15	    {
    16	        return response()->json(User::where('role', 'agent')->orderBy('nom')->get(['uuid','nom','prenom','email','telephone','actif','created_at']));
    17	    }
    18	
    19	    public function show(string $uuid)
    20	    {
    21	        $agent = User::where('uuid', $uuid)->where('role', 'agent')->firstOrFail();
    22	        $agent->load('debiteurs');
    23	        return response()->json($agent);
    24	    }
    25	
    26	    public function store(Request $request)
    27	    {
    28	        $request->validate(['nom' => 'required','prenom' => 'required','email' => 'required|email|unique:users']);
    29	        $agent = User::create([
    30	            'uuid' => Str::uuid(), 'nom' => $request->nom, 'prenom' => $request->prenom,
    31	            'email' => $request->email, 'telephone' => $request->telephone ?? '',
    32	            'role' => 'agent', 'password' => bcrypt($request->password ?? 'agent123'),
    33	        ]);
    34	        return response()->json(['success' => true, 'uuid' => $agent->uuid], 201);
    35	    }
    36	
    37	    public function update(Request $request, string $uuid)
    38	    {
    39	        $agent = User::where('uuid', $uuid)->where('role', 'agent')->firstOrFail();
    40	        $data = $request->only(['nom','prenom','email','telephone']);
    41	        if ($request->password) { $data['password'] = bcrypt($request->password); }
    42	        $agent->update($data);
    43	        return response()->json(['success' => true]);
    44	    }
    45	
    46	    public function destroy(Request $request, string $uuid)
    47	    {
    48	        $agent = User::where('uuid', $uuid)->where('role', 'agent')->firstOrFail();
    49	        $transferToUuid = $request->transfer_to;
    50	        $gerante = $request->user();
    51	        $nbTransferes = 0;
    52	
    53	        DB::transaction(function () use ($agent, $transferToUuid, $gerante, &$nbTransferes) {
    54	            if ($transferToUuid) {
    55	                $newAgent = User::where('uuid', $transferToUuid)->where('role', 'agent')->firstOrFail();
    56	
    57	                $debiteurs = Debiteur::where('agent_id', $agent->id)->get();
    58	                foreach ($debiteurs as $debiteur) {
    59	                    // Calculer le taux de recouvrement et le reste à payer
    60	                    $ventes = Vente::where('debiteur_id', $debiteur->id)->where('statut', 'en_cours')->get();
    61	                    $totalRestant = 0;
    62	                    $totalDu = 0;
    63	                    foreach ($ventes as $vente) {
    64	                        $totalDu += $vente->montant_total;
    65	                        $totalRestant += $vente->resteAPayer();
    66	                    }
    67	                    $tauxRecouvrement = $totalDu > 0 ? round((($totalDu - $totalRestant) / $totalDu) * 100, 2) : 100;
    68	
    69	                    $debiteur->update([
    70	                        'agent_id' => $newAgent->id,
    71	                        'ancien_agent_id' => $agent->id,
    72	                        'date_transfert' => now(),
    73	                        'taux_recouvrement_au_transfert' => $tauxRecouvrement,
    74	                        'reste_a_payer_au_transfert' => $totalRestant,
    75	                    ]);
    76	                    $nbTransferes++;
    77	                }
    78	
    79	                // Créer l'audit log
    80	                AuditLog::create([
    81	                    'gerante_id' => $gerante->id,
    82	                    'ancien_agent_id' => $agent->id,
    83	                    'nouvel_agent_id' => $newAgent->id,
    84	                    'nb_debiteurs_transferes' => $nbTransferes,
    85	                    'details' => [
    86	                        'ancien_agent' => $agent->nom . ' ' . $agent->prenom,
    87	                        'nouvel_agent' => $newAgent->nom . ' ' . $newAgent->prenom,
    88	                        'date' => now()->toDateTimeString(),
    89	                    ],
    90	                ]);
    91	            }
    92	
    93	            // Désactiver l'agent
    94	            $agent->update(['actif' => false, 'token' => null, 'token_expires_at' => null]);
    95	        });
    96	
    97	        return response()->json([
    98	            'success' => true,
    99	            'nb_transferes' => $nbTransferes,
   100	            'message' => "Agent désactivé. $nbTransferes débiteur(s) transféré(s)."
   101	        ]);
   102	    }
   103	
   104	    // Historique complet d'un agent (export)
   105	    public function exportHistory(string $uuid)
   106	    {
   107	        $agent = User::where('uuid', $uuid)->where('role', 'agent')->firstOrFail();
   108	        $debiteurs = Debiteur::where('agent_id', $agent->id)->with('ventes.recouvrements')->get();
   109	
   110	        $data = [];
   111	        foreach ($debiteurs as $d) {
   112	            foreach ($d->ventes as $v) {
   113	                $data[] = [
   114	                    'Débiteur' => $d->nom . ' ' . $d->prenom,
   115	                    'Téléphone' => $d->telephone,
   116	                    'Quartier' => $d->quartier,
   117	                    'Vente' => $v->uuid,
   118	                    'Montant total' => $v->montant_total,
   119	                    'Payé' => $v->totalPaye(),
   120	                    'Restant' => $v->resteAPayer(),
   121	                    'Statut' => $v->statut,
   122	                ];
   123	            }
   124	        }
   125	
   126	        return response()->json([
   127	            'agent' => $agent->nom . ' ' . $agent->prenom,
   128	            'nb_debiteurs' => $debiteurs->count(),
   129	            'donnees' => $data,
   130	        ]);
   131	    }
   132	}
>>> app/Http/Controllers/Api/AgentSpaceController.php
     1	<?php
     2	
     3	namespace App\Http\Controllers\Api;
     4	
     5	use App\Http\Controllers\Controller;
     6	use App\Models\Vente;
     7	use App\Models\Recouvrement;
     8	use App\Models\Echeance;
     9	use App\Models\TourneeDemarrage;
    10	use App\Services\PaiementService;
    11	use App\Services\CaisseService;
    12	use App\Services\ComptabiliteService;
    13	use App\Services\NotificationService;
    14	use Illuminate\Http\Request;
    15	use Illuminate\Support\Str;
    16	
    17	class AgentSpaceController extends Controller
    18	{
    19	    public function tournee(Request $request)
    20	    {
    21	        $user = $request->user();
    22	        $aujourdhui = now()->toDateString();
    23	        $query = Vente::where('statut', 'en_cours')
    24	            ->whereDate('date_debut', '<=', $aujourdhui)
    25	            ->whereDate('date_fin', '>=', $aujourdhui)
    26	            ->with(['debiteur', 'echeances' => fn($q) => $q->whereDate('date_echeance', $aujourdhui)]);
    27	
    28	        if ($user->role === 'agent') {
    29	            $query->where('agent_id', $user->id);
    30	        }
    31	
    32	        $ventesModeles = $query->get();
    33	
    34	        $ventes = $ventesModeles->map(function($v) use ($aujourdhui) {
    35	            $echeance = $v->echeances->first();
    36	            $enRetard = $v->echeances()
    37	                ->whereDate('date_echeance', '<', $aujourdhui)
    38	                ->whereNotIn('statut', ['paye'])
    39	                ->exists();
    40	
    41	            $joursTotal = max(1, $v->nombre_jours);
    42	            $joursPayes = $v->echeances()->where('statut', 'paye')->count();
    43	
    44	            return [
    45	                'uuid' => $v->uuid,
    46	                'debiteur_uuid' => $v->debiteur?->uuid,
    47	                'debiteur' => $v->debiteur?->nom.' '.$v->debiteur?->prenom,
    48	                'telephone' => $v->debiteur?->telephone,
    49	                'quartier' => $v->debiteur?->quartier,
    50	                'montant_journalier' => $echeance?->montant_prevu ?? $v->montant_journalier,
    51	                'reste_a_payer' => $v->resteAPayer(),
    52	                'statut_visite' => $echeance?->statut ?? 'en_attente',
    53	                'statut_global' => $enRetard ? 'en_retard' : 'a_jour',
    54	                'epargne_par_jour' => $v->epargne_par_jour,
    55	                'penalite_par_jour' => $v->penalite_par_jour,
    56	                'jours_payes' => $joursPayes,
    57	                'jours_total' => $joursTotal,
    58	                'peut_encaisser' => $v->echeances()
    59	                    ->whereIn('statut', ['en_retard', 'partiel', 'en_attente'])
    60	                    ->whereDate('date_echeance', '<=', $aujourdhui)
    61	                    ->exists(),
    62	            ];
    63	        });
    64	
    65	        $aVisiter = $ventes->whereIn('statut_visite', ['en_attente', 'en_retard'])->values();
    66	        $paye = $ventes->where('statut_visite', 'paye')->values();
    67	        $absent = $ventes->where('statut_visite', 'absent')->values();
    68	        $refus = $ventes->where('statut_visite', 'refus')->values();
    69	        $promesse = $ventes->where('statut_visite', 'promesse')->values();
    70	
    71	        $venteIds = $ventesModeles->pluck('id');
    72	
    73	        // Montant collecté AUJOURD'HUI : uniquement les échéances du jour payées
    74	        $montantCollecteAujourdhui = $ventesModeles->filter(function($v) use ($aujourdhui) {
    75	            $echeanceJour = $v->echeances->first();
    76	            return $echeanceJour && $echeanceJour->statut === 'paye';
    77	        })->sum(function($v) {
    78	            return $v->echeances->first()->montant_paye;
    79	        });
    80	
    81	        $nbRetard = Vente::whereIn('id', $venteIds)
    82	            ->whereHas('echeances', fn($q) => $q->whereDate('date_echeance', '<', $aujourdhui)->whereIn('statut', ['en_retard', 'partiel']))
    83	            ->distinct('debiteur_id')
    84	            ->count('debiteur_id');
    85	
    86	        $soldeImpaye = $ventesModeles->sum(fn($v) => $v->resteAPayer());
    87	
    88	        $montantRetard = Echeance::whereIn('vente_id', $venteIds)
    89	            ->whereDate('date_echeance', '<', $aujourdhui)
    90	            ->whereIn('statut', ['en_retard', 'partiel'])
    91	            ->selectRaw('SUM(montant_prevu - montant_paye) as total')
    92	            ->value('total') ?? 0;
    93	
    94	        return response()->json([
    95	            'a_visiter' => $aVisiter,
    96	            'paye' => $paye,
    97	            'absent' => $absent,
    98	            'refus' => $refus,
    99	            'promesse' => $promesse,
   100	            'stats' => [
   101	                'nb_total' => $ventes->count(),
   102	                'nb_paye' => $paye->count(),
   103	                'nb_absent' => $absent->count(),
   104	                'nb_refus' => $refus->count(),
   105	                'nb_promesse' => $promesse->count(),
   106	                'nb_a_visiter' => $aVisiter->count(),
   107	                'nb_retard' => $nbRetard,
   108	                'montant_attendu' => $ventes->sum('montant_journalier'),
   109	                'montant_collecte_aujourdhui' => $montantCollecteAujourdhui,
   110	                'solde_impaye' => $soldeImpaye,
   111	                'montant_retard' => $montantRetard,
   112	            ]
   113	        ]);
   114	    }
   115	
   116	    public function encaisser(Request $request)
   117	    {
   118	        $request->validate([
   119	            'vente_id' => 'required|exists:ventes,uuid',
   120	            'montant' => 'required|numeric|min:0',
   121	            'mode_paiement' => 'required|in:especes,mobile,autre',
   122	            'statut' => 'required|in:paye,partiel,absent,refus,promesse',
   123	            'commentaire' => 'nullable|string|required_if:statut,absent,refus,promesse',
   124	            'sync_uuid' => 'nullable|string|max:36',
   125	        ]);
   126	
   127	        $user = $request->user();
   128	        $aujourdhui = now()->toDateString();
   129	        $tourneeEnCours = TourneeDemarrage::where('agent_id', $user->id)
   130	            ->where('date_tournee', $aujourdhui)
   131	            ->where('statut', 'en_cours')
   132	            ->exists();
   133	
   134	        if (!$tourneeEnCours) {
   135	            return response()->json(['error' => 'Démarrez votre tournée avant d\'encaisser.'], 422);
   136	        }
   137	
   138	        // Idempotence : retry offline avec le même sync_uuid -> ne jamais dupliquer
   139	        if ($request->sync_uuid) {
   140	            $existant = Recouvrement::where('sync_uuid', $request->sync_uuid)->first();
   141	            if ($existant) {
   142	                return response()->json(['success' => true, 'deja_traite' => true]);
   143	            }
   144	        }
   145	
   146	        // Garde-fou : un paiement paye/partiel doit avoir un montant > 0
   147	        if (in_array($request->statut, ['paye', 'partiel']) && $request->montant <= 0) {
   148	            return response()->json(['error' => 'Le montant doit être supérieur à 0 pour un paiement payé ou partiel.'], 422);
   149	        }
   150	
   151	        $vente = Vente::where('uuid', $request->vente_id)->firstOrFail();
   152	
   153	        $rec = Recouvrement::create([
   154	            'uuid' => Str::uuid(),
   155	            'sync_uuid' => $request->sync_uuid,
   156	            'vente_id' => $vente->id,
   157	            'agent_id' => $vente->debiteur->agent_id ?? $user->id,
   158	            'montant' => $request->montant,
   159	            'date_recouvrement' => now()->toDateString(),
   160	            'heure_recouvrement' => now()->toTimeString(),
   161	            'mode_paiement' => $request->mode_paiement,
   162	            'statut' => $request->statut,
   163	            'commentaire' => $request->commentaire,
   164	            'synced' => true,
   165	        ]);
   166	
   167	        PaiementService::affecterPaiement($rec);
   168	
   169	        if (in_array($request->statut, ['paye', 'partiel']) && $request->montant > 0) {
   170	            CaisseService::enregistrerRecouvrement($rec);
   171	            ComptabiliteService::ecrireRecouvrement($rec);
   172	        }
   173	
   174	        NotificationService::generer();
   175	
   176	        return response()->json([
   177	            'success' => true,
   178	            'reste' => $vente->resteAPayer(),
   179	            'message' => self::messageStatut($request->statut),
   180	        ]);
   181	    }
   182	
   183	    private static function messageStatut($statut): string
   184	    {
   185	        return [
   186	            'paye' => 'Paiement enregistré.',
   187	            'partiel' => 'Paiement partiel enregistré.',
   188	            'absent' => 'Client absent signalé.',
   189	            'refus' => 'Refus de payer signalé.',
   190	            'promesse' => 'Promesse de paiement enregistrée.',
   191	        ][$statut] ?? 'OK';
   192	    }
   193	}

>>> app/Http/Controllers/Api/AuthController.php
     1	<?php
     2	namespace App\Http\Controllers\Api;
     3	use App\Http\Controllers\Controller;
     4	use App\Models\User;
     5	use Illuminate\Http\Request;
     6	use Illuminate\Support\Str;
     7	
     8	class AuthController extends Controller
     9	{
    10	    public function login(Request $request)
    11	    {
    12	        $request->validate([
    13	            'identifiant' => 'required_without:email|string',
    14	            'email' => 'required_without:identifiant|email',
    15	            'password' => 'required',
    16	            'role' => 'nullable|in:gerante,chef_agence,agent',
    17	        ]);
    18	
    19	        $identifiant = trim($request->identifiant ?? $request->email ?? '');
    20	        $user = User::where('actif', true)
    21	            ->where(function($q) use ($identifiant) {
    22	                $q->where('email', $identifiant)
    23	                  ->orWhere('telephone', $identifiant);
    24	            })
    25	            ->first();
    26	
    27	        if (!$user || !\Hash::check($request->password, $user->password)) {
    28	            return response()->json(['error' => 'Identifiants invalides'], 401);
    29	        }
    30	
    31	        if ($request->role && $user->role !== $request->role) {
    32	            return response()->json(['error' => "Ce compte n'a pas le rôle " . $request->role], 403);
    33	        }
    34	
    35	        $token = Str::random(64);
    36	        $user->update(['token' => $token, 'token_expires_at' => now()->addHours(24)]);
    37	
    38	        return response()->json([
    39	            'success' => true,
    40	            'token' => $token,
    41	            'user' => [
    42	                'id' => $user->uuid,
    43	                'nom' => $user->nom,
    44	                'prenom' => $user->prenom,
    45	                'role' => $user->role,
    46	            ]
    47	        ]);
    48	    }
    49	    public function me(Request $request)
    50	    {
    51	        $user = $request->user();
    52	        return response()->json([
    53	            'user' => [
    54	                'id' => $user->uuid,
    55	                'nom' => $user->nom,
    56	                'prenom' => $user->prenom,
    57	                'email' => $user->email,
    58	                'telephone' => $user->telephone,
    59	                'role' => $user->role,
    60	            ]
    61	        ]);
    62	    }
    63	
    64	    public function logout(Request $request)
    65	    {
    66	        $request->user()->update(['token' => null, 'token_expires_at' => null]);
    67	        return response()->json(['success' => true]);
    68	    }
    69	
    70	    public function changePassword(Request $request)
    71	    {
    72	        $request->validate(['password' => 'required|min:4']);
    73	        $request->user()->update(['password' => bcrypt($request->password)]);
    74	        return response()->json(['success' => true, 'message' => 'Mot de passe modifié.']);
    75	    }
    76	}
>>> app/Http/Controllers/Api/CaisseController.php
     1	<?php
     2	namespace App\Http\Controllers\Api;
     3	use App\Http\Controllers\Controller;
     4	use App\Models\Caisse;
     5	use App\Models\MouvementCaisse;
     6	use Illuminate\Http\Request;
     7	use Illuminate\Support\Str;
     8	
     9	class CaisseController extends Controller
    10	{
    11	    public function index(Request $request)
    12	    {
    13	        $user = $request->user();
    14	        $caisse = Caisse::where('user_id', $user->id)
    15	            ->where('statut', 'ouverte')
    16	            ->whereDate('date_ouverture', now()->toDateString())
    17	            ->orderByDesc('date_ouverture')
    18	            ->first();
    19	
    20	        $soldeInitial = 0;
    21	        if (!$caisse) {
    22	            $precedente = Caisse::where('user_id', $user->id)
    23	                ->where('statut', 'ouverte')
    24	                ->whereDate('date_ouverture', '<', now()->toDateString())
    25	                ->orderByDesc('date_ouverture')
    26	                ->first();
    27	
    28	            if ($precedente) {
    29	                $soldeInitial = $precedente->solde_theorique;
    30	                $precedente->update([
    31	                    'statut' => 'fermee',
    32	                    'date_fermeture' => now()->toDateString(),
    33	                ]);
    34	            }
    35	
    36	            $caisse = Caisse::create([
    37	                'uuid' => Str::uuid(),
    38	                'user_id' => $user->id,
    39	                'date_ouverture' => now()->toDateString(),
    40	                'statut' => 'ouverte',
    41	                'solde_initial' => $soldeInitial,
    42	            ]);
    43	        }
    44	        $caisse->load(['mouvements' => fn($q) => $q->orderByDesc('date_mouvement')->limit(100), 'mouvements.user']);
    45	        $mouvements = $caisse->mouvements;
    46	        $totalEncaissements = $mouvements->where('type', 'encaissement')->sum('montant');
    47	        $totalDecaissements = $mouvements->whereIn('type', ['decaissement', 'salaire'])->sum('montant');
    48	        $totalSalaires = $mouvements->where('type', 'salaire')->sum('montant');
    49	        $totalEpargnes = $mouvements->where('type', 'epargne')->sum('montant');
    50	        $totalPenalites = $mouvements->where('type', 'penalite')->sum('montant');
    51	        $totalRemises = $mouvements->where('type', 'remise')->sum('montant');
    52	        $soldeTheorique = $caisse->solde_initial + $totalEncaissements + $totalEpargnes + $totalPenalites - $totalDecaissements - $totalRemises;
    53	        return response()->json([
    54	            'total_epargnes' => $totalEpargnes,
    55	            'total_penalites' => $totalPenalites,
    56	            'total_remises' => $totalRemises,
    57	            'caisse' => $caisse,
    58	            'total_encaissements' => $totalEncaissements,
    59	            'total_decaissements' => $totalDecaissements,
    60	            'total_salaires' => $totalSalaires,
    61	            'solde_theorique' => $soldeTheorique,
    62	            'nb_mouvements' => $mouvements->count(),
    63	        ]);
    64	    }

    74	    {

    76	        $caisse = Caisse::where('user_id', $request->user()->id)->where('statut', 'ouverte')->whereDate('date_ouverture', now()->toDateString())->orderByDesc('date_ouverture')->firstOrFail();
    77	        MouvementCaisse::create([
    78	            'uuid' => Str::uuid(), 'caisse_id' => $caisse->id, 'user_id' => $request->user()->id,
    79	            'type' => 'decaissement', 'montant' => $request->montant, 'motif' => $request->motif, 'date_mouvement' => now(),
    80	        ]);
    81	        return response()->json(['success' => true]);

    87	        $caisse = Caisse::where('user_id', $request->user()->id)->where('statut', 'ouverte')->whereDate('date_ouverture', now()->toDateString())->orderByDesc('date_ouverture')->firstOrFail();
    88	        MouvementCaisse::create([
    89	            'uuid' => Str::uuid(), 'caisse_id' => $caisse->id, 'user_id' => $request->user()->id,
    90	            'type' => 'salaire', 'montant' => $request->montant, 'beneficiaire' => $request->beneficiaire,

    96	    public function cloturer(Request $request)
    97	    {
    98	        $request->validate(['solde_reel' => 'required|numeric|min:0', 'notes' => 'nullable|string']);
    99	        $caisse = Caisse::where('user_id', $request->user()->id)->where('statut', 'ouverte')->whereDate('date_ouverture', now()->toDateString())->orderByDesc('date_ouverture')->firstOrFail();
   100	        $mouvements = $caisse->mouvements;
   101	        $totalEncaissements = $mouvements->where('type', 'encaissement')->sum('montant');
   102	        $totalDecaissements = $mouvements->whereIn('type', ['decaissement', 'salaire'])->sum('montant');
   103	        $soldeTheorique = $caisse->solde_initial + $totalEncaissements - $totalDecaissements;
   104	        $ecart = $request->solde_reel - $soldeTheorique;
   105	        $caisse->update([
   106	            'solde_theorique' => $soldeTheorique,
   107	            'solde_reel' => $request->solde_reel,
   108	            'ecart' => $ecart,
   109	            'statut' => 'fermee',
   110	            'date_fermeture' => now()->toDateString(),
   111	            'notes' => $request->notes,
   112	        ]);
   113	        return response()->json(['success' => true, 'ecart' => $ecart, 'solde_theorique' => $soldeTheorique]);
   114	    }
   115	
   116	    /**
   117	     * Remise d'un agent vers la caisse d'agence.
   118	     */
   119	    public function remise(Request $request)
   120	    {
   121	        $request->validate([
   122	            'montant' => 'required|numeric|min:0',
   123	            'agent_id' => 'nullable|exists:users,uuid',
   124	            'sync_uuid' => 'nullable|string|max:36',
   125	        ]);
   126	
   127	        $agent = $request->agent_id
   128	            ? \App\Models\User::where('uuid', $request->agent_id)->first()
   129	            : $request->user();
   130	
   131	        if (!$agent) {
   132	            return response()->json(['error' => 'Agent introuvable.'], 422);
   133	        }
   134	
   135	        // Idempotence : si sync_uuid déjà traité, renvoyer succès sans doublon
   136	        if ($request->sync_uuid) {
   137	            $exist = MouvementCaisse::where('sync_uuid', $request->sync_uuid)->first();
   138	            if ($exist) {
   139	                return response()->json(['success' => true, 'deja_traite' => true]);
   140	            }
   141	        }
   142	
   143	        return \Illuminate\Support\Facades\DB::transaction(function () use ($agent, $request) {
   144	            $caisseAgent = \App\Services\CaisseService::getCaisseActive($agent->id, 'jour');
   145	            $caisseAgence = \App\Services\CaisseService::getCaisseActive(
   146	                \App\Models\User::where('role', 'chef_agence')->value('id') ?? $agent->id,
   147	                'jour'
   148	            );
   149	
   150	            $reference = 'REMISE-' . \Illuminate\Support\Str::upper(\Illuminate\Support\Str::random(8));
   151	
   152	            // Sortie de la caisse agent
   153	            MouvementCaisse::create([
   154	                'uuid' => \Illuminate\Support\Str::uuid(),
   155	                'caisse_id' => $caisseAgent->id,
   156	                'user_id' => $agent->id,
   157	                'type' => 'remise',
   158	                'montant' => $request->montant,
   159	                'montant_declare' => $request->montant,
   160	                'reference' => $reference,
   161	                'motif' => 'Remise à l agence',
   162	                'date_mouvement' => now(),
   163	                'statut_validation' => 'en_attente',
   164	                'sync_uuid' => $request->sync_uuid,
   165	            ]);
   166	
   167	            // Entrée dans la caisse agence
   168	            MouvementCaisse::create([
   169	                'uuid' => \Illuminate\Support\Str::uuid(),
   170	                'caisse_id' => $caisseAgence->id,
   171	                'user_id' => $agent->id,
   172	                'type' => 'versement',
   173	                'montant' => $request->montant,
   174	                'montant_declare' => $request->montant,
   175	                'reference' => $reference,
   176	                'motif' => 'Versement de ' . $agent->nom,
   177	                'date_mouvement' => now(),
   178	                'statut_validation' => 'en_attente',
   179	                'sync_uuid' => $request->sync_uuid,
   180	            ]);
   181	
   182	            \App\Services\CaisseService::recalculer($caisseAgent);
   183	            \App\Services\CaisseService::recalculer($caisseAgence);
   184	
   185	            return response()->json(['success' => true, 'reference' => $reference]);
   186	        });
   187	    }

   189	
   190	    public function consolidee(Request $request)
   191	    {
   192	        $debut = $request->debut ?? now()->startOfMonth()->toDateString();
   193	        $fin   = $request->fin ?? now()->toDateString();
   194	
   195	        $mouvements = MouvementCaisse::whereBetween('date_mouvement', [$debut . ' 00:00:00', $fin . ' 23:59:59'])
   196	            ->whereNotIn('type', ['remise', 'versement'])
   197	            ->get();
   198	
   199	        // Flux réels uniquement : exclure les transferts internes remise/versement
   200	        $encaissements = $mouvements->where('type', 'encaissement')->sum('montant');
   201	        $decaissements = $mouvements->whereIn('type', ['decaissement', 'salaire'])->sum('montant');
   202	        $epargnes      = $mouvements->where('type', 'epargne')->sum('montant');
   203	        $penalites     = $mouvements->where('type', 'penalite')->sum('montant');
   204	        $solde         = $encaissements + $epargnes + $penalites - $decaissements;
   205	
   206	        return response()->json([
   207	            'encaissements' => $encaissements,
   208	            'decaissements' => $decaissements,
   209	            'epargnes'      => $epargnes,
   210	            'penalites'     => $penalites,
   211	            'solde'         => $solde,
   212	        ]);
   213	    }
   214	
   215	
   216	    public function periode(Request $request)
   217	    {
   218	        $debut = $request->input('debut', now()->startOfMonth()->toDateString());
   219	        $fin = $request->input('fin', now()->endOfMonth()->toDateString());
   220	
   221	        $mouvements = MouvementCaisse::query()
   222	            ->whereBetween('date_mouvement', [$debut . ' 00:00:00', $fin . ' 23:59:59'])
   223	            ->whereNotIn('type', ['remise', 'versement'])
   224	            ->get()
   225	            ->groupBy(fn($m) => $m->date_mouvement->format('Y-m-d'));
   226	
   227	        $caisses = Caisse::query()
   228	            ->whereBetween('date_ouverture', [$debut, $fin])
   229	            ->get()
   230	            ->groupBy(fn($c) => \Carbon\Carbon::parse($c->date_ouverture)->format('Y-m-d'));
   231	
   232	        $resultat = [];
   233	
   234	        foreach ($mouvements as $jour => $liste) {
   235	            $caissesDuJour = $caisses->get($jour, collect());
   236	            $nonCloture = $caissesDuJour->isNotEmpty()
   237	                && $caissesDuJour->contains(fn($c) => $c->statut !== 'fermee');
   238	            $ecart = (float) $caissesDuJour->sum(fn($c) => (float)($c->ecart ?? 0));
   239	
   240	            $resultat[] = [
   241	                'jour' => $jour,
   242	                'encaissements' => (float) $liste->where('type', 'encaissement')->sum('montant'),
   243	                'decaissements' => (float) $liste->whereIn('type', ['decaissement','salaire'])->sum('montant'),
   244	                'epargnes' => (float) $liste->where('type', 'epargne')->sum('montant'),
   245	                'penalites' => (float) $liste->where('type', 'penalite')->sum('montant'),
   246	                'remises' => (float) $liste->where('type', 'remise')->sum('montant'),
   247	                'versements' => (float) $liste->where('type', 'versement')->sum('montant'),
   248	                'nb_mouvements' => $liste->count(),
   249	                'non_cloture' => $nonCloture,
   250	                'ecart' => $ecart,
   251	            ];
   252	        }
   253	
   254	        foreach ($caisses as $jour => $liste) {
   255	            if ($mouvements->has($jour)) continue;
   256	            $nonCloture = $liste->contains(fn($c) => $c->statut !== 'fermee');
   257	            if (!$nonCloture) continue;
   258	            $resultat[] = [
   259	                'jour' => $jour,
   260	                'encaissements' => 0,
   261	                'decaissements' => 0,
   262	                'epargnes' => 0,
   263	                'penalites' => 0,
   264	                'remises' => 0,
   265	                'versements' => 0,
   266	                'nb_mouvements' => 0,
   267	                'non_cloture' => true,
   268	                'ecart' => 0,
   269	            ];
   270	        }
   271	
   272	        usort($resultat, fn($a, $b) => strcmp($a['jour'], $b['jour']));
   273	        return response()->json($resultat);
   274	    }
   275	
   276	
   277	    public function exportPeriode(Request $request)
   278	    {
   279	        $request->validate([
   280	            'debut' => ['nullable', 'date'],
   281	            'fin' => ['nullable', 'date', 'after_or_equal:debut'],
   282	            'type' => ['nullable', 'string'],
   283	            'agent_uuid' => ['nullable', 'exists:users,uuid'],
   284	        ]);
   285	
   286	        $debut = $request->input('debut', now()->startOfMonth()->toDateString());
   287	        $fin = $request->input('fin', now()->toDateString());
   288	
   289	        $query = MouvementCaisse::query()
   290	            ->with('user')
   291	            ->whereBetween('date_mouvement', [
   292	                $debut . ' 00:00:00',
   293	                $fin . ' 23:59:59',
   294	            ]);
   295	
   296	        if ($request->filled('type')) {
   297	            $query->where('type', $request->input('type'));
   298	        }
   299	
   300	        if ($request->filled('agent_uuid')) {
   301	            $query->whereHas('user', function ($q) use ($request) {
   302	                $q->where('uuid', $request->input('agent_uuid'));
   303	            });
   304	        }
   305	
   306	        $mouvements = $query->orderBy('date_mouvement')->get();
   307	
   308	        return response()->streamDownload(function () use ($mouvements) {
   309	            $file = fopen('php://output', 'w');
   310	            fwrite($file, chr(0xEF) . chr(0xBB) . chr(0xBF));
   311	
   312	            fputcsv($file, ['Date', 'Heure', 'Type', 'Montant', 'Mode', 'Référence', 'Motif', 'Utilisateur']);
   313	
   314	            foreach ($mouvements as $mouvement) {
   315	                $date = $mouvement->date_mouvement;
   316	                fputcsv($file, [
   317	                    $date ? $date->format('Y-m-d') : '',
   318	                    $date ? $date->format('H:i') : '',
   319	                    $mouvement->type ?? '',
   320	                    $mouvement->montant ?? '',
   321	                    $mouvement->mode_paiement ?? '',
   322	                    $mouvement->reference ?? '',
   323	                    $mouvement->motif ?? '',
   324	                    trim(($mouvement->user->nom ?? '') . ' ' . ($mouvement->user->prenom ?? '')),
   325	                ]);
   326	            }
   327	
   328	            fclose($file);
   329	        }, "caisse_{$debut}_au_{$fin}.csv", [
   330	            'Content-Type' => 'text/csv; charset=UTF-8',
   331	        ]);
   332	    }

   334	
   335	    public function validerRemise(Request $request, string $reference)
   336	    {
   337	        $chef = $request->user();
   338	
   339	        if (!in_array($chef->role, ['chef_agence', 'gerante'], true)) {
   340	            return response()->json(['error' => 'Non autorisé'], 403);
   341	        }
   342	
   343	        return \Illuminate\Support\Facades\DB::transaction(function () use ($reference, $chef, $request) {
   344	            $mouvements = MouvementCaisse::where('reference', $reference)
   345	                ->whereIn('type', ['remise', 'versement'])
   346	                ->lockForUpdate()
   347	                ->get();
   348	
   349	            if ($mouvements->isEmpty()) {
   350	                return response()->json(['error' => 'Remise introuvable'], 404);
   351	            }
   352	
   353	            // Contrôle d'agence : le chef ne peut valider que les caisses de son agence
   354	            foreach ($mouvements as $m) {
   355	                $caisse = $m->caisse;
   356	                if ($caisse && $caisse->agence_id && $chef->agence_id && $caisse->agence_id !== $chef->agence_id) {
   357	                    return response()->json(['error' => 'Remise d une autre agence'], 403);
   358	                }
   359	                // Personne ne peut valider sa propre remise
   360	                if ($m->user_id === $chef->id) {
   361	                    return response()->json(['error' => 'Auto-validation interdite'], 403);
   362	                }
   363	                // Ne pas revalider un mouvement déjà traité
   364	                if ($m->statut_validation !== 'en_attente') {
   365	                    return response()->json(['error' => 'Remise déjà traitée'], 422);
   366	                }
   367	            }
   368	
   369	            $statut = $request->statut ?? 'valide'; // valide, ecart, rejete
   370	            $montantRecu = $request->montant_recu ?? null;
   371	            $motifRejet = $request->motif_rejet ?? null;
   372	
   373	            foreach ($mouvements as $m) {
   374	                $m->statut_validation = $statut;
   375	                $m->valide_par = $chef->id;
   376	                $m->valide_le = now();
   377	                if ($montantRecu !== null) {
   378	                    $m->montant_recu = $montantRecu;
   379	                    $m->ecart_remise = (float) $m->montant_declare - (float) $montantRecu;
   380	                }
   381	                if ($motifRejet) {
   382	                    $m->motif_rejet = $motifRejet;
   383	                }
   384	                $m->save();
   385	            }
   386	
   387	            return response()->json(['success' => true, 'reference' => $reference, 'statut' => $statut]);
   388	        });
   389	    }
   390	
   391	
   392	    public function remisesEnAttente(Request $request)
   393	    {
   394	        $remises = MouvementCaisse::where('type', 'remise')
   395	            ->where('statut_validation', 'en_attente')
   396	            ->with('user:id,uuid,nom,prenom')
   397	            ->orderBy('date_mouvement')
   398	            ->get();
   399	
   400	        return response()->json($remises);
   401	    }
   402	
   403	    public function export(Request $request)
   404	    {
   405	        $uuid = $request->uuid;
   406	        $caisse = Caisse::where('uuid', $uuid)->firstOrFail();
   407	        $mouvements = $caisse->mouvements()->with('user')->orderBy('date_mouvement')->get();
   408	        $headers = ['Content-Type' => 'text/csv; charset=utf-8', 'Content-Disposition' => 'attachment; filename=caisse_' . $caisse->date_ouverture . '.csv'];
   409	        $callback = function() use ($caisse, $mouvements) {
   410	            $file = fopen('php://output', 'w');
   411	            fprintf($file, chr(0xEF).chr(0xBB).chr(0xBF));
   412	            fputcsv($file, ['Date', 'Type', 'Montant', 'Mode', 'Bénéficiaire', 'Motif', 'Utilisateur']);
   413	            foreach ($mouvements as $m) {
   414	                fputcsv($file, [$m->date_mouvement, $m->type, $m->montant, $m->mode_paiement, $m->beneficiaire, $m->motif, $m->user?->nom]);
   415	            }
   416	            fclose($file);
   417	        };
   418	        return response()->stream($callback, 200, $headers);
   419	    }

     1	<?php

     3	namespace App\Http\Controllers\Api;
     4	
     5	use App\Http\Controllers\Controller;
     6	use App\Models\Vente;
     7	use App\Models\Recouvrement;

     9	use App\Models\Echeance;

    11	use App\Models\User;
    12	use App\Models\Alerte;
    13	use App\Services\CaisseService;

    20	class ChefAgenceController extends Controller
    21	{
    22	    public function dashboard()
    23	    {
    24	        $user = auth()->user();
    25	        $aujourdhui = now()->toDateString();
    26	
    27	        $debiteurs = Debiteur::where('actif', true)->get();
    28	        $debiteursEnRetard = collect();
    29	        foreach ($debiteurs as $d) {
    30	            $aDesRetards = $d->ventes()
    31	                ->where('statut', 'en_cours')
    32	                ->whereHas('echeances', fn($q) => $q->whereIn('statut', ['en_retard', 'partiel'])->whereDate('date_echeance', '<=', $aujourdhui))
    33	                ->exists();
    34	            if ($aDesRetards) $debiteursEnRetard->push($d);
    35	        }
    36	
    37	        $nbEnRetard = $debiteursEnRetard->count();
    38	        $nbAJour = $debiteurs->count() - $nbEnRetard;
    39	
    40	        $totalEcheancesRetard = Echeance::whereIn('statut', ['en_retard', 'partiel'])
    41	            ->whereDate('date_echeance', '<=', $aujourdhui)
    42	            ->whereHas('vente', fn($q) => $q->where('statut', 'en_cours'))
    43	            ->sum('montant_prevu');
    44	
    45	        return response()->json([
    46	            'kpis' => [
    47	                'agents_count' => User::where('role', 'agent')->count(),
    48	                'debiteurs_actifs' => Debiteur::where('actif', true)->count(),
    49	                'debiteurs_a_jour' => $nbAJour,
    50	                'debiteurs_en_retard' => $nbEnRetard,
    51	                'encaisse_du_jour' => Recouvrement::whereDate('date_recouvrement', today())->where('statut', 'paye')->sum('montant'),
    52	                'creances_en_retard' => Vente::where('statut', 'en_cours')
    53	                    ->whereHas('echeances', fn($q) => $q->whereIn('statut', ['en_retard', 'partiel'])->whereDate('date_echeance', '<=', $aujourdhui))
    54	                    ->get()
    55	                    ->sum(fn($v) => $v->resteAPayer()),
    56	                'total_echeances_retard' => $totalEcheancesRetard,
    57	            ],
    58	            'agents' => User::where('role', 'agent')->withCount('debiteurs')->get(),
    59	            'alertes' => Echeance::whereIn('statut', ['en_retard', 'partiel'])
    60	                ->whereDate('date_echeance', '<=', $aujourdhui)
    61	                ->whereHas('vente', fn($q) => $q->where('statut', 'en_cours'))
    62	                ->with(['vente.debiteur', 'vente.agent'])
    63	                ->orderByDesc('jours_retard')
    64	                ->limit(10)
    65	                ->get()
    66	                ->map(fn($e) => [
    67	                    'debiteur' => $e->vente->debiteur?->nom ?? '—',
    68	                    'agent' => $e->vente->agent?->nom ?? '—',
    69	                    'montant' => $e->montant_prevu,
    70	                    'jours_retard' => $e->jours_retard,
    71	                    'statut' => 'Retard',
    72	                ]),
    73	        ]);
    74	    }

    76	    public function venteComptant(Request $request)
    77	    {
    78	        $request->validate([
    79	            'nom' => 'required|string','telephone' => 'nullable|string',
    80	            'produit_id' => 'nullable|exists:produits,uuid','montant' => 'required|numeric|min:0',
    81	        ]);
    82	        $user = $request->user();
    83	        $produit = $request->produit_id ? Produit::where('uuid', $request->produit_id)->first() : null;
    84	        DB::table('clients_comptant')->insert([
    85	            'uuid' => Str::uuid(), 'nom' => $request->nom, 'telephone' => $request->telephone,
    86	            'quartier' => $request->quartier ?? '', 'agent_id' => $user->id,
    87	            'created_at' => now(), 'updated_at' => now(),
    88	        ]);
    89	        $vente = Vente::create([
    90	            'uuid' => Str::uuid(), 'debiteur_id' => null, 'agent_id' => $user->id,
    91	            'produit_id' => $produit?->id, 'type_vente' => 'comptant',
    92	            'montant_total' => $request->montant, 'montant_journalier' => $request->montant,
    93	            'nombre_jours' => 1, 'epargne' => 0,
    94	            'date_debut' => now()->toDateString(), 'date_fin' => now()->toDateString(), 'heure_creation' => now()->toTimeString(),
    95	            'statut' => 'termine',
    96	        ]);
    97	        $rec = Recouvrement::create([
    98	            'uuid' => Str::uuid(), 'vente_id' => $vente->id, 'agent_id' => $user->id,
    99	            'montant' => $request->montant, 'date_recouvrement' => now()->toDateString(),
   100	            'mode_paiement' => $request->mode_paiement ?? 'especes', 'statut' => 'paye', 'synced' => true,
   101	        ]);
   102	        if ($produit && $produit->stock > 0) $produit->decrement('stock');
   103	        CaisseService::enregistrerRecouvrement($rec);
   104	        NotificationService::generer();
   105	        return response()->json(['success' => true]);
   106	    }
   107	
   108	    public function venteCredit(Request $request)
   109	    {
   110	        $request->validate([
   111	            'debiteur_id' => 'required|exists:debiteurs,uuid',
   112	            'produit_id' => 'nullable|exists:produits,uuid',
   113	            'montant' => 'required|numeric|min:0',
   114	            'epargne_par_jour' => 'nullable|numeric|min:0',
   115	            'jours' => 'required|integer|min:1|max:20',
   116	            'delai_avant_echeances' => 'nullable|integer|min:0|max:30',
   117	            'motif_derogation' => 'nullable|string|max:500',
   118	            'produits' => 'nullable|array',
   119	            'produits.*.produit_id' => 'required',
   120	            'produits.*.quantite' => 'required|integer|min:1',
   121	        ]);
   122	        $user = $request->user();
   123	        $debiteur = Debiteur::where('uuid', $request->debiteur_id)->firstOrFail();
   124	
   125	        // 1. Contrôle du NOMBRE de crédits simultanés (pas un montant, conforme à la logique métier)
   126	        $nbCreditsEnCours = Vente::where('debiteur_id', $debiteur->id)->where('statut', 'en_cours')->count();
   127	        $maxCredits = max(1, (int) ($debiteur->credits_autorises ?: 1));
   128	        if ($nbCreditsEnCours >= $maxCredits) {
   129	            return response()->json([
   130	                'error' => "Ce débiteur a déjà {$nbCreditsEnCours} crédit(s) en cours (maximum autorisé : {$maxCredits}). Le chef d'agence doit augmenter le nombre de crédits autorisés dans la fiche débiteur pour permettre cet achat.",
   131	            ], 422);
   132	        }
   133	
   134	        // 2. Contrôle du score : en dessous de 40%, un motif de dérogation est obligatoire
   135	        $scoreResultat = app(\App\Services\ScoreSolvabiliteService::class)->calculer($debiteur);
   136	        $score = $scoreResultat['score'] ?? 50;
   137	        $motifDerogation = trim($request->motif_derogation ?? '');
   138	        if ($score < 40 && $motifDerogation === '') {
   139	            return response()->json([
   140	                'error' => "Le score de solvabilité de ce débiteur est critique ({$score}%). Un motif de dérogation est obligatoire pour valider cette vente.",
   141	                'score' => $score,
   142	            ], 422);
   143	        }
   144	
   145	        $epargneParJour = $request->epargne_par_jour ?? 300;
   146	        $epargneTotal = $epargneParJour * $request->jours;
   147	        $total = $request->montant + $epargneTotal;
   148	        
   149	        if ($request->has('produits') && is_array($request->produits) && count($request->produits) > 0) {
   150	            $totalProduits = 0;
   151	            foreach ($request->produits as $p) {
   152	                $prod = \App\Models\Produit::where('uuid', $p['produit_id'])->first();
   153	                if ($prod) {
   154	                    $totalProduits += ($prod->prix_vente ?? 0) * ($p['quantite'] ?? 1);
   155	                }
   156	            }
   157	            if ($totalProduits > 0) {
   158	                $total = $totalProduits + $epargneTotal;
   159	            }
   160	        }
   161	        $journalier = round($total / $request->jours, 2);
   162	        $produit = $request->produit_id ? Produit::where('uuid', $request->produit_id)->first() : null;
   163	        $vente = Vente::create([
   164	            'uuid' => Str::uuid(), 'debiteur_id' => $debiteur->id,
   165	            'agent_id' => $debiteur->agent_id ?: $user->id,
   166	            'created_by' => $user->id,
   167	            'produit_id' => $produit?->id, 'type_vente' => 'credit',
   168	            'montant_total' => $total, 'montant_journalier' => $journalier,
   169	            'nombre_jours' => $request->jours,
   170	            'delai_avant_echeances' => (int) ($request->delai_avant_echeances ?? 1),
   171	            'epargne' => $epargneTotal,
   172	            'epargne_par_jour' => $epargneParJour,
   173	            'epargne_total' => $epargneTotal,
   174	            'penalite_par_jour' => $request->penalite_par_jour ?? 1000,
   175	            'mise_agent' => $epargneParJour,
   176	            'date_debut' => now()->toDateString(),
   177	            'date_fin' => now()->addDays((int) ($request->delai_avant_echeances ?? 1) + $request->jours - 1)->toDateString(),
   178	            'statut' => 'en_cours',
   179	        ]);
   180	        // Si dérogation utilisée (score bas mais motif fourni), la tracer pour le futur recalibrage V2
   181	        if ($score < 40 && $motifDerogation !== '') {
   182	            app(\App\Services\ScoreSolvabiliteService::class)->enregistrerDerogation($debiteur, $vente->id, $motifDerogation, $request);
   183	        }
   184	
   185	        if ($request->has('produits') && is_array($request->produits) && count($request->produits) > 0) {
   186	            foreach ($request->produits as $p) {
   187	                $prod = \App\Models\Produit::where('uuid', $p['produit_id'])->orWhere('id', $p['produit_id'])->first();
   188	                if ($prod) {
   189	                    \App\Models\VenteProduit::create([
   190	                        'vente_id' => $vente->id,
   191	                        'produit_id' => $prod->id,
   192	                        'quantite' => $p['quantite'] ?? 1,
   193	                        'prix_unitaire' => $prod->prix_vente ?? 0,
   194	                    ]);
   195	                    $qte = (int) ($p['quantite'] ?? 1);
   196	                    $avant = (int) $prod->stock;
   197	                    $prod->decrement('stock', $qte);
   198	                    \App\Models\MouvementStock::create([
   199	                        'uuid' => Str::uuid(),
   200	                        'produit_id' => $prod->id,
   201	                        'user_id' => $user->id,
   202	                        'type' => 'sortie',
   203	                        'action' => 'vente',
   204	                        'date_mouvement' => now()->toDateString(),
   205	                        'quantite' => $qte,
   206	                        'prix_unitaire' => $prod->prix_vente ?? 0,
   207	                        'stock_avant' => $avant,
   208	                        'stock_apres' => $avant - $qte,
   209	                        'motif' => 'Vente credit #' . substr($vente->uuid, 0, 8),
   210	                        'created_at' => now(),
   211	                    ]);
   212	                }
   213	            }
   214	        }
   215	        
   216	        $vente->genererEcheances();
   217	        ComptabiliteService::ecrireVenteCredit($vente);
   218	        NotificationService::generer();
   219	        return response()->json([
   220	            'success' => true,
   221	            'vente_id' => $vente->uuid,
   222	            'journalier' => $journalier,
   223	            'epargne_par_jour' => $epargneParJour,
   224	            'epargne_total' => $epargneTotal,
   225	            'date_fin' => $vente->date_fin,
   226	        ]);
   227	    }
   228	
   229	
   230	    public function historiqueDebiteur($uuid)
   231	    {
   232	        $debiteur = Debiteur::where('uuid', $uuid)->firstOrFail();
   233	        
   234	        $ventes = Vente::where('debiteur_id', $debiteur->id)
   235	            ->with(['produit', 'venteProduits.produit'])
   236	            ->orderByDesc('date_debut')
   237	            ->get()
   238	            ->map(function($v) {
   239	                return [
   240	                    'uuid' => $v->uuid,
   241	                    'date' => $v->date_debut->format('d/m/Y'),
   242	                    'produit' => $v->produit?->nom ?? '--',
   243	                    'produits' => $v->produitsPanier(),
   244	                    'produits_resume' => empty($v->produitsPanier()) ? '--' : implode(', ', array_map(function($p){ return $p['nom'].' x'.$p['quantite']; }, $v->produitsPanier())),
   245	                    'montant_total' => $v->montant_total,
   246	                    'statut' => $v->statut,
   247	                    'type_vente' => $v->type_vente,
   248	                    'reste_a_payer' => $v->resteAPayer(),
   249	                ];
   250	            });
   251	        
   252	        $paiements = Recouvrement::whereIn('vente_id', Vente::where('debiteur_id', $debiteur->id)->pluck('id'))
   253	            ->orderByDesc('date_recouvrement')
   254	            ->get()
   255	            ->map(function($r) {
   256	                return [
   257	                    'id' => $r->id,
   258	                    'date' => $r->date_recouvrement->format('d/m/Y'),
   259	                    'montant' => $r->montant,
   260	                    'mode' => $r->mode_paiement,
   261	                    'statut' => $r->statut,
   262	                ];
   263	            });
   264	        
   265	        $soldeTotal = Vente::where('debiteur_id', $debiteur->id)
   266	            ->where('statut', 'en_cours')
   267	            ->get()
   268	            ->sum(function($v) { return $v->resteAPayer(); });
   269	        
   270	        return response()->json([
   271	            'success' => true,
   272	            'debiteur' => [
   273	                'nom' => $debiteur->nom . ' ' . ($debiteur->prenom ?? ''),
   274	                'code_client' => $debiteur->code_client,
   275	                'score_solvabilite' => $debiteur->score_solvabilite,
   276	                'solde_total' => $soldeTotal,
   277	            ],
   278	            'ventes' => $ventes,
   279	            'paiements' => $paiements,
   280	        ]);
   281	    }
   282	    
   283	    public function scoreDebiteur($uuid)

   288	        return response()->json($resultat);
   289	    }

     1	<?php
     2	namespace App\Http\Controllers\Api;
     3	use App\Http\Controllers\Controller;
     4	use App\Models\Journal;
     5	use App\Models\Vente;
     6	use App\Models\Recouvrement;
     7	use App\Models\Produit;
     8	use Illuminate\Http\Request;
     9	
    10	class ComptabiliteController extends Controller
    11	{
    12	    public function journal(Request $request)
    13	    {
    14	        $query = Journal::orderByDesc('date_ecriture')->orderByDesc('id');
    15	        if ($request->compte) $query->where(function($q) use ($request) { $q->where('compte_debit', $request->compte)->orWhere('compte_credit', $request->compte); });
    16	        if ($request->date_debut) $query->whereDate('date_ecriture', '>=', $request->date_debut);
    17	        if ($request->date_fin) $query->whereDate('date_ecriture', '<=', $request->date_fin);
    18	
    19	        $page = max(1, (int) ($request->page ?? 1));
    20	        $parPage = max(1, min(100, (int) ($request->par_page ?? 50)));
    21	        $total = $query->count();
    22	        $ecritures = $query->skip(($page - 1) * $parPage)->take($parPage)->get();
    23	
    24	        return response()->json([
    25	            'donnees' => $ecritures,
    26	            'page' => $page,
    27	            'par_page' => $parPage,
    28	            'total' => $total,
    29	            'total_pages' => (int) ceil($total / $parPage),
    30	        ]);
    31	    }
    32	
    33	    public function grandLivre(Request $request)
    34	    {
    35	        $journals = Journal::orderBy('compte_debit')->orderBy('date_ecriture')->get();
    36	        $comptes = [];
    37	        foreach ($journals as $j) {
    38	            $debit = $j->compte_debit;
    39	            $credit = $j->compte_credit;
    40	            if (!isset($comptes[$debit])) $comptes[$debit] = ['compte' => $debit, 'libelle' => $this->libelleCompte($debit), 'debit' => 0, 'credit' => 0];
    41	            if (!isset($comptes[$credit])) $comptes[$credit] = ['compte' => $credit, 'libelle' => $this->libelleCompte($credit), 'debit' => 0, 'credit' => 0];
    42	            $comptes[$debit]['debit'] += $j->montant;
    43	            $comptes[$credit]['credit'] += $j->montant;
    44	        }
    45	        foreach ($comptes as &$c) { $c['solde'] = $c['debit'] - $c['credit']; }
    46	        return response()->json(array_values($comptes));
    47	    }
    48	
    49	    public function balance(Request $request)
    50	    {
    51	        $totalDebit = Journal::sum('montant');
    52	        $totalCredit = Journal::sum('montant');
    53	        return response()->json([
    54	            'total_debit' => $totalDebit,
    55	            'total_credit' => $totalCredit,
    56	            'equilibree' => abs($totalDebit - $totalCredit) < 0.01,
    57	        'equilibre' => abs($totalDebit - $totalCredit) < 0.01,
    58	            'comptes' => $this->grandLivre($request)->original ?? [],
    59	        ]);
    60	    }
    61	
    62	    public function etatsFinanciers(Request $request)
    63	    {
    64	        $debut = $request->date_debut ?? now()->startOfMonth()->toDateString();
    65	        $fin = $request->date_fin ?? now()->toDateString();
    66	
    67	        $ventesActives = Vente::actives()->whereDate('date_debut', '>=', $debut)->whereDate('date_debut', '<=', $fin);
    68	        $chiffreAffaires = $ventesActives->sum('montant_total');
    69	        $encaissements = Recouvrement::where('statut', 'paye')->whereDate('date_recouvrement', '>=', $debut)->whereDate('date_recouvrement', '<=', $fin)->sum('montant');
    70	        
    71	        $valeurStock = Produit::where('actif', true)->get()->sum(fn($p) => $p->stock * $p->prix_achat);
    72	        
    73	        $charges = Journal::where('compte_debit', 'like', '601%')->whereDate('date_ecriture', '>=', $debut)->whereDate('date_ecriture', '<=', $fin)->sum('montant');
    74	        
    75	        $beneficeBrut = $chiffreAffaires - $charges;
    76	
    77	        return response()->json([
    78	            'compte_resultat' => [
    79	                'chiffre_affaires' => $chiffreAffaires,
    80	                'encaissements' => $encaissements,
    81	                'charges' => $charges,
    82	                'benefice_brut' => $beneficeBrut,
    83	                'valeur_stock' => $valeurStock,
    84	            ],
    85	            'bilan_synthetique' => [
    86	                'actif_total' => $valeurStock + $encaissements,
    87	                'passif_total' => $chiffreAffaires - $encaissements,
    88	            ],
    89	        ]);
    90	    }
    91	
    92	    public function exportJournal(Request $request)
    93	    {
    94	        $journal = $this->journal($request);
    95	        $ecritures = $journal->original['donnees'] ?? $journal->original ?? [];
    96	
    97	        $filename = 'journal_comptable_' . now()->format('Ymd_His') . '.csv';
    98	        $path = storage_path('app/public/' . $filename);
    99	
   100	        $f = fopen($path, 'w');
   101	        fputcsv($f, ['Date', 'Numéro', 'Compte débit', 'Compte crédit', 'Libellé', 'Montant', 'Référence']);
   102	        foreach ($ecritures as $e) {
   103	            fputcsv($f, [$e['date_ecriture'], $e['numero_ecriture'], $e['compte_debit'], $e['compte_credit'], $e['libelle'], $e['montant'], $e['reference']]);
   104	        }
   105	        fclose($f);
   106	
   107	        return response()->download($path, $filename)->deleteFileAfterSend(true);
   108	    }

   121	        return response()->download($path, $filename)->deleteFileAfterSend(true);

   137	        fputcsv($f, ['Passif total', $etats['bilan_synthetique']['passif_total'] ?? 0]);
   138	        fclose($f);

   141	
   142	    private function libelleCompte(string $compte): string { return match(substr($compte, 0, 3)) { '411' => 'Clients', '521' => 'Caisse', '701' => 'Ventes', '601' => 'Charges', default => 'Compte ' . $compte }; }

>>> app/Http/Controllers/Api/DashboardController.php
     1	<?php

     3	use App\Services\SyntheseService;
     4	use App\Http\Controllers\Controller;
     5	use App\Models\Recouvrement;
     6	use App\Models\User;

    15	class DashboardController extends Controller
    16	{
    17	    public function index()
    18	{
    19	    AlerteService::verifierEtGenerer();
    20	    SyntheseService::generer();
    21	
    22	    $aujourdhui = now()->toDateString();
    23	
    24	    // 1. Top Agents du Jour
    25	    $topAgents = Recouvrement::with('agent:id,nom,prenom')
    26	        ->whereDate('date_recouvrement', $aujourdhui)
    27	        ->where('statut', 'paye')
    28	        ->select('agent_id', DB::raw('SUM(montant) as total_encaisse'))
    29	        ->groupBy('agent_id')
    30	        ->orderByDesc('total_encaisse')
    31	        ->get();
    32	
    33	    // 2. Alertes Retard
    34	    $alertesRetard = $this->alertesRetard();
    35	
    36	    // 3. Performance Agents
    37	    $performanceAgents = User::where('role', 'agent')
    38	        ->where('actif', true)
    39	        ->withCount(['debiteurs' => function ($q) {
    40	            $q->where('actif', true);
    41	        }])
    42	        ->get()
    43	        ->map(function ($agent) {
    44	            return [
    45	                'nom' => $agent->nom,
    46	                'prenom' => $agent->prenom,
    47	                'debiteurs_count' => $agent->debiteurs_count,
    48	                'portefeuille' => Vente::where('agent_id', $agent->id)
    49	                    ->where('statut', 'en_cours')
    50	                    ->sum('montant_total')
    51	            ];
    52	        });
    53	
    54	    return response()->json([
    55	        'kpis' => [
    56	            'total_debiteurs' => Debiteur::where('actif', true)->count(),
    57	            'debiteurs_actifs' => Vente::where('statut', 'en_cours')->distinct('debiteur_id')->count(),
    58	            'debiteurs_a_jour' => $this->debiteursAjour($aujourdhui),
    59	            'debiteurs_en_retard' => $this->debiteursEnRetard($aujourdhui),
    60	            'encours_total' => Vente::where('statut', 'en_cours')->sum('montant_total'),
    61	            'creances_totales' => $this->creancesTotales(),
    62	            'encaisse_aujourdhui' => Recouvrement::whereDate('date_recouvrement', $aujourdhui)->where('statut', 'paye')->sum('montant'),
    63	            'attendu_aujourdhui' => Vente::where('statut', 'en_cours')->whereDate('date_debut', '<=', $aujourdhui)->whereDate('date_fin', '>=', $aujourdhui)->sum('montant_journalier'),
    64	            'restant_aujourdhui' => $this->restantAujourdhui($aujourdhui),
    65	            'paye_aujourdhui' => $this->payeAujourdhui($aujourdhui),
    66	            'non_paye_aujourdhui' => $this->nonPayeAujourdhui($aujourdhui),
    67	            'chiffre_affaires_jour' => Recouvrement::whereDate('date_recouvrement', $aujourdhui)->where('statut', 'paye')->sum('montant'),
    68	            'taux_recouvrement' => $this->tauxRecouvrement(),
    69	            'stocks_faibles' => Produit::where('actif', true)->where('stock', '<=', DB::raw('seuil_alerte'))->count(),
    70	            'alertes_non_lues' => \App\Models\Alerte::where('lue', false)->count(),
    71	        ],
    72	        'top_agents' => $topAgents,
    73	        'alertes_retard' => $alertesRetard,
    74	        'performance_agents' => $performanceAgents,
    75	        'produits_plus_vendus' => $this->produitsPlusVendus(),
    76	        'evolution_encaissements' => $this->evolutionEncaissements(7),
    77	        'evolution_retards' => $this->retardsParJour(7),
    78	    ]);
    79	}
    80	    private function debiteursAjour(string $date): int
    81	    {
    82	        // Débiteurs actifs sans échéance impayée passée ou aujourd'hui
    83	        return DB::table('ventes')
    84	            ->where('ventes.statut', 'en_cours')
    85	            ->whereDate('ventes.date_debut', '<=', $date)
    86	            ->whereDate('ventes.date_fin', '>=', $date)
    87	            ->whereNotExists(function ($q) use ($date) {
    88	                $q->select(DB::raw(1))
    89	                  ->from('echeances')
    90	                  ->whereColumn('echeances.vente_id', 'ventes.id')
    91	                  ->whereDate('echeances.date_echeance', '<=', $date)
    92	                  ->where(function($q) use ($date) {
    93	                        $q->where('echeances.statut', 'en_retard')
    94	                          ->orWhere(function($q2) use ($date) {
    95	                              $q2->where('echeances.statut', 'partiel')
    96	                                 ->whereDate('echeances.date_echeance', '<', $date);
    97	                          });
    98	                    });
    99	            })
   100	            ->distinct('ventes.debiteur_id')
   101	            ->count();
   102	    }
   103	
   104	    private function debiteursEnRetard(string $date): int
   105	    {
   106	        return DB::table('ventes')
   107	            ->where('ventes.statut', 'en_cours')
   108	            ->whereDate('ventes.date_debut', '<=', $date)
   109	            ->whereDate('ventes.date_fin', '>=', $date)
   110	            ->whereExists(function ($q) use ($date) {
   111	                $q->select(DB::raw(1))
   112	                  ->from('echeances')
   113	                  ->whereColumn('echeances.vente_id', 'ventes.id')
   114	                  ->whereDate('echeances.date_echeance', '<=', $date)
   115	                  ->where(function($q) use ($date) {
   116	                        $q->where('echeances.statut', 'en_retard')
   117	                          ->orWhere(function($q2) use ($date) {
   118	                              $q2->where('echeances.statut', 'partiel')
   119	                                 ->whereDate('echeances.date_echeance', '<', $date);
   120	                          });
   121	                    });
   122	            })
   123	            ->distinct('ventes.debiteur_id')
   124	            ->count();
   125	    }
   126	
   127	    private function creancesTotales(): float
   128	    {
   129	        return Vente::where('statut', 'en_cours')->get()->sum(fn($v) => $v->resteAPayer());
   130	    }
   131	
   132	    private function restantAujourdhui(string $date): float
   133	    {
   134	        return DB::table('echeances')
   135	            ->whereDate('date_echeance', $date)
   136	            ->whereIn('statut', ['en_attente', 'en_retard', 'partiel'])
   137	            ->sum(DB::raw('montant_prevu - montant_paye'));
   138	    }
   139	
   140	    private function payeAujourdhui(string $date): int
   141	    {
   142	        return DB::table('ventes')->where('statut', 'en_cours')->whereDate('date_debut', '<=', $date)->whereDate('date_fin', '>=', $date)
   143	            ->whereExists(function ($q) use ($date) {
   144	                $q->select(DB::raw(1))->from('echeances')->whereColumn('echeances.vente_id', 'ventes.id')->whereDate('echeances.date_echeance', $date)->whereIn('echeances.statut', ['paye', 'partiel']);
   145	            })->distinct('debiteur_id')->count();
   146	    }
   147	
   148	    private function nonPayeAujourdhui(string $date): int
   149	    {
   150	        return DB::table('ventes')->where('statut', 'en_cours')->whereDate('date_debut', '<=', $date)->whereDate('date_fin', '>=', $date)
   151	            ->whereExists(function ($q) use ($date) {
   152	                $q->select(DB::raw(1))->from('echeances')->whereColumn('echeances.vente_id', 'ventes.id')->whereDate('echeances.date_echeance', $date)->whereIn('echeances.statut', ['en_attente', 'en_retard']);
   153	            })->distinct('debiteur_id')->count();
   154	    }
   155	
   156	    private function tauxRecouvrement(): float
   157	    {
   158	        $totalDu = Vente::where('statut', 'en_cours')->sum('montant_total');
   159	        if ($totalDu <= 0) return 0;
   160	        return round((Recouvrement::where('statut', 'paye')->sum('montant') / $totalDu) * 100, 1);
   161	    }
   162	
   163	    private function topAgents(string $date): array
   164	    {
   165	        return Recouvrement::whereDate('date_recouvrement', $date)->where('statut', 'paye')
   166	            ->selectRaw('agent_id, SUM(montant) as total_encaisse')->with('agent:id,uuid,nom,prenom')
   167	            ->groupBy('agent_id')->orderByDesc('total_encaisse')->get()->toArray();
   168	    }
   169	
   170	    private function alertesRetard(): array
   171	    {
   172	        return \App\Models\Echeance::where('statut', 'en_retard')
   173	            ->whereHas('vente', fn($q) => $q->where('statut', 'en_cours'))
   174	            ->with(['vente.debiteur:id,nom,quartier'])
   175	            ->orderByDesc('jours_retard')
   176	            ->limit(10)
   177	            ->get()
   178	            ->map(fn($e) => [
   179	                'debiteur' => [
   180	                    'nom' => $e->vente->debiteur->nom,
   181	                    'quartier' => $e->vente->debiteur->quartier,
   182	                ],
   183	                'statut' => 'Retard',
   184	            ])
   185	            ->toArray();
   186	    }
   187	
   188	    private function performanceAgents(): array
   189	    {
   190	        return User::where('role', 'agent')->where('actif', true)->withCount('debiteurs')
   191	            ->withSum(['ventes as portefeuille' => fn($q) => $q->where('statut', 'en_cours')], 'montant_total')
   192	            ->get(['id','uuid','nom','prenom'])->toArray();
   193	    }
   194	
   195	    private function produitsPlusVendus(): array
   196	    {
   197	        return DB::table('vente_produits as vp')
   198	            ->join('ventes as v', 'v.id', '=', 'vp.vente_id')
   199	            ->join('produits as p', 'p.id', '=', 'vp.produit_id')
   200	            ->where('v.statut', '!=', 'annule')
   201	            ->groupBy('vp.produit_id', 'p.nom')
   202	            ->selectRaw('vp.produit_id, p.nom as nom, SUM(vp.quantite) as nb_ventes, SUM(vp.quantite * vp.prix_unitaire) as total')
   203	            ->orderByDesc('nb_ventes')
   204	            ->limit(5)
   205	            ->get()
   206	            ->map(fn($r) => (array) $r)
   207	            ->toArray();
   208	    }
   209	
   210	    private function retardsParJour(int $jours): array
   211	    {
   212	        $labels = []; $retards = [];
   213	        for ($i = $jours - 1; $i >= 0; $i--) {
   214	            $date = now()->subDays($i)->toDateString();
   215	            $labels[] = now()->subDays($i)->format('d/m');
   216	            $retards[] = \App\Models\Echeance::whereDate('date_echeance', $date)
   217	                ->where('statut', 'en_retard')
   218	                ->whereHas('vente', fn($q) => $q->where('statut', 'en_cours'))
   219	                ->sum('montant_prevu');
   220	        }
   221	        return ['labels' => $labels, 'montants' => $retards];
   222	    }
   223	
   224	    private function evolutionEncaissements(int $jours): array
   225	    {
   226	        $labels = []; $montants = [];
   227	        for ($i = $jours - 1; $i >= 0; $i--) {
   228	            $date = now()->subDays($i)->toDateString();
   229	            $labels[] = now()->subDays($i)->format('d/m');
   230	            $montants[] = Recouvrement::whereDate('date_recouvrement', $date)->where('statut', 'paye')->sum('montant');
   231	        }
   232	        return ['labels' => $labels, 'montants' => $montants];
   233	    }
   234	}
