     1	<?php
     2	namespace App\Models;
     3	use Illuminate\Database\Eloquent\Model;
     4	
     5	class Debiteur extends Model
     6	{
     7	    protected $attributes = [
     8	        'credits_autorises' => 1,
     9	        'actif' => true,
    10	    ];
    11	
    12	    protected $fillable = [
    13	        'uuid', 'code_client', 'agent_id', 'nom', 'prenom', 'telephone',
    14	        'quartier', 'adresse', 'personne_reference_nom', 'personne_reference_tel',
    15	        'activite', 'score_solvabilite', 'credits_autorises', 'actif',
    16	        'ancien_agent_id', 'date_transfert',
    17	        'taux_recouvrement_au_transfert', 'reste_a_payer_au_transfert',
    18	    ];
    19	
    20	    protected $casts = [
    21	        'actif' => 'boolean',
    22	        'date_transfert' => 'datetime',
    23	        'score_solvabilite' => 'float',
    24	        'credits_autorises' => 'integer',
    25	    ];
    26	
    27	    protected static function booted(): void
    28	    {
    29	        static::creating(function (self $d) {
    30	            $d->uuid = $d->uuid ?: (string) \Illuminate\Support\Str::uuid();
    31	            if (blank($d->code_client)) {
    32	                do {
    33	                    $code = 'CLI-' . strtoupper(\Illuminate\Support\Str::random(8));
    34	                } while (self::where('code_client', $code)->exists());
    35	                $d->code_client = $code;
    36	            }
    37	        });
    38	    }
    39	
    40	    public function agent() { return $this->belongsTo(User::class, 'agent_id'); }
    41	    public function ancienAgent() { return $this->belongsTo(User::class, 'ancien_agent_id'); }
    42	    public function ventes() { return $this->hasMany(Vente::class); }
    43	    public function score() { return $this->hasOne(ScoreSolvabilite::class); }
    44	}

>>> app/Models/Echeance.php
     1	<?php
     2	namespace App\Models;
     3	use Illuminate\Database\Eloquent\Model;
     4	
     5	class Echeance extends Model
     6	{
     7	    protected $fillable = ['uuid','vente_id','date_echeance','montant_prevu','montant_paye','statut','jours_retard','date_paiement'];
     8	    protected $casts = ['date_echeance' => 'date', 'date_paiement' => 'date'];
     9	
    10	    public function vente() { return $this->belongsTo(Vente::class); }
    11	
    12	    public function restant(): float
    13	    {
    14	        return max(0, $this->montant_prevu - $this->montant_paye);
    15	    }
    16	
    17	    public function mettreAJourStatut(): void
    18	    {
    19	        if ($this->montant_paye >= $this->montant_prevu) {
    20	            $this->statut = 'paye';
    21	            $this->jours_retard = 0;
    22	        } elseif ($this->montant_paye > 0) {
    23	            $this->statut = 'partiel';
    24	        } elseif ($this->date_echeance->startOfDay()->lessThanOrEqualTo(now()->startOfDay())) {
    25	            $this->statut = 'en_retard';
    26	            $this->jours_retard = $this->date_echeance->startOfDay()->diffInDays(now()->startOfDay());
    27	        } else {
    28	            $this->statut = 'en_attente';
    29	            $this->jours_retard = 0;
    30	        }
    31	        $this->save();
    32	    }
    33	}

>>> app/Models/Permission.php
     1	<?php
     2	namespace App\Models;
     3	use Illuminate\Database\Eloquent\Model;
     4	
     5	class Permission extends Model
     6	{
     7	    protected $fillable = ['user_id', 'page', 'acces'];
     8	    protected $casts = ['acces' => 'boolean'];
     9

>>> app/Models/Produit.php
     1	<?php
     2	namespace App\Models;
     3	use Illuminate\Database\Eloquent\Model;
     4	
     5	class Produit extends Model
     6	{
     7	    protected $fillable = [
     8	        'uuid', 'categorie_id', 'code', 'nom', 'description',
     9	        'conditionnement', 'unite', 'prix_achat', 'prix_vente',
    10	        'stock', 'stock_initial', 'seuil_alerte', 'actif',
    11	    ];
    12	    
    13	    protected $casts = ['actif' => 'boolean'];
    14	    
    15	    public function categorie() { return $this->belongsTo(Categorie::class); }
    16	    public function approvisionnements() { return $this->hasMany(Approvisionnement::class); }
    17	    public function ventes() { return $this->hasMany(Vente::class); }
    18	    
    19	    // Stock logique = stock_initial + total approvisionnements - total ventes
    20	    public function stockLogique(): float
    21	    {
    22	        $totalAppros = $this->approvisionnements()->sum('quantite');
    23	        $totalVentes = $this->ventes()->sum('quantite') ?? 0;
    24	        return $this->stock_initial + $totalAppros - $totalVentes;
    25	    }
    26	}

     2

     9	class User extends Authenticatable

>>> app/Models/Vente.php
     1	<?php
     2	namespace App\Models;
     3	use Illuminate\Database\Eloquent\Model;
     4	use App\Observers\VenteObserver;
     5	
     6	class Vente extends Model
     7	{
     8	    protected static function booted(): void
     9	    {
    10	        static::observe(VenteObserver::class);
    11	    }
    12	
    13	    protected $fillable = [
    14	        'uuid','debiteur_id','agent_id','created_by','produit_id',
    15	        'type_vente','montant_total','montant_journalier',
    16	        'nombre_jours','delai_avant_echeances','epargne','epargne_par_jour','epargne_total',
    17	        'penalite_par_jour','penalite_active','motif_penalite','mise_agent',
    18	        'date_debut','date_fin','heure_creation','statut',
    19	    ];
    20	    protected $casts = ['date_debut' => 'date','date_fin' => 'date'];
    21	
    22	    public function debiteur() { return $this->belongsTo(Debiteur::class); }
    23	    public function agent() { return $this->belongsTo(User::class, 'agent_id'); }
    24	    public function createur() { return $this->belongsTo(User::class, 'created_by'); }
    25	    public function produit() { return $this->belongsTo(Produit::class); }
    26	    public function venteProduits() { return $this->hasMany(VenteProduit::class); }
    27	    public function recouvrements() { return $this->hasMany(Recouvrement::class); }
    28	    public function penalites() { return $this->hasMany(Penalite::class); }
    29	    public function echeances() { return $this->hasMany(Echeance::class)->orderBy('date_echeance'); }
    30	    public function demandeRemboursement() { return $this->hasOne(DemandeRemboursement::class); }
    31	
    32	    public function ratioEpargne(): float
    33	    {
    34	        return $this->montant_total > 0 ? ($this->epargne_total ?? 0) / $this->montant_total : 0;
    35	    }
    36	
    37	    public function montantEpargnePaye(): float
    38	    {
    39	        return round($this->totalPaye() * $this->ratioEpargne(), 2);
    40	    }
    41	
    42	    public function montantCreditPaye(): float
    43	    {
    44	        return round($this->totalPaye() - $this->montantEpargnePaye(), 2);
    45	    }
    46	
    47	    public function scopeActives($q) { return $q->whereIn('statut', ['en_cours', 'termine', 'defaut']); }
    48	
    49	    public function totalPaye(): float
    50	    {
    51	        return $this->recouvrements()->where('statut', 'paye')->sum('montant');
    52	    }
    53	    public function resteAPayer(): float
    54	    {
    55	        return max(0, $this->montant_total - $this->totalPaye());
    56	    }
    57	
    58	    // Tous les articles du panier, avec repli sur le produit unique (ventes historiques)
    59	    public function produitsPanier(): array
    60	    {
    61	        $items = $this->venteProduits()->with('produit')->get()->map(function ($vp) {
    62	            return [
    63	                'nom'           => $vp->produit?->nom ?? '--',
    64	                'quantite'      => (int) $vp->quantite,
    65	                'prix_unitaire' => (float) $vp->prix_unitaire,
    66	                'total'         => (float) $vp->quantite * (float) $vp->prix_unitaire,
    67	            ];
    68	        })->values()->all();
    69	
    70	        if (empty($items) && $this->produit) {
    71	            $items[] = [
    72	                'nom'           => $this->produit->nom,
    73	                'quantite'      => 1,
    74	                'prix_unitaire' => (float) ($this->produit->prix_vente ?? 0),
    75	                'total'         => (float) ($this->produit->prix_vente ?? 0),
    76	            ];
    77	        }
    78	        return $items;
    79	    }
    80	
    81	    // Générer les échéances
    82	    public function genererEcheances(): void
    83	    {
    84	        if ($this->type_vente !== 'credit') return;
    85	        $this->echeances()->delete();
    86	        $delai = max(0, (int) ($this->delai_avant_echeances ?? 1));
    87	        $reste = $this->montant_total;
    88	        $jours = (int) $this->nombre_jours;
    89	        $journalier = $this->montant_journalier;
    90	        for ($i = 0; $i < $jours; $i++) {
    91	            $montant = ($i === $jours - 1) ? $reste : $journalier;
    92	            $this->echeances()->create([
    93	                'uuid' => \Illuminate\Support\Str::uuid(),
    94	                'date_echeance' => $this->date_debut->copy()->addDays($delai + $i),
    95	                'montant_prevu' => $montant,
    96	                'statut' => 'en_attente',
    97	            ]);
    98	            $reste -= $montant;
    99	        }
   100	    }
   101	
   102	    // Mettre à jour le statut global de la vente
   103	    public function mettreAJourStatutGlobal(): void
   104	    {
   105	        $toutesPayees = $this->echeances()->where('statut', '!=', 'paye')->doesntExist();
   106	        if ($toutesPayees && $this->echeances()->count() > 0) {
   107	            $this->update(['statut' => 'termine']);
   108	        }
   109	    }
   110	}

    10	
    11	    public function vente() { return $this->belongsTo(Vente::class); }
    12	    public function produit() { return $this->belongsTo(Produit::class); }
    13	}

--- 9.2 Pour chaque table : colonnes (DESCRIBE) ---

===== agences =====
   id | int(11) | NO | Key=PRI | Default=
   uuid | varchar(36) | YES | Key=UNI | Default=
   nom | varchar(150) | YES | Key= | Default=
   adresse | text | YES | Key= | Default=
   telephone | varchar(20) | YES | Key= | Default=
   actif | tinyint(1) | YES | Key= | Default=1
   created_at | timestamp | YES | Key= | Default=current_timestamp()
   updated_at | timestamp | YES | Key= | Default=
   INDEX: PRIMARY (col=id, unique=yes)
   INDEX: uuid (col=uuid, unique=yes)

===== alertes =====
   id | bigint(20) unsigned | NO | Key=PRI | Default=
   uuid | char(36) | NO | Key=UNI | Default=
   vente_id | bigint(20) unsigned | YES | Key=MUL | Default=
   debiteur_id | bigint(20) unsigned | YES | Key=MUL | Default=
   produit_id | bigint(20) unsigned | YES | Key=MUL | Default=
   type | varchar(255) | NO | Key= | Default=
   titre | varchar(255) | NO | Key= | Default=
   message | text | YES | Key= | Default=
   niveau | enum('info','avertissement','critique') | NO | Key= | Default=info
   lue | tinyint(1) | NO | Key=MUL | Default=0
   date_alerte | timestamp | NO | Key= | Default=current_timestamp()
   created_at | timestamp | YES | Key= | Default=
   updated_at | timestamp | YES | Key= | Default=
   INDEX: PRIMARY (col=id, unique=yes)
   INDEX: alertes_uuid_unique (col=uuid, unique=yes)
   INDEX: alertes_vente_id_foreign (col=vente_id, unique=no)
   INDEX: alertes_debiteur_id_foreign (col=debiteur_id, unique=no)
   INDEX: alertes_produit_id_foreign (col=produit_id, unique=no)
   INDEX: alertes_lue_date_alerte_index (col=lue, unique=no)
   INDEX: alertes_lue_date_alerte_index (col=date_alerte, unique=no)
   FK: alertes_debiteur_id_foreign -> debiteurs.id (col=debiteur_id)
   FK: alertes_produit_id_foreign -> produits.id (col=produit_id)
   FK: alertes_vente_id_foreign -> ventes.id (col=vente_id)

===== approvisionnements =====
   id | bigint(20) unsigned | NO | Key=PRI | Default=
   uuid | char(36) | NO | Key=UNI | Default=
   produit_id | bigint(20) unsigned | NO | Key=MUL | Default=
   quantite | decimal(10,2) | NO | Key= | Default=
   prix_achat_unitaire | decimal(10,2) | NO | Key= | Default=0.00
   prix_vente_unitaire | decimal(10,2) | NO | Key= | Default=0.00
   fournisseur | varchar(255) | YES | Key= | Default=
   reference | varchar(255) | YES | Key= | Default=
   user_id | bigint(20) unsigned | YES | Key=MUL | Default=
   created_at | timestamp | YES | Key= | Default=
   updated_at | timestamp | YES | Key= | Default=
   INDEX: PRIMARY (col=id, unique=yes)
   INDEX: approvisionnements_uuid_unique (col=uuid, unique=yes)
   INDEX: approvisionnements_produit_id_foreign (col=produit_id, unique=no)
   INDEX: approvisionnements_user_id_foreign (col=user_id, unique=no)
   FK: approvisionnements_produit_id_foreign -> produits.id (col=produit_id)
   FK: approvisionnements_user_id_foreign -> users.id (col=user_id)

===== audit_logs =====
   id | bigint(20) unsigned | NO | Key=PRI | Default=
   gerante_id | bigint(20) unsigned | NO | Key=MUL | Default=
   ancien_agent_id | bigint(20) unsigned | NO | Key=MUL | Default=

   heure_recouvrement | time | YES | Key= | Default=
   mode_paiement | enum('especes','mobile','autre') | NO | Key= | Default=especes
   statut | enum('paye','partiel','refus','absent','promesse') | NO | Key= | Default=paye

   synced | tinyint(1) | NO | Key= | Default=0
   sync_uuid | char(36) | YES | Key= | Default=
   created_at | timestamp | YES | Key= | Default=
   updated_at | timestamp | YES | Key= | Default=
   INDEX: PRIMARY (col=id, unique=yes)
   INDEX: recouvrements_uuid_unique (col=uuid, unique=yes)
   INDEX: recouvrements_vente_id_foreign (col=vente_id, unique=no)
   INDEX: recouvrements_agent_id_foreign (col=agent_id, unique=no)
   FK: recouvrements_agent_id_foreign -> users.id (col=agent_id)
   FK: recouvrements_vente_id_foreign -> ventes.id (col=vente_id)

===== ventes =====
   id | bigint(20) unsigned | NO | Key=PRI | Default=
   uuid | char(36) | NO | Key=UNI | Default=
   debiteur_id | bigint(20) unsigned | NO | Key=MUL | Default=
   agent_id | bigint(20) unsigned | NO | Key=MUL | Default=
   created_by | bigint(20) unsigned | YES | Key=MUL | Default=
   produit_id | bigint(20) unsigned | YES | Key=MUL | Default=
   type_vente | enum('comptant','credit') | NO | Key= | Default=credit
   montant_total | decimal(12,2) | NO | Key= | Default=
   montant_journalier | decimal(12,2) | NO | Key= | Default=
   nombre_jours | int(11) | NO | Key= | Default=

   epargne | decimal(12,2) | NO | Key= | Default=0.00
   epargne_par_jour | decimal(8,2) | NO | Key= | Default=0.00
   epargne_total | decimal(10,2) | NO | Key= | Default=0.00
   penalite_par_jour | decimal(8,2) | NO | Key= | Default=1000.00
   penalite_active | tinyint(1) | NO | Key= | Default=1
   motif_penalite | text | YES | Key= | Default=
   mise_agent | decimal(8,2) | NO | Key= | Default=0.00
   date_debut | date | NO | Key= | Default=
   date_fin | date | NO | Key= | Default=
   heure_creation | time | YES | Key= | Default=
   statut | enum('en_cours','termine','defaut','annule') | NO | Key= | Default=en_cours
   created_at | timestamp | YES | Key= | Default=
   updated_at | timestamp | YES | Key= | Default=
   INDEX: PRIMARY (col=id, unique=yes)
   INDEX: ventes_uuid_unique (col=uuid, unique=yes)
   INDEX: ventes_debiteur_id_foreign (col=debiteur_id, unique=no)
   INDEX: ventes_agent_id_foreign (col=agent_id, unique=no)
   INDEX: ventes_produit_id_foreign (col=produit_id, unique=no)
   INDEX: ventes_created_by_index (col=created_by, unique=no)
   FK: ventes_agent_id_foreign -> users.id (col=agent_id)
   FK: ventes_debiteur_id_foreign -> debiteurs.id (col=debiteur_id)
   FK: ventes_produit_id_foreign -> produits.id (col=produit_id)

============================================================
== 10. FICHIERS FRONTEND AGENT (contenu intégral)

===== public/agent-app/app.js =====
     1	const AgentApp = {
     2	  API: 'https://sourougnon.alwaysdata.net',
     3	  token: localStorage.getItem('agent_token'),
     4	  user: JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('agent_user') || '{}'),
     5	  tourneeData: JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('agent_tournee_cache') || 'null'),
     6	  debiteursData: JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('agent_debiteurs_cache') || '[]'),
     7	  currentTab: 'tous',
     8	  currentVente: null,
     9	  currentClient: null,
    10	  tourneeEnCours: false,
    11	
    12	  async login(identifiant, password) {
    13	    try {
    14	      const res = await fetch(this.API + '/api/auth/login', {
    15	        method: 'POST',
    16	        headers: {'Content-Type': 'application/json'},
    17	        body: JSON.stringify({identifiant, password, role: 'agent'})
    18	      });
    19	      const data = await res.json();
    20	      if (data.token) {
    21	        localStorage.setItem('agent_token', data.token);
    22	        localStorage.setItem('agent_user', JSON.stringify(data.user || {}));
    23	        this.token = data.token;
    24	        this.user = data.user || {};
    25	        return {success: true};
    26	      }
    27	      return {error: data.error || 'Erreur de connexion'};
    28	    } catch (e) {
    29	      return {error: 'Réseau indisponible'};
    30	    }
    31	  },
    32	
    33	  logout() {
    34	    localStorage.removeItem('agent_token');
    35	    localStorage.removeItem('agent_user');
    36	    localStorage.removeItem('agent_tournee_cache');
    37	    localStorage.removeItem('agent_debiteurs_cache');
    38	    window.location.href = 'login.html';
    39	  },
    40	
    41	  async _call(path, options = {}) {
    42	    let res;
    43	    try {
    44	      res = await fetch(this.API + '/api' + path, {
    45	        ...options,
    46	        headers: {
    47	          ...(options.headers || {}),
    48	          'Authorization': 'Bearer ' + (this.token || ''),
    49	          'Accept': 'application/json'
    50	        }
    51	      });
    52	    } catch (e) {
    53	      return { error: 'Hors ligne ou réseau indisponible', networkError: true };
    54	    }
    55	
    56	    if (res.status === 401) {
    57	      this.logout();
    58	      return { error: 'Session expirée' };
    59	    }
    60	
    61	    try {
    62	      const data = await res.json();
    63	      if (!res.ok) {
    64	        return { error: data.message || data.error || 'Erreur ' + res.status, httpError: res.status };
    65	      }
    66	      return data;
    67	    } catch (e) {
    68	      return { error: 'Réponse invalide du serveur', httpError: res.status };
    69	    }
    70	  },
    71	
    72	  async apiGet(path) {
    73	    const data = await this._call(path);
    74	    if (data && !data.error) {
    75	      try { await window.SourougnonDB.setCached(path, data); } catch (e) {}
    76	      return data;
    77	    }
    78	    // Réseau mort ou erreur : tentative cache
    79	    try {
    80	      const cached = await window.SourougnonDB.getCached(path);
    81	      if (cached) return cached;
    82	    } catch (e) {}
    83	    return data;
    84	  },
    85	
    86	  apiPost(path, body) {
    87	    return this._call(path, {
    88	      method: 'POST',
    89	      headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
    90	      body: JSON.stringify(body)
    91	    });
    92	  },
    93	
    94	  async apiPostOrQueue(path, body) {
    95	    // Idempotence : générer un sync_uuid unique et stable pour cette opération
    96	    if (!body.sync_uuid) {
    97	      body.sync_uuid = (crypto.randomUUID ? crypto.randomUUID() : 'sync-' + Date.now() + '-' + Math.random().toString(36).slice(2));
    98	    }
    99	
   100	    if (navigator.onLine) {
   101	      const res = await this.apiPost(path, body);
   102	      if (!res.networkError) {
   103	        return res;
   104	      }
   105	      // Réseau mort détecté, on retombe dans la file
   106	    }
   107	
   108	    try {
   109	      const op = {
   110	        endpoint: path,
   111	        body: body,
   112	        createdAt: new Date().toISOString(),
   113	        synced: false
   114	      };
   115	      const id = await window.SourougnonDB.addPending(op);
   116	      console.log('Opération mise en attente hors ligne:', id, path, body.sync_uuid);
   117	      return { success: true, offline: true, message: 'Opération enregistrée localement. Synchronisation requise.' };
   118	    } catch (e) {
   119	      console.error('Erreur IndexedDB:', e);
   120	      return { error: 'Impossible de sauvegarder hors ligne.' };
   121	    }
   122	  },
   123	
   124	  async confirmClearPending() {
   125	    const pending = await window.SourougnonDB.getAllPending();
   126	    if (pending.length === 0) {
   127	      this.ui.showAlert('Aucune opération en attente.');
   128	      return;
   129	    }
   130	    const ok = await this.ui.confirm(`Vider la file d'attente ? (${pending.length} opération(s) seront perdues.)`);
   131	    if (!ok) return;
   132	    await window.SourougnonDB.clearPending();
   133	    this.ui.showAlert('File purgée.');
   134	    await this.loadTournee();
   135	  },
   136	
   137	  async syncAll() {
   138	    if (!navigator.onLine) {
   139	      this.ui.showAlert('Hors ligne. Synchronisation impossible.');
   140	      return false;
   141	    }
   142	    const pending = await window.SourougnonDB.getAllPending();
   143	    if (pending.length === 0) {
   144	      this.ui.showAlert('Aucune opération en attente.');
   145	      return true;
   146	    }
   147	    let successCount = 0;
   148	    for (const op of pending) {
   149	      try {
   150	        const res = await this.apiPost(op.endpoint, op.body);
   151	        if (res.error) {
   152	          console.warn('Sync échouée pour', op.endpoint, res.error);
   153	        } else {
   154	          await window.SourougnonDB.deletePending(op.id);
   155	          successCount++;
   156	        }
   157	      } catch (e) {
   158	        console.error('Erreur réseau pendant sync:', e);
   159	        break;
   160	      }
   161	    }
   162	    const remaining = await window.SourougnonDB.getAllPending();
   163	    if (remaining.length === 0) {
   164	      this.ui.showAlert('Synchronisation terminée : ' + successCount + ' opération(s) envoyée(s).');
   165	    } else {
   166	      this.ui.showAlert('Synchronisation partielle : ' + successCount + ' envoyée(s), ' + remaining.length + ' restante(s).');
   167	    }
   168	    await this.loadTournee();
   169	    await this.loadDebiteurs();
   170	    return remaining.length === 0;
   171	  },
   172	
   173	  showHome() { this.showScreen('home'); },
   174	
   175	  async cacheDebiteurs(list) {
   176	    try {
   177	      const db = await openDB();
   178	      const tx = db.transaction('debiteurs', 'readwrite');
   179	      const store = tx.objectStore('debiteurs');
   180	      if (!store) return;
   181	      for (const d of list) {
   182	        store.put(d);
   183	      }
   184	    } catch (e) {}
   185	  },
   186	
   187	  async getDebiteurLocal(uuid) {
   188	    try {
   189	      const db = await openDB();
   190	      const tx = db.transaction('debiteurs', 'readonly');
   191	      const store = tx.objectStore('debiteurs');
   192	      if (!store) return null;
   193	      const req = store.get(uuid);
   194	      return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
   195	        req.onsuccess = () => resolve(req.result || null);
   196	        req.onerror = () => resolve(null);
   197	      });
   198	    } catch (e) { return null; }
   199	  },
   200	
   201	  async getAllDebiteursLocal() {
   202	    try {
   203	      const db = await openDB();
   204	      const tx = db.transaction('debiteurs', 'readonly');
   205	      const store = tx.objectStore('debiteurs');
   206	      if (!store) return [];
   207	      const req = store.getAll();
   208	      return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
   209	        req.onsuccess = () => resolve(req.result || []);
   210	        req.onerror = () => resolve([]);
   211	      });
   212	    } catch (e) { return []; }
   213	  },
   214	
   215	
   216	  async init() {
   217	    if (!this.token) { window.location.href = 'login.html'; return; }
   218	    if (this.user.nom) {
   219	      document.getElementById('greeting').textContent = 'Bonjour ' + this.user.nom + ' ' + (this.user.prenom || '') + ' 👋';
   220	      document.getElementById('userInitials').textContent = (this.user.nom?.[0] || 'A') + (this.user.prenom?.[0] || 'G');
   221	    }
   222	    this.setToday();
   223	    await this.preloadAllData();
   224	    await this.loadTournee();
   225	    await this.loadTourneeStatut();
   226	    this.showHome();
   227	  },
   228	
   229	  setToday() {
   230	    const now = new Date();
   231	    document.getElementById('todayLabel').textContent = new Intl.DateTimeFormat('fr-FR', {weekday:'long', day:'numeric', month:'long', year:'numeric'}).format(now);
   232	  },
   233	
   234	  async loadTourneeStatut() {
   235	    const res = await this.apiGet('/agent/tournee/statut');
   236	    this.tourneeEnCours = res.en_cours || false;
   237	    this.renderTourneeButton();
   238	  },
   239	
   240	  renderTourneeButton() {
   241	    const btn = document.getElementById('btnToggleTournee');
   242	    if (!btn) return;
   243	    if (this.tourneeEnCours) {
   244	      btn.textContent = '✓ Terminer la tournée';
   245	      btn.classList.remove('cta--blue');
   246	      btn.classList.add('cta--green');
   247	    } else {
   248	      btn.textContent = '➤ Démarrer la tournée';
   249	      btn.classList.add('cta--blue');
   250	      btn.classList.remove('cta--green');
   251	    }
   252	  },
   253	
   254	  async toggleTournee() {
   255	    if (!navigator.onLine) {
   256	      const opType = this.tourneeEnCours ? 'terminer' : 'demarrer';
   257	      await window.SourougnonDB.addPending({ endpoint: this.tourneeEnCours ? '/agent/tournee/terminer' : '/agent/tournee/demarrer', body: {}, createdAt: new Date().toISOString(), synced: false });
   258	      this.tourneeEnCours = !this.tourneeEnCours;
   259	      this.renderTourneeButton();
   260	      this.ui.showAlert('Tournée enregistrée localement. Synchronisation requise.');
   261	      return;
   262	    }
   263	    const res = this.tourneeEnCours ? await this.apiPost('/agent/tournee/terminer', {}) : await this.apiPost('/agent/tournee/demarrer', {});
   264	    if (res.error) { this.ui.showAlert(res.error); return; }
   265	    this.tourneeEnCours = !this.tourneeEnCours;
   266	    this.renderTourneeButton();
   267	    this.ui.showAlert(this.tourneeEnCours ? 'Tournée démarrée.' : 'Tournée terminée.');
   268	  },
   269	
   270	  async loadTournee() {
   271	    // Si hors ligne et cache dispo, utiliser le cache
   272	    if (!navigator.onLine && this.tourneeData) {
   273	      this.renderHomeKPIs();
   274	      this.renderHomeList();
   275	      return;
   276	    }
   277	    const d = await this.apiGet('/agent-space/tournee');
   278	    if (d.error) {
   279	      if (this.tourneeData) {
   280	        this.renderHomeKPIs();
   281	        this.renderHomeList();
   282	        this.ui.showAlert('Hors ligne. Affichage de la dernière tournée.');
   283	      } else {
   284	        this.ui.showAlert('Impossible de charger la tournée.');
   285	      }
   286	      return;
   287	    }
   288	    this.tourneeData = d;
   289	    localStorage.setItem('agent_tournee_cache', JSON.stringify(d));
   290	    this.renderHomeKPIs();
   291	    this.renderHomeList();
   292	  },
   293	
   294	  async preloadAllData() {
   295	    const endpoints = [
   296	      '/agent-space/tournee',
   297	      '/agent/debiteurs',
   298	      '/agent/epargnes',
   299	      '/agent/penalites',
   300	      '/notifications',
   301	      '/agent/tournee/statut',
   302	      '/auth/me',
   303	    ];
   304	
   305	    for (const endpoint of endpoints) {
   306	      try {
   307	        const data = await this.apiGet(endpoint);
   308	        if (!data.error) {
   309	          await window.SourougnonDB.setCached(endpoint, data);
   310	          console.log('✅ préchargé:', endpoint);
   311	        }
   312	      } catch (e) {
   313	        console.warn('Erreur préchargement', endpoint, e);
   314	      }
   315	    }
   316	    // Précharger aussi les détails des débiteurs
   317	    const debiteurs = await this.apiGet('/agent/debiteurs');
   318	    if (Array.isArray(debiteurs)) {
   319	      for (const d of debiteurs.slice(0, 50)) {
   320	        try {
   321	          const detail = await this.apiGet('/agent/debiteurs/' + d.uuid);
   322	          if (!detail.error) {
   323	            await window.SourougnonDB.setCached('/agent/debiteurs/' + d.uuid, detail);
   324	          }
   325	        } catch (e) {}
   326	      }
   327	    }
   328	  },
   329	
   330	  async loadDebiteurs() {
   331	    if (!navigator.onLine) {
   332	      const cached = await window.SourougnonDB.getCached('/agent/debiteurs');
   333	      if (cached) {
   334	        this.debiteursData = cached;
   335	        return;
   336	      }
   337	    }
   338	    const d = await this.apiGet('/agent/debiteurs');
   339	    if (!d.error) {
   340	      this.debiteursData = d;
   341	      try { await window.SourougnonDB.setCached('/agent/debiteurs', d); } catch(e) {}
   342	    }
   343	  },
   344	
   345	  renderHomeKPIs() {
   346	    const d = this.tourneeData || {};
   347	    const stats = d.stats || {};
   348	    const total = stats.nb_total || 0;
   349	    const paye = stats.nb_paye || 0;
   350	    const retards = stats.nb_retard ?? 0;
   351	    const collecte = stats.montant_collecte_aujourdhui || 0;
   352	    const soldeImpaye = stats.solde_impaye || 0;
   353	    const attendu = stats.montant_attendu || 0;
   354	    const montantRetard = stats.montant_retard || 0;
   355	    const score = attendu > 0 ? Math.round((collecte / attendu) * 100) : 0;
   356	
   357	    document.getElementById('heroVisits').textContent = total + ' visite(s) planifiée(s)';
   358	    document.getElementById('heroSummary').textContent = (stats.nb_a_visiter || 0) + ' à visiter • ' + paye + ' payés • ' + retards + ' en retard';
   359	    document.getElementById('heroScore').textContent = score + '%';
   360	    document.getElementById('statVisites').textContent = total;
   361	    document.getElementById('statPaiements').textContent = paye;
   362	    document.getElementById('statRetards').textContent = retards;
   363	    document.getElementById('statCollecte').textContent = collecte.toLocaleString('fr-FR') + ' F';
   364	    document.getElementById('statSolde').textContent = soldeImpaye.toLocaleString('fr-FR') + ' F';
   365	    document.getElementById('statRetardImpaye').textContent = montantRetard.toLocaleString('fr-FR') + ' F';
   366	  },
   367	
   368	  renderHomeList() {
   369	    const d = this.tourneeData || {};
   370	    const all = [...(d.a_visiter || []), ...(d.paye || []), ...(d.absent || []), ...(d.refus || []), ...(d.promesse || [])];
   371	    const filters = ['tous', 'a_visiter', 'paye', 'retard', 'absent', 'refus', 'promesse'];
   372	    const labels = {'tous':'Tous','a_visiter':'À visiter','paye':'Payés','retard':'Retards','absent':'Absents','refus':'Refus','promesse':'Promesses'};
   373	    document.getElementById('homeFilters').innerHTML = filters.map(f => {
   374	      let count = 0;
   375	      if (f === 'tous') count = all.length;
   376	      else if (f === 'retard') count = all.filter(v => v.statut_global === 'en_retard').length;
   377	      else if (f === 'paye') count = all.filter(v => v.statut_visite === 'paye').length;
   378	      else if (f === 'absent') count = all.filter(v => v.statut_visite === 'absent').length;
   379	      else if (f === 'refus') count = all.filter(v => v.statut_visite === 'refus').length;
   380	      else if (f === 'promesse') count = all.filter(v => v.statut_visite === 'promesse').length;
   381	      else count = all.filter(v => !['paye','absent','refus','promesse'].includes(v.statut_visite)).length;
   382	      return `<button class="chip ${f===this.currentTab?'chip--active':''}" data-filter="${f}">${labels[f]} <span>${count}</span></button>`;
   383	    }).join('');
   384	
   385	    document.querySelectorAll('#homeFilters .chip').forEach(chip => {
   386	      chip.addEventListener('click', () => {
   387	        this.currentTab = chip.dataset.filter;
   388	        this.renderHomeList();
   389	      });
   390	    });
   391	
   392	    const filtered = this.filterHome(all);
   393	    const money = v => new Intl.NumberFormat('fr-FR', {style:'currency', currency:'XOF', maximumFractionDigits:0}).format(v||0);
   394	    document.getElementById('clientList').innerHTML = filtered.length === 0
   395	      ? '<p class="muted" style="text-align:center;padding:20px;">Aucun client</p>'
   396	      : filtered.map(v => {
   397	          let statutClass = 'status--success';
   398	          let statutTexte = 'À jour';
   399	          if (v.statut_global === 'en_retard') { statutClass = 'status--danger'; statutTexte = 'En retard'; }
   400	          else if (v.statut_visite === 'paye') { statutTexte = 'Payé'; }
   401	          else if (v.statut_visite === 'absent') { statutTexte = 'Absent'; statutClass = 'status--warning'; }
   402	          else if (v.statut_visite === 'refus') { statutTexte = 'Refus'; statutClass = 'status--warning'; }
   403	          else if (v.statut_visite === 'promesse') { statutTexte = 'Promesse'; statutClass = 'status--warning'; }
   404	          else { statutTexte = 'À visiter'; statutClass = 'status--warning'; }
   405	          return `<article class="client-card">
   406	            <div class="avatar avatar--blue">${v.debiteur?.charAt(0) || '?'}${v.debiteur?.charAt(v.debiteur.indexOf(' ')+1) || ''}</div>
   407	            <div class="client-card__main">
   408	              <div class="client-card__title">
   409	                <div><h3>${v.debiteur}</h3><p>${v.quartier || '-'}</p></div>
   410	                <span class="status ${statutClass}">${statutTexte}</span>
   411	              </div>
   412	              <div class="client-card__meta"><span>📞 ${v.telephone || '-'}</span></div>
   413	              <div class="client-card__bottom">
   414	                <strong>${money(v.montant_journalier)}</strong>
   415	                <button class="small-btn small-btn--primary" ${v.peut_encaisser === false ? 'disabled style="opacity:.5;cursor:not-allowed;"' : ''} onclick='AgentApp.openVenteFromTournee(${JSON.stringify(v)})'>${v.peut_encaisser === false ? 'Déjà payé' : 'Voir client'}</button>
   416	              </div>
   417	            </div>
   418	          </article>`;
   419	        }).join('');
   420	  },
   421	
   422	  filterHome(all) {
   423	    if (this.currentTab === 'tous') return all;
   424	    if (this.currentTab === 'retard') return all.filter(v => v.statut_global === 'en_retard');
   425	    if (this.currentTab === 'paye') return all.filter(v => v.statut_visite === 'paye');
   426	    if (this.currentTab === 'absent') return all.filter(v => v.statut_visite === 'absent');
   427	    if (this.currentTab === 'refus') return all.filter(v => v.statut_visite === 'refus');
   428	    if (this.currentTab === 'promesse') return all.filter(v => v.statut_visite === 'promesse');
   429	    return all.filter(v => !['paye','absent','refus','promesse'].includes(v.statut_visite));
   430	  },
   431	
   432	  async openVenteFromTournee(v) {
   433	    if (!this.tourneeEnCours) {
   434	      this.ui.showAlert(`Démarrez votre tournée avant d'encaisser.`);
   435	      return;
   436	    }
   437	    this.currentVente = v;
   438	    await this.openClient(v.debiteur_uuid);
   439	  },
   440	
   441	  async openClient(uuid) {
   442	    let deb = null;
   443	    try {
   444	      deb = await window.SourougnonDB.getCached('/agent/debiteurs/' + uuid);
   445	    } catch (e) {}
   446	
   447	    if (!deb && navigator.onLine) {
   448	      const apiDeb = await this.apiGet('/agent/debiteurs/' + uuid);
   449	      if (!apiDeb.error) {
   450	        deb = apiDeb;
   451	      }
   452	    }
   453	
   454	    if (!deb) {
   455	      this.ui.showAlert('Fiche client non disponible hors-ligne.');
   456	      return;
   457	    }
   458	
   459	    this.currentClient = deb;
   460	    this.currentVente = deb.ventes && deb.ventes.length > 0 ? deb.ventes.find(v => v.statut === 'en_cours') : null;
   461	    this.renderClientDetail(deb);
   462	    this.showScreen('client');
   463	  },
   464	
   465	  renderClientDetail(deb) {
   466	    const money = v => new Intl.NumberFormat('fr-FR', {style:'currency', currency:'XOF', maximumFractionDigits:0}).format(v||0);
   467	    const initial = (deb.nom?.[0] || '') + (deb.prenom?.[0] || '');
   468	    const score = deb.score_solvabilite ?? (deb.score?.score ?? '—');
   469	
   470	    const ventesHtml = (deb.ventes || []).length === 0
   471	      ? '<p class="muted">Aucune vente</p>'
   472	      : deb.ventes.map(v => {
   473	          const produits = v.vente_produits?.length
   474	            ? v.vente_produits.map(vp => `<li>${vp.produit?.nom || '—'} × ${vp.quantite}</li>`).join('')
   475	            : (v.produit ? `<li>${v.produit.nom} × 1</li>` : '');
   476	          return `<div class="info-card">
   477	            <div class="info-row"><span>Type</span><strong>${v.type_vente === 'credit' ? 'Crédit' : 'Comptant'}</strong></div>
   478	            <div class="info-row"><span>Montant total</span><strong>${money(v.montant_total)}</strong></div>
   479	            <div class="info-row"><span>Payé</span><strong>${money(v.total_paye || 0)}</strong></div>
   480	            <div class="info-row"><span>Restant</span><strong>${money(v.reste_a_payer || 0)}</strong></div>
   481	            <div class="info-row"><span>Jours payés</span><strong>${v.jours_payes || 0}/${v.jours_total || 0}</strong></div>
   482	            <div class="info-row"><span>Statut</span><strong>${v.statut}</strong></div>
   483	            <ul>${produits}</ul>
   484	          </div>`;
   485	        }).join('');
   486	
   487	    document.getElementById('clientDetail').innerHTML = `
   488	      <div class="profile-card">
   489	        <div class="avatar avatar--xl avatar--blue">${initial}</div>
   490	        <div><h2>${deb.nom} ${deb.prenom || ''}</h2><p class="muted">${deb.activite || '-'}</p><span class="status status--success">Client actif</span></div>
   491	        <div class="hero-banner__score"><span class="hero-banner__score-label">Score</span><strong>${score}</strong></div>
   492	      </div>
   493	      <div class="detail-list">
   494	        <div class="detail-item"><span>📞</span><div><small>Téléphone</small><strong>${deb.telephone || '-'}</strong></div></div>
   495	        <div class="detail-item"><span>📍</span><div><small>Quartier</small><strong>${deb.quartier || '-'}</strong></div></div>
   496	        <div class="detail-item"><span>📅</span><div><small>Client depuis</small><strong>${new Date(deb.created_at).toLocaleDateString('fr-FR')}</strong></div></div>
   497	      </div>
   498	      <h3 style="margin-top:16px;">Historique des ventes</h3>
   499	      ${ventesHtml}
   500	      <button class="cta cta--blue" onclick="AgentApp.showCollection()">💳 Encaisser un paiement</button>
   501	    `;
   502	  },
   503	
   504	  showCollection() {
   505	    if (!this.tourneeEnCours) {
   506	      this.ui.showAlert(`Démarrez votre tournée avant d'encaisser.`);
   507	      return;
   508	    }
   509	    if (!this.currentVente) {
   510	      this.ui.showAlert('Veuillez d\'abord choisir un client dans la tournée ou la page débiteurs.');
   511	      this.showScreen('home');
   512	      return;
   513	    }
   514	    const money = v => new Intl.NumberFormat('fr-FR', {style:'currency', currency:'XOF', maximumFractionDigits:0}).format(v||0);
   515	    document.getElementById('collectionClient').innerHTML = `
   516	      <div class="collection-client">
   517	        <div class="avatar avatar--blue">${(this.currentClient?.nom?.[0]||'')}</div>
   518	        <div><h3>${this.currentClient?.nom || ''} ${this.currentClient?.prenom||''}</h3><p class="muted">Encours actuel</p></div>
   519	        <strong>${money(this.currentVente.reste_a_payer || 0)}</strong>
   520	      </div>
   521	    `;
   522	    document.getElementById('amount').value = (this.currentVente.montant_journalier || '').toString().replace(',', '.');
   523	    document.getElementById('comment').value = '';
   524	    this.showScreen('collection');
   525	  },
   526	
   527	  showScreen(name) {
   528	    document.querySelectorAll('.screen').forEach(s => s.classList.remove('is-active'));
   529	    const map = {home:'screen-home', client:'screen-client', collection:'screen-collection', success:'screen-success', debtors:'screen-debtors', notifications:'screen-notifications', profile:'screen-profile', plus:'screen-plus', epargnes:'screen-epargnes', penalites:'screen-penalites'};
   530	    document.getElementById(map[name]).classList.add('is-active');
   531	    document.querySelectorAll('.nav-item').forEach(btn => btn.classList.toggle('is-active', btn.dataset.screen === name));
   532	    window.scrollTo({top:0, behavior:'smooth'});
   533	  },
   534	
   535	  async openDebtors() {
   536	    this.showScreen('debtors');
   537	    document.getElementById('debtorsList').innerHTML =
   538	      '<p class="muted" style="text-align:center;padding:20px;">Chargement…</p>';
   539	
   540	    try { await this.loadDebiteurs(); } catch (e) { console.warn(e); }
   541	
   542	    let d = this.debiteursData;
   543	    if (!Array.isArray(d) || !d.length) {
   544	      const cached = await window.SourougnonDB.getCached('/agent/debiteurs');
   545	      d = Array.isArray(cached) ? cached : (cached?.debiteurs || cached?.data || []);
   546	      this.debiteursData = d;
   547	    }
   548	
   549	    this.renderDebtors(d);
   550	  },
   551	
   552	  renderDebtors(d) {
   553	    const money = v => new Intl.NumberFormat('fr-FR', {style:'currency', currency:'XOF', maximumFractionDigits:0}).format(v||0);
   554	    document.getElementById('debtorsCount').textContent = (d || []).length + ' client(s) attitré(s)';
   555	    document.getElementById('debtorsList').innerHTML = (!d || d.length === 0)
   556	      ? '<p class="muted" style="text-align:center;padding:20px;">Aucun débiteur</p>'
   557	      : d.map(deb => `<article class="debtor-card">
   558	          <div class="avatar avatar--blue">${(deb.nom?.[0] || '')}${(deb.prenom?.[0] || '')}</div>
   559	          <div class="debtor-main">
   560	            <div class="client-card__title"><div><h3>${deb.nom} ${deb.prenom || ''}</h3><p>${deb.activite || '-'}</p></div><span class="status ${deb.statut_global === 'en_retard' ? 'status--danger' : 'status--success'}">${deb.statut_global === 'en_retard' ? 'EN RETARD' : 'À JOUR'}</span></div>
   561	            <div class="debtor-grid">
   562	              <div><small>Restant</small><strong>${money(deb.reste_a_payer_total || 0)}</strong></div>
   563	            </div>
   564	            <div class="debtor-actions">
   565	              <button class="small-btn small-btn--secondary" onclick="AgentApp.callClient('${deb.telephone || ''}')">📞 Appeler</button>
   566	              <button class="small-btn small-btn--primary" onclick="AgentApp.openClient('${deb.uuid}')">Voir</button>
   567	            </div>
   568	          </div>
   569	        </article>`).join('');
   570	  },
   571	
   572	  callClient(tel) { if (tel) window.location.href = 'tel:' + tel; },
   573	
   574	  async submitEncaisser(e) {
   575	    e.preventDefault();
   576	    if (!this.currentVente) { this.ui.showAlert('Aucune vente sélectionnée'); return; }
   577	    const data = {
   578	      vente_id: this.currentVente.uuid,
   579	      montant: parseFloat(document.getElementById('amount').value) || 0,
   580	      mode_paiement: document.getElementById('paymentMode').value,
   581	      statut: document.getElementById('paymentStatus').value,
   582	      commentaire: document.getElementById('comment').value,
   583	    };
   584	    const res = await this.apiPostOrQueue('/agent-space/encaisser', data);
   585	    if (res.error) { this.ui.showAlert(res.error); return; }
   586	    if (res.offline) {
   587	      this.ui.showAlert('Paiement enregistré localement.');
   588	    } else {
   589	      this.showSuccess(res, data);
   590	    }
   591	    await this.loadTournee();
   592	  },
   593	
   594	  showSuccess(res, data) {
   595	    document.getElementById('successReceipt').innerHTML = `
   596	      <div><small>Client</small><strong>${this.currentClient?.nom || '-'}</strong></div>
   597	      <div><small>Montant</small><strong class="text-green">${data.montant.toLocaleString('fr-FR')} FCFA</strong></div>
   598	      <div><small>Date</small><strong>${new Intl.DateTimeFormat('fr-FR', {day:'2-digit', month:'long', year:'numeric', hour:'2-digit', minute:'2-digit'}).format(new Date())}</strong></div>
   599	      <div><small>Mode</small><strong>${data.mode_paiement}</strong></div>
   600	    `;
   601	    this.showScreen('success');
   602	    setTimeout(() => this.loadTournee(), 1000);
   603	  },
   604	
   605	  async loadNotifications() {
   606	    const notifs = await this.apiGet('/notifications');
   607	    if (notifs.error) { this.ui.showAlert(notifs.error); return; }
   608	    const list = Array.isArray(notifs) ? notifs : (notifs.notifications || []);
   609	    document.getElementById('notifBadge').textContent = list.filter(n => !n.lue).length;
   610	    document.getElementById('notifCountSmall').textContent = list.filter(n => !n.lue).length;
   611	    document.getElementById('notificationList').innerHTML = list.length === 0
   612	      ? '<p class="muted" style="text-align:center;padding:20px;">Aucune notification</p>'
   613	      : list.map(n => `<article class="notification-card">
   614	          <div class="notification-icon ${n.type === 'stock' ? 'notification-icon--red' : 'notification-icon--blue'}">${n.type === 'echeance' ? '⏰' : n.type === 'retard' ? '!' : n.type === 'stock' ? '📦' : '💬'}</div>
   615	          <div><h3>${n.titre}</h3><p>${n.message}</p><time>${new Date(n.created_at).toLocaleDateString('fr-FR')}</time></div>
   616	        </article>`).join('');
   617	    this.showScreen('notifications');
   618	  },
   619	
   620	  async openEpargnes() {
   621	    const list = await this.apiGet('/agent/epargnes');
   622	    if (list.error) { this.ui.showAlert(list.error); return; }
   623	    const money = v => new Intl.NumberFormat('fr-FR', {style:'currency', currency:'XOF', maximumFractionDigits:0}).format(v||0);
   624	    const total = list.reduce((sum, e) => sum + parseFloat(e.montant || 0), 0);
   625	    const collecte = list.filter(e => e.statut === 'collecte');
   626	    document.getElementById('agentEpargnesContent').innerHTML = `
   627	      <div class="stats-grid">
   628	        <div class="metric-card"><div class="metric-card__icon metric-card__icon--green">₣</div><div><span>Total</span><strong>${money(total)}</strong></div></div>
   629	        <div class="metric-card"><div class="metric-card__icon metric-card__icon--blue">✓</div><div><span>Collectes</span><strong>${list.length}</strong></div></div>
   630	      </div>
   631	      <div class="client-list">
   632	        ${collecte.length === 0 ? '<p class="muted" style="text-align:center;padding:20px;">Aucune collecte</p>' :
   633	          collecte.map(e => `
   634	            <div class="client-card">
   635	              <div class="avatar avatar--green">${(e.debiteur?.nom?.[0] || '')}${(e.debiteur?.prenom?.[0] || '')}</div>
   636	              <div class="client-card__main">
   637	                <div class="client-card__title">
   638	                  <div><h3>${e.debiteur?.nom} ${e.debiteur?.prenom}</h3><p>${new Date(e.date_collecte).toLocaleDateString('fr-FR')}</p></div>
   639	                  <span class="status status--success">Collectée</span>
   640	                </div>
   641	                <strong>${money(e.montant)}</strong>
   642	              </div>
   643	            </div>`).join('')
   644	        }
   645	      </div>
   646	    `;
   647	    this.showScreen('epargnes');
   648	  },
   649	
   650	  async openPenalites() {
   651	    const list = await this.apiGet('/agent/penalites');
   652	    if (list.error) { this.ui.showAlert(list.error); return; }
   653	    const money = v => new Intl.NumberFormat('fr-FR', {style:'currency', currency:'XOF', maximumFractionDigits:0}).format(v||0);
   654	    const enAttente = list.filter(p => p.statut === 'en_attente');
   655	    document.getElementById('agentPenalitesContent').innerHTML = `
   656	      <div class="stats-grid">
   657	        <div class="metric-card"><div class="metric-card__icon metric-card__icon--red">⚠</div><div><span>En attente</span><strong>${money(enAttente.reduce((sum,p)=>sum+parseFloat(p.montant||0),0))}</strong></div></div>
   658	        <div class="metric-card"><div class="metric-card__icon metric-card__icon--green">✓</div><div><span>Recouvrées</span><strong>${list.filter(p => p.statut === 'payee').length}</strong></div></div>
   659	      </div>
   660	      <div class="client-list">
   661	        ${enAttente.length === 0 ? '<p class="muted" style="text-align:center;padding:20px;">Aucune pénalité en attente</p>' :
   662	          enAttente.map(p => `
   663	            <div class="debtor-card">
   664	              <div class="avatar avatar--red">${(p.debiteur?.nom?.[0] || '')}${(p.debiteur?.prenom?.[0] || '')}</div>
   665	              <div class="debtor-main">
   666	                <div class="client-card__title">
   667	                  <div><h3>${p.debiteur?.nom} ${p.debiteur?.prenom}</h3><p>Retard: ${p.jours_retard} jour(s)</p></div>
   668	                  <span class="status status--danger">${money(p.montant)}</span>
   669	                </div>
   670	                <div class="debtor-actions">
   671	                  <button class="small-btn small-btn--primary" onclick="AgentApp.payerPenalite('${p.uuid}')">Payer</button>
   672	                  <button class="small-btn small-btn--danger" onclick="AgentApp.refuserPenalite('${p.uuid}')">Refus</button>
   673	                </div>
   674	              </div>
   675	            </div>`).join('')
   676	        }
   677	      </div>
   678	    `;
   679	    this.showScreen('penalites');
   680	  },
   681	
   682	  async payerPenalite(uuid) {
   683	    if (!(await this.ui.confirm("Confirmer le paiement de cette pénalité ?"))) return;
   684	    const res = await this.apiPostOrQueue(`/agent/penalites/${uuid}/payer`, {mode_paiement: 'especes'});
   685	    if (res.error) { this.ui.showAlert(res.error); return; }
   686	    this.ui.showAlert(res.offline ? 'Pénalité enregistrée localement.' : (res.message || 'Pénalité payée.'));
   687	    this.openPenalites();
   688	  },
   689	
   690	  async refuserPenalite(uuid) {
   691	    if (!(await this.ui.confirm("Signaler le refus de payer ? L'épargne sera débitée."))) return;
   692	    const res = await this.apiPostOrQueue(`/agent/penalites/${uuid}/refuser`, {});
   693	    if (res.error) { this.ui.showAlert(res.error); return; }
   694	    this.ui.showAlert(res.offline ? 'Refus enregistré localement.' : (res.message || 'Refus enregistré.'));
   695	    this.openPenalites();
   696	  },
   697	
   698	  async loadProfile() {
   699	    const me = await this.apiGet('/auth/me');
   700	    if (me.error) { this.ui.showAlert(me.error); return; }
   701	    this.user = me.user || {};
   702	    localStorage.setItem('agent_user', JSON.stringify(this.user));
   703	    const u = this.user;
   704	    document.getElementById('profileContent').innerHTML = `
   705	      <div class="profile-card profile-card--big">
   706	        <div class="avatar avatar--xxl avatar--blue">${(u.nom?.[0]||'')}${(u.prenom?.[0]||'')}</div>
   707	        <div><h2>${u.nom} ${u.prenom}</h2><p class="muted">Agent terrain • Bénin</p><span class="status status--success">Actif</span></div>
   708	      </div>
   709	      <div class="info-card">
   710	        <div class="info-row"><span>Téléphone</span><strong>${u.telephone || '-'}</strong></div>
   711	        <div class="info-row"><span>Email</span><strong>${u.email || '-'}</strong></div>
   712	        <div class="info-row"><span>Rôle</span><strong>${u.role || 'agent'}</strong></div>
   713	      </div>
   714	    `;
   715	    this.showScreen('profile');
   716	  },
   717	
   718	  setupEvents() {
   719	    document.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
   720	      const btn = e.target.closest('[data-screen]');
   721	      if (btn) {
   722	        const name = btn.dataset.screen;
   723	        if (name === 'debtors') this.openDebtors();
   724	        else if (name === 'notifications') this.loadNotifications();
   725	        else if (name === 'profile') this.loadProfile();
   726	        else if (name === 'plus') this.showScreen('plus');
   727	        else if (name === 'epargnes') this.openEpargnes();
   728	        else if (name === 'penalites') this.openPenalites();
   729	        else if (name === 'collection') this.showCollection();
   730	        else this.showScreen(name);
   731	      }
   732	    });
   733	  },
   734	
   735	  updateNetwork() {
   736	    const online = navigator.onLine;
   737	    document.getElementById('networkPill').classList.toggle('network-pill--offline', !online);
   738	    document.getElementById('networkPill').classList.toggle('network-pill--online', online);
   739	    document.querySelector('.network-label').textContent = online ? 'En ligne' : 'Hors ligne';
   740	    document.getElementById('offlineBar').classList.toggle('is-hidden', online);
   741	  },
   742	
   743	  ui: {
   744	    showAlert(message) {
   745	      const backdrop = document.getElementById('ui-modal-backdrop');
   746	      if (!backdrop) return;
   747	      document.getElementById('ui-modal-icon').textContent = 'ℹ️';
   748	      document.getElementById('ui-modal-message').textContent = message;
   749	      document.getElementById('ui-modal-actions').innerHTML = `<button class="cta cta--blue" style="margin-top:0;min-height:44px;" onclick="AgentApp.ui.closeAlert()">OK</button>`;
   750	      backdrop.style.display = 'flex';
   751	    },
   752	    closeAlert() {
   753	      document.getElementById('ui-modal-backdrop').style.display = 'none';
   754	    },
   755	    async confirm(message) {
   756	      const backdrop = document.getElementById('ui-modal-backdrop');
   757	      if (!backdrop) return false;
   758	      return new Promise((resolve) => {
   759	        document.getElementById('ui-modal-icon').textContent = '⚠️';
   760	        document.getElementById('ui-modal-message').textContent = message;
   761	        document.getElementById('ui-modal-actions').innerHTML = `
   762	          <button class="cta cta--outline" style="margin-top:0;min-height:44px;" onclick="AgentApp.ui.resolveConfirm(false)">Annuler</button>
   763	          <button class="cta cta--blue" style="margin-top:0;min-height:44px;" onclick="AgentApp.ui.resolveConfirm(true)">Confirmer</button>
   764	        `;
   765	        AgentApp.ui._resolve = resolve;
   766	        backdrop.style.display = 'flex';
   767	      });
   768	    },
   769	    resolveConfirm(value) {
   770	      document.getElementById('ui-modal-backdrop').style.display = 'none';
   771	      if (this._resolve) { this._resolve(value); this._resolve = null; }
   772	    },
   773	  },
   774	};
   775	
   776	document.getElementById('collectionForm').addEventListener('submit', (e) => AgentApp.submitEncaisser(e));
   777	document.getElementById('syncBtn').addEventListener('click', () => AgentApp.syncAll());
   778	window.addEventListener('online', () => { AgentApp.updateNetwork(); AgentApp.syncAll(); });
   779	window.addEventListener('offline', () => AgentApp.updateNetwork());
   780	
   781	if (document.getElementById('splash') && document.getElementById('app')) {
   782	  setTimeout(() => {
   783	    document.getElementById('splash').style.opacity = '0';
   784	    document.getElementById('splash').style.transition = 'opacity .45s ease';
   785	    setTimeout(() => {
   786	      document.getElementById('splash').remove();
   787	      document.getElementById('app').classList.remove('is-hidden');
   788	      AgentApp.setupEvents();
   789	      AgentApp.updateNetwork();
   790	      AgentApp.init();
   791	    }, 450);
   792	  }, 1200);
   793	}

===== public/agent-app/db.js =====
     1	// db.js — IndexedDB pour l'application agent
     2	const DB_NAME = 'sourougnon-agent-db';
     3	const DB_VERSION = 2;
     4	const STORE_PENDING = 'pending_operations';
     5	const STORE_CACHE = 'cached_responses';
     6	
     7	let dbPromise = null;
     8	
     9	function openDB() {
    10	  if (dbPromise) return dbPromise;
    11	  dbPromise = new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
    12	    const req = indexedDB.open(DB_NAME, DB_VERSION);
    13	    req.onupgradeneeded = (e) => {
    14	      const db = e.target.result;
    15	      if (!db.objectStoreNames.contains(STORE_PENDING)) {
    16	        db.createObjectStore(STORE_PENDING, { keyPath: 'id', autoIncrement: true });
    17	      }
    18	      if (!db.objectStoreNames.contains(STORE_CACHE)) {
    19	        db.createObjectStore(STORE_CACHE, { keyPath: 'key' });
    20	      }
    21	    };
    22	    req.onsuccess = (e) => resolve(e.target.result);
    23	    req.onerror = (e) => reject(e.target.error);
    24	  });
    25	  return dbPromise;
    26	}
    27	
    28	async function addPending(operation) {
    29	  const db = await openDB();
    30	  return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
    31	    const tx = db.transaction(STORE_PENDING, 'readwrite');
    32	    const store = tx.objectStore(STORE_PENDING);
    33	    const req = store.add(operation);
    34	    req.onsuccess = () => resolve(req.result);
    35	    req.onerror = () => reject(req.error);
    36	  });
    37	}
    38	
    39	async function getAllPending() {
    40	  const db = await openDB();
    41	  return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
    42	    const tx = db.transaction(STORE_PENDING, 'readonly');
    43	    const store = tx.objectStore(STORE_PENDING);
    44	    const req = store.getAll();
    45	    req.onsuccess = () => resolve(req.result || []);
    46	    req.onerror = () => reject(req.error);
    47	  });
    48	}
    49	
    50	async function deletePending(id) {
    51	  const db = await openDB();
    52	  return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
    53	    const tx = db.transaction(STORE_PENDING, 'readwrite');
    54	    const store = tx.objectStore(STORE_PENDING);
    55	    const req = store.delete(id);
    56	    req.onsuccess = () => resolve();
    57	    req.onerror = () => reject(req.error);
    58	  });
    59	}
    60	
    61	async function clearPending() {
    62	  const db = await openDB();
    63	  return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
    64	    const tx = db.transaction(STORE_PENDING, 'readwrite');
    65	    const store = tx.objectStore(STORE_PENDING);
    66	    const req = store.clear();
    67	    req.onsuccess = () => resolve();
    68	    req.onerror = () => reject(req.error);
    69	  });
    70	}
    71	
    72	// ==================== CACHE GÉNÉRIQUE ====================
    73	async function setCached(key, value) {
    74	  const db = await openDB();
    75	  return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
    76	    const tx = db.transaction(STORE_CACHE, 'readwrite');
    77	    const store = tx.objectStore(STORE_CACHE);
    78	    store.put({ key, value, updatedAt: new Date().toISOString() });
    79	    tx.oncomplete = () => resolve();
    80	    tx.onerror = () => reject(tx.error);
    81	  });
    82	}
    83	
    84	async function getCached(key) {
    85	  const db = await openDB();
    86	  return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
    87	    const tx = db.transaction(STORE_CACHE, 'readonly');
    88	    const store = tx.objectStore(STORE_CACHE);
    89	    const req = store.get(key);
    90	    req.onsuccess = () => resolve(req.result ? req.result.value : null);
    91	    req.onerror = () => resolve(null);
    92	  });
    93	}
    94	
    95	window.SourougnonDB = { addPending, getAllPending, deletePending, clearPending, setCached, getCached };

===== public/agent-app/sw.js =====
     1	const CACHE = 'sourougnon-agent-v7';
     2	const ASSETS = ['./','./login.html','./index.html','./styles.css','./app.js','./db.js','./manifest.json'];
     3	
     4	self.addEventListener('install', (e) => {
     5	  e.waitUntil((async () => {
     6	    const cache = await caches.open(CACHE);
     7	    await Promise.allSettled(ASSETS.map(u => cache.add(u)));
     8	    self.skipWaiting();
     9	  })());
    10	});
    11	
    12	self.addEventListener('activate', (e) => {
    13	  e.waitUntil((async () => {
    14	    const keys = await caches.keys();
    15	    await Promise.all(keys.filter(k => k !== CACHE).map(k => caches.delete(k)));
    16	    self.clients.claim();
    17	  })());
    18	});
    19	
    20	self.addEventListener('fetch', (e) => {
    21	  const url = new URL(e.request.url);
    22	  if (url.pathname.startsWith('/api/')) return;
    23	
    24	  if (e.request.mode === 'navigate') {
    25	    e.respondWith(
    26	      fetch(e.request)
    27	        .then(r => { const cl = r.clone(); caches.open(CACHE).then(c => c.put(e.request, cl)); return r; })
    28	        .catch(() => caches.match(e.request).then(m => m || caches.match('./index.html')))
    29	    );
    30	    return;
    31	  }
    32	
    33	  e.respondWith(
    34	    caches.match(e.request).then(m => m || fetch(e.request).then(r => {
    35	      const cl = r.clone(); caches.open(CACHE).then(c => c.put(e.request, cl)); return r;
    36	    }))
    37	  );
    38	});

   317	<script>
   318	
   319	</script>
   320	<script>
   321	  if ('serviceWorker' in navigator) {

===== public/agent-app/login.html =====
     1	<!DOCTYPE html>
     2	<html lang="fr">
     3	<head>
     4	  <meta charset="UTF-8" />
     5	  <meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1, viewport-fit=cover" />
     6	  <meta name="theme-color" content="#0F172A" />
     7	  <link rel="stylesheet" href="styles.css" />
     8	  <title>Sourougnon Agent - Connexion</title>
     9	</head>
    10	<body>
    11	  <div class="login-screen">
    12	    <div class="splash__logo-wrap" style="margin-bottom:20px;">
    13	      <img src="assets/logo-placeholder.svg" alt="Logo" class="splash__logo" />
    14	    </div>
    15	    <div class="splash__brand">Sourou<span>gnon</span></div>
    16	    <div class="splash__baseline">Crédit • Vente • Confiance</div>
    17	
    18	    <div class="login-card">
    19	      <h2>Bonjour !</h2>
    20	      <p class="muted" style="margin-bottom:18px;">Connectez-vous pour continuer</p>
    21	      <form id="loginForm">
    22	        <input type="text" id="identifiant" placeholder="Téléphone ou email" required />
    23	        <input type="password" id="password" placeholder="Mot de passe" required />
    24	        <button type="submit">SE CONNECTER</button>
    25	        <div id="loginError" class="login-error"></div>
    26	      </form>
    27	    </div>
    28	  </div>
    29	
    30	  <script>
    31	    // Anti-boucle : rediriger vers index si token présent, une seule fois
    32	    (function() {
    33	      const token = localStorage.getItem('agent_token');
    34	      if (token && !sessionStorage.getItem('redir_agent')) {
    35	        sessionStorage.setItem('redir_agent', '1');
    36	        location.replace('index.html');
    37	      }
    38	    })();
    39	
    40	    const API_BASE = 'https://sourougnon.alwaysdata.net';
    41	
    42	    document.getElementById('loginForm').addEventListener('submit', async function(e) {
    43	      e.preventDefault();
    44	      const identifiant = document.getElementById('identifiant').value.trim();
    45	      const password = document.getElementById('password').value;
    46	      const err = document.getElementById('loginError');
    47	      err.textContent = '';
    48	
    49	      try {
    50	        const res = await fetch(API_BASE + '/api/auth/login', {
    51	          method: 'POST',
    52	          headers: {'Content-Type': 'application/json', 'Accept': 'application/json'},
    53	          body: JSON.stringify({identifiant, password, role: 'agent'})
    54	        });
    55	        const data = await res.json();
    56	
    57	        if (data.token) {
    58	          localStorage.setItem('agent_token', data.token);
    59	          localStorage.setItem('agent_user', JSON.stringify(data.user || {}));
    60	          window.location.href = 'index.html?login=ok';
    61	        } else {
    62	          err.textContent = data.error || 'Identifiants incorrects.';
    63	        }
    64	      } catch (e) {
    65	        console.error('Erreur réseau login:', e);
    66	        err.textContent = 'Réseau indisponible. Vérifiez votre connexion.';
    67	      }
    68	    });
    69	  </script>
    70	
    71	<script>
    72	if ('serviceWorker' in navigator) {
    73	  navigator.serviceWorker.register('sw.js').catch(function(err){ console.log('SW error', err); });
    74	}
    75	</script>
    76	</body>
    77	</html>

     3	function loadAgentSpace(c){apiFetch('/agent-space/tournee').then(function(d){function f(v){return new Intl.NumberFormat('fr-FR',{style:'currency',currency:'XOF',maximumFractionDigits:0}).format(v||0);}var s=d.stats;var tabs=['a_visiter','paye','absent','refus','promesse'];var labels={'a_visiter':'A visiter','paye':'Paye','absent':'Absent','refus':'Refus','promesse':'Promesse'};var currentTab='a_visiter';function renderTab(tab){var list=d[tab]||[];return list.map(function(v){var badgeRetard=v.statut_global==='en_retard'?'<span class=\"badge badge-danger\">En retard</span>':'<span class=\"badge badge-success\">A jour</span>';var onclick='openEncaisserModal(&#39;'+v.uuid+'&#39;,&#39;'+v.debiteur.replace(/'/g,'')+'&#39;,&#39;'+(v.quartier||'').replace(/'/g,'')+'&#39;,&#39;'+(v.telephone||'').replace(/'/g,'')+'&#39;,'+v.montant_journalier+','+v.reste_a_payer+',&#39;'+v.statut_global+'&#39;,&#39;'+v.statut_visite+'&#39;)';return '<tr><td class=\"font-medium\">'+v.debiteur+'</td><td>'+(v.quartier||'-')+'</td><td>'+(v.telephone||'-')+'</td><td>'+f(v.montant_journalier)+'</td><td>'+f(v.reste_a_payer)+'</td><td>'+(v.jours_payes||0)+'/'+(v.jours_total||0)+' j</td><td>'+badgeRetard+'</td><td><button onclick=\"'+onclick+'\" class=\"bg-green-600 text-white px-3 py-1 rounded-lg text-xs font-semibold hover:bg-green-700\">Encaisser</button></td></tr>';}).join('');}c.innerHTML='<div class=\"fade-in space-y-6\"><h3 class=\"text-xl font-bold\">Mon Espace Agent</h3><div class=\"kpi-grid\"><div class=\"kpi-card\" style=\"border-left:3px solid #0066ff\"><div class=\"kpi-label\">Total</div><div class=\"kpi-value\">'+s.nb_total+'</div></div><div class=\"kpi-card\" style=\"border-left:3px solid #00c853\"><div class=\"kpi-label\">Paye</div><div class=\"kpi-value\">'+s.nb_paye+'</div></div><div class=\"kpi-card\" style=\"border-left:3px solid #ef4444\"><div class=\"kpi-label\">Absent</div><div class=\"kpi-value\">'+s.nb_absent+'</div></div><div class=\"kpi-card\" style=\"border-left:3px solid #f59e0b\"><div class=\"kpi-label\">Attendu</div><div class=\"kpi-value\">'+f(s.montant_attendu)+'</div></div></div><div class=\"flex gap-2 overflow-x-auto pb-2\">'+tabs.map(function(t){return '<button onclick=\"switchAgentTab(&#39;'+t+'&#39;)\" class=\"agent-tab px-4 py-2 rounded-lg text-sm font-semibold whitespace-nowrap '+(t===currentTab?'bg-accent text-white':'bg-slate-200 text-slate-700')+'\" data-tab=\"'+t+'\">'+labels[t]+' ('+(d[t]||[]).length+')</button>';}).join('')+'</div><div class=\"panel\"><div class=\"table-wrapper\"><table><thead><tr><th>Debiteur</th><th>Quartier</th><th>Tel</th><th>Journalier</th><th>Restant</th><th>Jours</th><th>Statut</th><th>Action</th></tr></thead><tbody id=\"agent-table-body\">'+renderTab(currentTab)+'</tbody></table></div></div></div>';window.switchAgentTab=function(tab){currentTab=tab;document.querySelectorAll('.agent-tab').forEach(function(b){b.className='agent-tab px-4 py-2 rounded-lg text-sm font-semibold whitespace-nowrap '+(b.getAttribute('data-tab')===tab?'bg-accent text-white':'bg-slate-200 text-slate-700');});document.getElementById('agent-table-body').innerHTML=renderTab(tab);};}).catch(function(){c.innerHTML='<div class=\"panel text-center py-12\"><p class=\"text-muted\">Erreur chargement.</p></div>';});}

     5	function openEncaisserModal(uuid,debiteur,quartier,tel,attendu,reste,statutGlobal,statutVisite){var modal=document.getElementById('encaisser-modal'); if(modal){modal.classList.remove('hidden'); modal.style.display='flex';}document.getElementById('enc-vente-id').value=uuid;document.getElementById('enc-debiteur').textContent=debiteur;document.getElementById('enc-quartier').textContent=quartier||'-';document.getElementById('enc-telephone').textContent=tel||'-';document.getElementById('enc-attendu').textContent=new Intl.NumberFormat('fr-FR',{style:'currency',currency:'XOF',maximumFractionDigits:0}).format(attendu);document.getElementById('enc-reste').textContent=new Intl.NumberFormat('fr-FR',{style:'currency',currency:'XOF',maximumFractionDigits:0}).format(reste);document.getElementById('enc-statut-global').innerHTML=statutGlobal==='en_retard'?'<span class=\"badge badge-danger\">En retard</span>':'<span class=\"badge badge-success\">A jour</span>';document.getElementById('enc-montant').value=attendu;document.getElementById('enc-statut').value='paye';document.getElementById('enc-commentaire').value='';document.getElementById('enc-commentaire-obligatoire').classList.add('hidden');document.getElementById('encaisser-modal').classList.remove('hidden');}
     6	function closeEncaisserModal(){var modal=document.getElementById('encaisser-modal'); if(modal){modal.classList.add('hidden'); modal.style.display='none';}}
     7	function onStatutChange(){var s=document.getElementById('enc-statut').value;var o=document.getElementById('enc-commentaire-obligatoire');var c=document.getElementById('enc-commentaire');if(s==='absent'||s==='refus'||s==='promesse'){o.classList.remove('hidden');c.required=true;}else{o.classList.add('hidden');c.required=false;}}
     8	function submitEncaisser(e){e.preventDefault();var data={vente_id:document.getElementById('enc-vente-id').value,montant:parseFloat(document.getElementById('enc-montant').value)||0,mode_paiement:document.getElementById('enc-mode').value,statut:document.getElementById('enc-statut').value,commentaire:document.getElementById('enc-commentaire').value};apiFetch('/agent-space/encaisser',{method:'POST',body:JSON.stringify(data)}).then(function(r){if(r.error){showAlert(r.error);return;}closeEncaisserModal();showAlert(r.message || 'Opération réussie.');loadPage('agent-space');});}

     1	function loadAgents(c){apiFetch('/agents').then(function(a){c.innerHTML='<div class="fade-in space-y-6"><div class="flex items-center justify-between"><h3 class="text-xl font-bold">Agents</h3><button onclick="showAddAgentModal()" class="bg-accent text-white px-4 py-2 rounded-lg text-sm font-semibold hover:bg-blue-700">+ Nouvel Agent</button></div><div class="panel"><div class="table-wrapper"><table><thead><tr><th>Nom</th><th>Email</th><th>Telephone</th><th>Statut</th><th>Actions</th></tr></thead><tbody>'+a.map(function(x){return '<tr><td class="font-medium">'+x.nom+' '+(x.prenom||'')+'</td><td>'+x.email+'</td><td>'+(x.telephone||'--')+'</td><td><span class="badge '+(x.actif?'badge-success':'badge-danger')+'">'+(x.actif?'Actif':'Inactif')+'</span></td><td><div class="flex gap-2"><button onclick="resetAgentPassword(\''+x.uuid+'\',\''+x.nom+'\')" class="text-amber-600 text-xs">Cle</button><button onclick="confirmDeleteAgent(\''+x.uuid+'\',\''+x.nom+' '+(x.prenom||'')+'\')" class="text-red-600 text-xs">Suppr</button></div></td></tr>';}).join('')+'</tbody></table></div></div></div>';});}
     2	function showAddAgentModal(){document.getElementById('add-agent-modal').classList.remove('hidden');}
     3	function closeAddAgentModal(){document.getElementById('add-agent-modal').classList.add('hidden');}
     4	function createAgent(e){e.preventDefault();apiFetch('/agents',{method:'POST',body:JSON.stringify({nom:document.getElementById('agent-nom').value,prenom:document.getElementById('agent-prenom').value,email:document.getElementById('agent-email').value,telephone:document.getElementById('agent-telephone').value,password:document.getElementById('agent-password').value})}).then(function(){closeAddAgentModal();loadPage('agents');});}
     5	function confirmDeleteAgent(u,n){currentDeleteAgentUuid=u;document.getElementById('delete-agent-name').textContent='Agent : '+n;document.getElementById('delete-agent-modal').classList.remove('hidden');loadAgentsForTransfer();}
     6	function closeDeleteAgentModal(){document.getElementById('delete-agent-modal').classList.add('hidden');}
     7	function loadAgentsForTransfer(){apiFetch('/agents').then(function(a){document.getElementById('transfer-agent-select').innerHTML='<option value="">Selectionner...</option>'+a.filter(function(x){return x.uuid!==currentDeleteAgentUuid&&x.actif;}).map(function(x){return '<option value="'+x.uuid+'">'+x.nom+' '+(x.prenom||'')+'</option>';}).join('');toggleDeleteButton();});}
     8	function toggleDeleteButton(){var b=document.getElementById('confirm-delete-btn');var s=document.getElementById('transfer-agent-select');if(b&&s){if(s.value){b.disabled=false;b.className='flex-1 px-4 py-2.5 bg-red-600 text-white rounded-xl text-sm font-semibold hover:bg-red-700 transition-colors';}else{b.disabled=true;b.className='flex-1 px-4 py-2.5 bg-slate-300 text-slate-500 rounded-xl text-sm font-semibold cursor-not-allowed transition-colors';}}}
     9	function exportAgentData(){apiFetch('/agents/export/'+currentDeleteAgentUuid).then(function(d){var csv='Agent: '+d.agent+'\n\nDebiteur,Telephone,Quartier,Vente,Montant,Paye,Restant,Statut\n';d.donnees.forEach(function(x){csv+=[x['Debiteur'],x['Telephone'],x['Quartier'],x['Vente'],x['Montant total'],x['Paye'],x['Restant'],x['Statut']].join(',')+'\n';});var a=document.createElement('a');a.href=URL.createObjectURL(new Blob([csv],{type:'text/csv'}));a.download='agent_'+d.agent.replace(' ','_')+'.csv';a.click();});}
    10	function deleteAgent(){var t=document.getElementById('transfer-agent-select').value;if(!t){showAlert('Veuillez sélectionner un agent de remplacement.');return;}apiFetch('/agents/'+currentDeleteAgentUuid,{method:'DELETE',body:JSON.stringify({transfer_to:t})}).then(function(r){closeDeleteAgentModal();showAlert(r.message||'Supprime.');loadPage('agents');});}
    11	function resetAgentPassword(u,n){currentPasswordAgentUuid=u;document.getElementById('password-agent-name').textContent='Agent : '+n;document.getElementById('password-modal').classList.remove('hidden');}
    12	function closePasswordModal(){document.getElementById('password-modal').classList.add('hidden');}
    13	function changeAgentPassword(e){e.preventDefault();apiFetch('/agents/'+currentPasswordAgentUuid,{method:'PUT',body:JSON.stringify({password:document.getElementById('new-password').value})}).then(function(){closePasswordModal();showAlert('Mot de passe modifié avec succès.');});}

     1	function loadAlertes(c){apiFetch('/alertes').then(function(a){var nbNonLues=a.filter(function(x){return!x.lue;}).length;var badge=document.getElementById('alert-badge');if(badge)badge.classList.toggle('hidden',nbNonLues===0);var typeBadge={retard:'badge-danger',echeance_jour:'badge-info',stock_faible:'badge-warning',credit_finissant:'badge-warning'};var niveauBadge={info:'badge-info',avertissement:'badge-warning',critique:'badge-danger'};c.innerHTML='<div class="fade-in space-y-6"><div class="flex items-center justify-between"><h3 class="text-xl font-bold">Alertes</h3><button onclick="marquerToutesLues()" class="bg-slate-200 text-slate-700 px-4 py-2 rounded-lg text-sm font-semibold hover:bg-slate-300">Tout marquer lu</button></div><div class="panel"><div class="table-wrapper"><table><thead><tr><th>Date</th><th>Type</th><th>Niveau</th><th>Message</th><th>Etat</th></tr></thead><tbody>'+(a.length>0?a.map(function(x){return '<tr class="'+(x.lue?'':'bg-amber-50')+'"><td class="text-xs">'+x.date_alerte.substring(0,10)+'</td><td><span class="badge '+(typeBadge[x.type]||'badge-info')+'">'+x.type+'</span></td><td><span class="badge '+(niveauBadge[x.niveau]||'badge-info')+'">'+x.niveau+'</span></td><td>'+x.message+'</td><td>'+(x.lue?'<span class="text-green-600 text-xs">Lu</span>':'<span class="text-amber-600 text-xs font-semibold">Nouveau</span>')+'</td></tr>';}).join(''):'<tr><td colspan="5" class="text-center text-muted py-4">Aucune alerte</td></tr>')+'</tbody></table></div></div></div>';});}
     2	function marquerToutesLues(){apiFetch('/alertes/toutes-lues',{method:'PUT',body:'{}'}).then(function(){loadPage('alertes');});}

     3	var API_BASE = '/api';
     4	var authToken = '';
     5	var currentUser = {};
     6	var dashboardChart = null;
     7	var currentDeleteAgentUuid = null;
     8	var currentPasswordAgentUuid = null;

    10	try { authToken = localStorage.getItem('sourougnon_token') || ''; } catch (e) {}
    11	try { var b = localStorage.getItem('sourougnon_user'); currentUser = b ? JSON.parse(b) : {}; } catch (e) { localStorage.removeItem('sourougnon_user'); currentUser = {}; }
    12	if (!authToken) window.location.href = '/dashboard/login.html';
    13	
    14	document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function () {
    15	    function S(id, v) { var e = document.getElementById(id); if (e) e.textContent = v; }
    16	    if (currentUser && currentUser.nom) {
    17	        S('user-name', currentUser.nom);
    18	        S('user-initials', (currentUser.prenom || currentUser.nom).charAt(0).toUpperCase());
    19	        var g = document.getElementById('greeting'); if (g) g.innerHTML = 'Bonjour ' + (currentUser.prenom || currentUser.nom);
    20	    }
    21	    S('current-date', new Date().toLocaleDateString('fr-FR', { weekday: 'long', year: 'numeric', month: 'long', day: 'numeric' }));
    22	    var nav = document.querySelectorAll('.nav-item');
    23	    nav.forEach(function (item) {
    24	        item.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
    25	            e.preventDefault();
    26	            nav.forEach(function (n) { n.classList.remove('active'); });
    27	            item.classList.add('active');
    28	            loadPage(item.getAttribute('data-page'));
    29	            if (window.innerWidth < 1024) toggleSidebar();
    30	        });
    31	    });
    32	    loadPage('dashboard');
    33	});
