    35	function toggleSidebar() { var s = document.getElementById('sidebar'); var o = document.getElementById('sidebar-overlay'); if (s) s.classList.toggle('-translate-x-full'); if (o) o.classList.toggle('hidden'); }
    36	
    37	function apiFetch(endpoint, options) {
    38	    options = options || {}; options.headers = options.headers || {};
    39	    options.headers['Authorization'] = 'Bearer ' + authToken;
    40	    options.headers['Content-Type'] = 'application/json';
    41	    if (options.body && typeof options.body === 'object') { options.body = JSON.stringify(options.body); }
    42	    return fetch(API_BASE + endpoint, options).then(function (r) {
    43	        if (r.status === 401) { localStorage.clear(); window.location.href = '/dashboard/login.html'; }
    44	        return r.json();
    45	    });
    46	}
    47	
    48	
    49	
    50	async function apiDownload(endpoint, filename) {
    51	    try {
    52	        const res = await fetch(API_BASE + endpoint, {
    53	            headers: {'Authorization': 'Bearer ' + authToken}
    54	        });
    55	        if (!res.ok) throw new Error('Erreur ' + res.status);
    56	        const blob = await res.blob();
    57	        const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
    58	        const a = document.createElement('a');
    59	        a.href = url;
    60	        a.download = filename;
    61	        document.body.appendChild(a);
    62	        a.click();
    63	        a.remove();
    64	        URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
    65	    } catch (e) {
    66	        showAlert('Erreur export : ' + e.message);
    67	    }
    68	}
    69	function showAlert(message) {
    70	    return new Promise(function(resolve) {
    71	        var overlay = document.createElement('div');
    72	        overlay.className = 'fixed inset-0 z-[100] flex items-center justify-center bg-black/50 p-4';
    73	        overlay.innerHTML = '<div class="bg-white rounded-2xl p-6 w-full max-w-sm shadow-2xl"><p class="text-slate-800 text-sm mb-4"></p><button class="w-full py-2.5 bg-blue-600 text-white rounded-xl text-sm font-semibold">OK</button></div>';
    74	        document.body.appendChild(overlay);
    75	        overlay.querySelector('p').textContent = message;
    76	        overlay.querySelector('button').onclick = function(){ overlay.remove(); resolve(); };
    77	    });
    78	}
    79	
    80	
    81	function showPrompt(message, valeurParDefaut) {
    82	    return new Promise(function(resolve) {
    83	        var overlay = document.createElement('div');
    84	        overlay.className = 'fixed inset-0 z-[100] flex items-center justify-center bg-black/50 p-4';
    85	        overlay.innerHTML = '<div class="bg-white rounded-2xl p-6 w-full max-w-sm shadow-2xl"><p class="text-slate-800 text-sm mb-4"></p><input type="text" class="w-full px-4 py-2.5 border border-slate-200 rounded-xl text-sm mb-4" /><div class="flex gap-2"><button data-act="cancel" class="flex-1 py-2.5 bg-slate-200 text-slate-700 rounded-xl text-sm font-semibold">Annuler</button><button data-act="ok" class="flex-1 py-2.5 bg-blue-600 text-white rounded-xl text-sm font-semibold">Valider</button></div></div>';
    86	        document.body.appendChild(overlay);
    87	        overlay.querySelector('p').textContent = message;
    88	        var input = overlay.querySelector('input');
    89	        input.value = valeurParDefaut || '';
    90	        function close(val) { overlay.remove(); resolve(val); }
    91	        overlay.querySelector('[data-act="cancel"]').onclick = function(){ close(null); };
    92	        overlay.querySelector('[data-act="ok"]').onclick = function(){ close(input.value); };
    93	    });
    94	}
    95	function showConfirm(message) {
    96	    return new Promise(function(resolve) {
    97	        var overlay = document.createElement('div');
    98	        overlay.className = 'fixed inset-0 z-[100] flex items-center justify-center bg-black/50 p-4';
    99	        overlay.innerHTML = '<div class="bg-white rounded-2xl p-6 w-full max-w-sm shadow-2xl"><p class="text-slate-800 text-sm mb-4"></p><div class="flex gap-2"><button data-act="cancel" class="flex-1 py-2.5 bg-slate-200 text-slate-700 rounded-xl text-sm font-semibold">Annuler</button><button data-act="ok" class="flex-1 py-2.5 bg-blue-600 text-white rounded-xl text-sm font-semibold">Confirmer</button></div></div>';
   100	        document.body.appendChild(overlay);
   101	        overlay.querySelector('p').textContent = message;
   102	        function close(val) { overlay.remove(); resolve(val); }
   103	        overlay.querySelector('[data-act="cancel"]').onclick = function(){ close(false); };
   104	        overlay.querySelector('[data-act="ok"]').onclick = function(){ close(true); };
   105	    });
   106	}
   107	function confirmLogout() { document.getElementById('logout-modal').classList.remove('hidden'); }
   108	function cancelLogout() { document.getElementById('logout-modal').classList.add('hidden'); }
   109	function logout() { document.getElementById('logout-modal').classList.add('hidden'); fetch(API_BASE + '/auth/logout', { method: 'POST', headers: { 'Authorization': 'Bearer ' + authToken } }).finally(function () { localStorage.clear(); window.location.href = '/dashboard/login.html'; }); }
   110	
   111	function loadPage(page) {
   112	    var c = document.getElementById('page-content');
   113	    c.innerHTML = '<div style="text-align:center;padding:60px;"><div style="width:32px;height:32px;border:3px solid #e2e8f0;border-top-color:#0066ff;border-radius:50%;animation:spin 0.8s linear infinite;margin:0 auto;"></div></div>';
   114	    var funcs = {
   115	        'dashboard': loadDashboard,

===== public/dashboard/chef/js/benin-localisation.js =====
     1	(function () {
     2	    'use strict';
     3	
     4	    const SELECT_IDS = {
     5	        departement: 'deb-departement',
     6	        commune: 'deb-commune',
     7	        arrondissement: 'deb-arrondissement',
     8	        quartier: 'deb-village'
     9	    };
    10	
    11	    const API_URL = '/api/localisations';
    12	
    13	    function getSelect(id) {
    14	        return document.getElementById(id);
    15	    }
    16	
    17	    function resetSelect(id, placeholder, disabled) {
    18	        const select = getSelect(id);
    19	        if (!select) return;
    20	        select.innerHTML = '';
    21	        select.appendChild(new Option(placeholder, ''));
    22	        select.disabled = Boolean(disabled);
    23	    }
    24	
    25	    function setLoading(id, placeholder) {
    26	        const select = getSelect(id);
    27	        if (!select) return;
    28	        select.innerHTML = '';
    29	        select.appendChild(new Option(placeholder, ''));
    30	        select.disabled = true;
    31	    }
    32	
    33	    async function chargerOptions(type, parent, selectId, placeholder) {
    34	        const select = getSelect(selectId);
    35	        if (!select) {
    36	            console.warn('[Localisation] Select introuvable : #' + selectId);
    37	            return;
    38	        }
    39	
    40	        setLoading(selectId, 'Chargement...');
    41	
    42	        const params = new URLSearchParams({ type: type });
    43	        if (parent) params.set('parent', parent);
    44	
    45	        try {
    46	            const response = await fetch(API_URL + '?' + params.toString(), {
    47	                headers: { 'Accept': 'application/json' },
    48	                cache: 'no-store'
    49	            });
    50	
    51	            const contentType = response.headers.get('content-type') || '';
    52	            const data = contentType.includes('application/json')
    53	                ? await response.json()
    54	                : null;
    55	
    56	            if (!response.ok) throw new Error('Erreur HTTP ' + response.status);
    57	            if (!Array.isArray(data)) throw new Error('Reponse non JSON');
    58	
    59	            select.innerHTML = '';
    60	            select.appendChild(new Option(placeholder, ''));
    61	            data.forEach(item => select.appendChild(new Option(item.nom, item.nom)));
    62	            select.disabled = false;
    63	            console.log('[Localisation]', type, ':', data.length, 'element(s) charge(s)');
    64	        } catch (error) {
    65	            console.error('[Localisation] Echec chargement', { type, parent, erreur: error });
    66	            select.innerHTML = '';
    67	            select.appendChild(new Option('Erreur de chargement', ''));
    68	            select.disabled = true;
    69	        }
    70	    }
    71	
    72	    function initSelects(root) {
    73	        const scope = root || document;
    74	        const dep = scope.querySelector('#' + SELECT_IDS.departement);
    75	        const com = scope.querySelector('#' + SELECT_IDS.commune);
    76	        const arr = scope.querySelector('#' + SELECT_IDS.arrondissement);
    77	        const quartier = scope.querySelector('#' + SELECT_IDS.quartier);
    78	
    79	        if (!dep) {
    80	            console.warn('[Localisation] #deb-departement introuvable');
    81	            return false;
    82	        }
    83	
    84	        if (dep.dataset.localisationReady === '1') return true;
    85	        dep.dataset.localisationReady = '1';
    86	
    87	        if (com) com.disabled = true;
    88	        if (arr) arr.disabled = true;
    89	        if (quartier) quartier.disabled = true;
    90	
    91	        chargerOptions('departement', '', SELECT_IDS.departement, '-- Departement --');
    92	
    93	        dep.addEventListener('change', function () {
    94	            resetSelect(SELECT_IDS.commune, '-- Commune --', true);
    95	            resetSelect(SELECT_IDS.arrondissement, '-- Arrondissement --', true);
    96	            resetSelect(SELECT_IDS.quartier, '-- Quartier --', true);
    97	            if (this.value) chargerOptions('commune', this.value, SELECT_IDS.commune, '-- Commune --');
    98	        });
    99	
   100	        if (com) com.addEventListener('change', function () {
   101	            resetSelect(SELECT_IDS.arrondissement, '-- Arrondissement --', true);
   102	            resetSelect(SELECT_IDS.quartier, '-- Quartier --', true);
   103	            if (this.value) chargerOptions('arrondissement', this.value, SELECT_IDS.arrondissement, '-- Arrondissement --');
   104	        });
   105	
   106	        if (arr) arr.addEventListener('change', function () {
   107	            resetSelect(SELECT_IDS.quartier, '-- Quartier --', true);
   108	            if (this.value) chargerOptions('quartier', this.value, SELECT_IDS.quartier, '-- Quartier --');
   109	        });
   110	
   111	        return true;
   112	    }
   113	
   114	    function initWhenAvailable() {
   115	        if (initSelects()) return;
   116	        const observer = new MutationObserver(function () {
   117	            if (initSelects()) observer.disconnect();
   118	        });
   119	        observer.observe(document.body, { childList: true, subtree: true });
   120	    }
   121	
   122	    window.initLocalisationBenin = initSelects;
   123	window.initSelects = initSelects;
   124	
   125	    if (document.readyState === 'loading') {
   126	        document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', initWhenAvailable);
   127	    } else {
   128	        initWhenAvailable();
   129	    }
   130	})();

     1	// === PAGE CAISSE AVEC CALENDRIER ET REMISES ===

     3	let currentCaisseDate = new Date();

     6	function loadCaisse(c) {
     7	    renderCaisseCalendrier(c);
     8	    loadRemisesEnAttente();

    10	
    11	function renderCaisseCalendrier(c) {
    12	    const selector = `
    13	        <div class="flex gap-2 overflow-x-auto pb-2">
    14	            ${['jour','semaine','mois','annee'].map(p => `
    15	                <button class="chip ${p===currentCaissePeriod?'chip--active':''}" onclick="setCaissePeriod('${p}')">
    16	                    ${p.charAt(0).toUpperCase() + p.slice(1)}
    17	                </button>`).join('')}
    18	        </div>
    19	    `;
    20	
    21	    const nav = `
    22	        <div class="flex items-center justify-between mb-3">
    23	            <button class="text-btn" onclick="changeCaisseMonth(-1)">◀</button>
    24	            <h4 class="font-semibold">${new Intl.DateTimeFormat('fr-FR', {month:'long', year:'numeric'}).format(currentCaisseDate)}</h4>
    25	            <button class="text-btn" onclick="changeCaisseMonth(1)">▶</button>
    26	        </div>
    27	    `;
    28	
    29	    c.innerHTML = `<div class="fade-in space-y-6">
    30	        <div class="flex items-center justify-between">
    31	            <h3 class="text-xl font-bold">Caisse</h3>
    32	            <div class="flex gap-2">
    33	                <button onclick="showDecaissementModal()" class="bg-red-600 text-white px-4 py-2 rounded-lg text-sm font-semibold hover:bg-red-700">- Dépense</button>
    34	                <button onclick="showClotureModal()" class="bg-purple-600 text-white px-4 py-2 rounded-lg text-sm font-semibold hover:bg-purple-700">Clôturer</button>
    35	                <button onclick="exportCaissePeriode()" class="bg-slate-600 text-white px-4 py-2 rounded-lg text-sm font-semibold hover:bg-slate-700">Export CSV</button>
    36	            </div>
    37	        </div>
    38	        ${selector}
    39	        <div class="panel p-4">
    40	            ${nav}
    41	            <div id="caisse-calendrier" class="grid grid-cols-7 gap-1"></div>
    42	        </div>
    43	        <div id="caisse-kpis" class="grid grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-5 gap-3"></div>
    44	        <div id="remises-attente" class="panel p-4"></div>
    45	        <div class="panel">
    46	            <h4 class="font-semibold p-4 pb-0">Mouvements</h4>
    47	            <div class="table-wrapper" id="caisse-mouvements">
    48	                <p class="text-center text-muted py-4">Sélectionnez un jour</p>
    49	            </div>
    50	        </div>
    51	    </div>`;
    52	
    53	    drawCaisseMonth();
    54	    loadCaisseKPIs();
    55	}
    56	
    57	function setCaissePeriod(period) {
    58	    currentCaissePeriod = period;
    59	    document.querySelectorAll('.chip').forEach(ch => ch.classList.remove('chip--active'));
    60	    event.target.classList.add('chip--active');
    61	    renderCaisseCalendrier(document.getElementById('page-content'));
    62	    loadRemisesEnAttente();
    63	}
    64	
    65	function changeCaisseMonth(delta) {
    66	    currentCaisseDate.setMonth(currentCaisseDate.getMonth() + delta);
    67	    renderCaisseCalendrier(document.getElementById('page-content'));
    68	}
    69	
    70	function drawCaisseMonth() {
    71	    const year = currentCaisseDate.getFullYear();
    72	    const month = currentCaisseDate.getMonth();
    73	    const firstDay = new Date(year, month, 1);
    74	    const lastDay = new Date(year, month + 1, 0);
    75	    const startOffset = (firstDay.getDay() + 6) % 7;
    76	    const daysInMonth = lastDay.getDate();
    77	    const today = new Date();
    78	
    79	    const container = document.getElementById('caisse-calendrier');
    80	    container.innerHTML = '';
    81	
    82	    for (let i = 0; i < startOffset; i++) {
    83	        container.innerHTML += '<div></div>';
    84	    }
    85	
    86	    for (let d = 1; d <= daysInMonth; d++) {
    87	        const dateStr = `${year}-${String(month+1).padStart(2,'0')}-${String(d).padStart(2,'0')}`;
    88	        const isToday = d === today.getDate() && month === today.getMonth() && year === today.getFullYear();
    89	        const dayBtn = document.createElement('button');
    90	        dayBtn.className = `p-2 rounded-lg text-sm border ${isToday ? 'border-blue-500' : 'border-slate-200'} hover:bg-slate-50`;
    91	        dayBtn.innerHTML = `<div class="font-medium">${d}</div><div id="solde-${dateStr}" class="text-xs mt-1">...</div><div id="pastille-${dateStr}" class="mt-1"><span class="w-2 h-2 inline-block rounded-full bg-gray-300"></span></div>`;
    92	        dayBtn.onclick = () => selectCaisseDay(dateStr);
    93	        container.appendChild(dayBtn);
    94	    }
    95	
    96	    loadCaissePeriode(year, month);
    97	}
    98	
    99	async function loadCaissePeriode(year, month) {
   100	    const debut = `${year}-${String(month+1).padStart(2,'0')}-01`;
   101	    const fin = new Date(year, month+1, 0).toISOString().split('T')[0];
   102	
   103	    try {
   104	        const res = await apiFetch(`/caisse/periode?debut=${debut}&fin=${fin}`);
   105	        if (!Array.isArray(res)) return;
   106	
   107	        res.forEach(item => {
   108	            const soldeEl = document.getElementById(`solde-${item.jour}`);
   109	            const pastilleEl = document.getElementById(`pastille-${item.jour}`);
   110	
   111	            const encaissements = Number(item.encaissements || 0);
   112	            const decaissements = Number(item.decaissements || 0);
   113	            const epargnes = Number(item.epargnes || 0);
   114	            const penalites = Number(item.penalites || 0);
   115	            const ecart = Number(item.ecart || 0);
   116	            const nbMouvements = Number(item.nb_mouvements || 0);
   117	            const solde = encaissements + epargnes + penalites - decaissements;
   118	
   119	            if (soldeEl) {
   120	                soldeEl.textContent = nbMouvements > 0
   121	                    ? new Intl.NumberFormat('fr-FR', { maximumFractionDigits: 0 }).format(solde) + ' F'
   122	                    : '';
   123	            }
   124	
   125	            if (!pastilleEl) return;
   126	
   127	            let couleur = 'bg-gray-300';
   128	            let titre = 'Aucun mouvement';
   129	
   130	            if (item.non_cloture) {
   131	                couleur = 'bg-red-500';
   132	                titre = 'Caisse non clôturée';
   133	            } else if (Math.abs(ecart) > 0.009) {
   134	                couleur = 'bg-orange-500';
   135	                titre = 'Écart de caisse : ' + ecart;
   136	            } else if (nbMouvements > 0) {
   137	                couleur = 'bg-green-500';
   138	                titre = nbMouvements + ' mouvement(s)';
   139	            }
   140	
   141	            pastilleEl.innerHTML = `<span title="${titre}" class="w-2 h-2 inline-block rounded-full ${couleur}"></span>`;
   142	        });
   143	    } catch (e) {
   144	        console.error('loadCaissePeriode', e);
   145	    }
   146	}
   147	
   148	function selectCaisseDay(dateStr) {
   149	    currentCaissePeriod = 'jour';
   150	    currentCaisseDate = new Date(dateStr + 'T00:00:00');
   151	    document.querySelectorAll('.chip').forEach(ch => ch.classList.remove('chip--active'));
   152	    const jourChip = [...document.querySelectorAll('.chip')].find(ch => ch.textContent.trim() === 'Jour');
   153	    if (jourChip) jourChip.classList.add('chip--active');
   154	
   155	    loadCaisseKPIs(dateStr);
   156	    loadCaisseMouvements(dateStr);
   157	}

   159	async function loadCaisseKPIs(date = null) {
   160	    const debut = date || new Date(currentCaisseDate.getFullYear(), currentCaisseDate.getMonth(), 1).toISOString().split('T')[0];
   161	    const fin = date || new Date(currentCaisseDate.getFullYear(), currentCaisseDate.getMonth()+1, 0).toISOString().split('T')[0];
   162	
   163	    try {
   164	        const data = await apiFetch(`/caisse/consolidee?debut=${debut}&fin=${fin}`);
   165	        const f = v => new Intl.NumberFormat('fr-FR',{style:'currency',currency:'XOF',maximumFractionDigits:0}).format(v||0);
   166	
   167	        document.getElementById('caisse-kpis').innerHTML = `
   168	            <div class="kpi-card" style="border-left:3px solid #10b981"><div class="kpi-label">Encaissements</div><div class="kpi-value">${f(data.encaissements)}</div></div>
   169	            <div class="kpi-card" style="border-left:3px solid #ef4444"><div class="kpi-label">Dépenses</div><div class="kpi-value">${f(data.decaissements)}</div></div>
   170	            <div class="kpi-card" style="border-left:3px solid #3b82f6"><div class="kpi-label">Épargnes</div><div class="kpi-value">${f(data.epargnes)}</div></div>
   171	            <div class="kpi-card" style="border-left:3px solid #f59e0b"><div class="kpi-label">Pénalités</div><div class="kpi-value">${f(data.penalites)}</div></div>
   172	            <div class="kpi-card" style="border-left:3px solid #8b5cf6"><div class="kpi-label">Solde</div><div class="kpi-value">${f(data.solde)}</div></div>
   173	        `;
   174	    } catch(e) {
   175	        console.warn('Erreur KPI caisse', e);
   176	    }
   177	}
   178	
   179	async function loadCaisseMouvements(date) {
   180	    try {
   181	        const caisseData = await apiFetch('/caisse');
   182	        const mouvements = (caisseData.caisse && caisseData.caisse.mouvements) || [];
   183	        const f = v => new Intl.NumberFormat('fr-FR',{style:'currency',currency:'XOF',maximumFractionDigits:0}).format(v||0);
   184

   186	            ? '<p class="text-center text-muted py-4">Aucun mouvement</p>'

   188	                ${mouvements.map(m => `<tr><td class="text-xs">${new Date(m.date_mouvement).toLocaleTimeString('fr-FR')}</td><td><span class="badge ${m.type==='encaissement'?'badge-success':'badge-danger'}">${m.type}</span></td><td class="font-semibold">${f(m.montant)}</td><td>${m.mode_paiement||''}</td><td>${m.motif||''}</td></tr>`).join('')}
   189	            </tbody></table>`;
   190	    } catch(e) {
   191	        console.warn('Erreur mouvements', e);
   192	    }
   193	}

   195	async function loadRemisesEnAttente() {
   196	    try {
   197	        const remises = await apiFetch('/caisse/remises-en-attente');
   198	        const f = v => new Intl.NumberFormat('fr-FR',{style:'currency',currency:'XOF',maximumFractionDigits:0}).format(v||0);
   199	        const container = document.getElementById('remises-attente');
   200	        if (!container) return;
   201	
   202	        if (!Array.isArray(remises) || remises.length === 0) {
   203	            container.innerHTML = '<p class="text-center text-muted py-2">Aucune remise en attente</p>';
   204	            return;
   205	        }
   206	
   207	        container.innerHTML = `<h4 class="font-semibold mb-2">Remises en attente de validation</h4>
   208	        <div class="table-wrapper"><table class="min-w-full">
   209	            <thead><tr><th>Agent</th><th>Montant déclaré</th><th>Référence</th><th>Actions</th></tr></thead>
   210	            <tbody>${remises.map(r => `<tr>
   211	                <td>${r.user ? r.user.nom + ' ' + r.user.prenom : '-'}</td>
   212	                <td>${f(r.montant_declare || r.montant)}</td>
   213	                <td class="text-xs">${r.reference}</td>
   214	                <td>
   215	                    <button class="small-btn small-btn--primary" onclick="validerRemiseAction('${r.reference}')">Valider</button>
   216	                    <button class="small-btn small-btn--secondary" onclick="rejeterRemiseAction('${r.reference}')">Rejeter</button>
   217	                </td></tr>`).join('')}
   218	            </tbody></table></div>`;
   219	    } catch(e) {
   220	        console.warn('Erreur remises attente', e);
   221	    }
   222	}
   223	
   224	async function validerRemiseAction(reference) {
   225	    if (!(await showConfirm('Valider cette remise ?'))) return;
   226	    apiFetch(`/caisse/remise/${reference}/valider`, {method:'POST', body: JSON.stringify({statut:'valide'})}).then(() => {
   227	        showAlert('Remise validée.');
   228	        loadPage('caisse');
   229	    }).catch(e => showAlert('Erreur : ' + e.message));
   230	}
   231	
   232	async function rejeterRemiseAction(reference) {
   233	    const motif = await showPrompt('Motif du rejet :');
   234	    if (!motif) return;
   235	    apiFetch(`/caisse/remise/${reference}/valider`, {method:'POST', body: JSON.stringify({statut:'rejete', motif_rejet:motif})}).then(() => {
   236	        showAlert('Remise rejetée.');
   237	        loadPage('caisse');
   238	    }).catch(e => showAlert('Erreur : ' + e.message));
   239	}

   243	function submitDecaissement(e){e.preventDefault();apiFetch('/caisse/decaisser',{method:'POST',body:JSON.stringify({montant:parseFloat(document.getElementById('caisse-decaissement-montant').value),motif:document.getElementById('caisse-decaissement-motif').value})}).then(function(){closeDecaissementModal();loadPage('caisse');});}
   244	function showClotureModal(){document.getElementById('caisse-cloture-modal').classList.remove('hidden');}
   245	function closeClotureModal(){document.getElementById('caisse-cloture-modal').classList.add('hidden');}
   246	async function submitCloture(e){e.preventDefault();var solde = await showPrompt('Solde réel compté (FCFA) :', '0'); if(!solde) return;apiFetch('/caisse/cloturer',{method:'POST',body:JSON.stringify({solde_reel:parseFloat(solde),notes:document.getElementById('cloture-notes').value})}).then(function(r){closeClotureModal();showAlert('Caisse clôturée. Écart : '+new Intl.NumberFormat('fr-FR',{style:'currency',currency:'XOF',maximumFractionDigits:0}).format(r.ecart));loadPage('caisse');});}

   248	function exportCaissePeriode() {
   249	    const y = currentCaisseDate.getFullYear();
   250	    const m = currentCaisseDate.getMonth();
   251	    const debut = `${y}-${String(m+1).padStart(2,'0')}-01`;
   252	    const fin = new Date(y, m+1, 0).toISOString().split('T')[0];
   253	    if (typeof apiDownload === 'function') {
   254	        apiDownload(`/caisse/export-periode?debut=${debut}&fin=${fin}`, `caisse_${debut}_au_${fin}.csv`);
   255	    } else {
   256	        window.location.href = `/api/caisse/export-periode?debut=${debut}&fin=${fin}`;
   257	    }
   258	}

===== public/dashboard/chef/js/comptabilite-functions.js =====
     1	(function () {
     2	    'use strict';
     3	
     4	    function formaterMontant(valeur) {
     5	        var nombre = Number(valeur || 0);
     6	        return new Intl.NumberFormat('fr-FR', {
     7	            style: 'currency',
     8	            currency: 'XOF',
     9	            maximumFractionDigits: 0
    10	        }).format(nombre);
    11	    }
    12	
    13	    function echapper(valeur) {
    14	        var element = document.createElement('div');
    15	        element.textContent = valeur == null ? '' : String(valeur);
    16	        return element.innerHTML;
    17	    }
    18	
    19	    function chargerJournal(page) {
    20	        page = page || 1;
    21	        return apiFetch('/comptabilite/journal?page=' + page + '&par_page=50')
    22	            .then(function (reponse) {
    23	                return reponse || { donnees: [], page: 1, total_pages: 1 };
    24	            });
    25	    }
    26	
    27	    function chargerBalance() {
    28	        return apiFetch('/comptabilite/balance');
    29	    }
    30	
    31	    function chargerGrandLivre() {
    32	        return apiFetch('/comptabilite/grand-livre');
    33	    }
    34	
    35	    function chargerEtatsFinanciers() {
    36	        return apiFetch('/comptabilite/etats-financiers');
    37	    }
    38	
    39	    function afficherErreur(conteneur, erreur) {
    40	        console.error('Erreur comptabilité:', erreur);
    41	        conteneur.innerHTML =
    42	            '<div class="panel text-center py-12">' +
    43	                '<p class="text-red-600 font-semibold">Erreur de chargement de la comptabilité.</p>' +
    44	                '<p class="text-sm text-muted mt-2">' +
    45	                    echapper(erreur && erreur.message ? erreur.message : 'Erreur inconnue') +
    46	                '</p>' +
    47	            '</div>';
    48	    }
    49	
    50	    function construireLignesGrandLivre(comptes) {
    51	        if (!Array.isArray(comptes) || comptes.length === 0) {
    52	            return '<tr><td colspan="5" class="text-center text-muted py-4">Aucune écriture</td></tr>';
    53	        }
    54	
    55	        return comptes.map(function (compte) {
    56	            var solde = Number(compte.solde || 0);
    57	            var classeSolde = solde >= 0 ? 'text-green-600' : 'text-red-600';
    58	
    59	            return '<tr>' +
    60	                '<td class="font-mono text-xs">' + echapper(compte.compte) + '</td>' +
    61	                '<td class="font-medium">' + echapper(compte.libelle) + '</td>' +
    62	                '<td class="text-right">' + formaterMontant(compte.debit) + '</td>' +
    63	                '<td class="text-right">' + formaterMontant(compte.credit) + '</td>' +
    64	                '<td class="text-right font-semibold ' + classeSolde + '">' + formaterMontant(solde) + '</td>' +
    65	            '</tr>';
    66	        }).join('');
    67	    }
    68	
    69	    function construireLignesJournal(journal) {
    70	        if (!Array.isArray(journal) || journal.length === 0) {
    71	            return '<tr><td colspan="7" class="text-center text-muted py-4">Aucune écriture comptable</td></tr>';
    72	        }
    73	
    74	        return journal.map(function (ecriture) {
    75	            return '<tr>' +
    76	                '<td>' + echapper(ecriture.date_ecriture) + '</td>' +
    77	                '<td>' + echapper(ecriture.numero_ecriture) + '</td>' +
    78	                '<td>' + echapper(ecriture.compte_debit) + '</td>' +
    79	                '<td>' + echapper(ecriture.compte_credit) + '</td>' +
    80	                '<td>' + echapper(ecriture.libelle) + '</td>' +
    81	                '<td class="text-right">' + formaterMontant(ecriture.montant) + '</td>' +
    82	                '<td>' + echapper(ecriture.reference) + '</td>' +
    83	            '</tr>';
    84	        }).join('');
    85	    }
    86	
    87	    function afficherPagination(journal) {
    88	        var page = Number(journal.page || 1);
    89	        var totalPages = Math.max(1, Number(journal.total_pages || 1));
    90	
    91	        return '<div class="flex gap-2 items-center">' +
    92	            '<button id="journal-page-precedente" class="px-3 py-1 border rounded-lg text-sm" ' + (page <= 1 ? 'disabled' : '') + '>Precedente</button>' +
    93	            '<span class="text-sm">Page ' + page + ' / ' + totalPages + '</span>' +
    94	            '<button id="journal-page-suivante" class="px-3 py-1 border rounded-lg text-sm" ' + (page >= totalPages ? 'disabled' : '') + '>Suivante</button>' +
    95	        '</div>';
    96	    }
    97	
    98	    function loadComptabilite(conteneur) {
    99	        var pageJournal = 1;
   100	
   101	        function chargerEtAfficher() {
   102	            Promise.all([
   103	                chargerBalance(),
   104	                chargerGrandLivre(),
   105	                chargerJournal(pageJournal),
   106	                chargerEtatsFinanciers()
   107	            ])
   108	            .then(function (resultats) {
   109	                var balance = resultats[0] || {};
   110	                var grandLivre = Array.isArray(resultats[1]) ? resultats[1] : [];
   111	                var journal = resultats[2] || {};
   112	                var etats = resultats[3] || {};
   113	
   114	                var totalDebit = Number(balance.total_debit || 0);
   115	                var totalCredit = Number(balance.total_credit || 0);
   116	                var ecart = Number(balance.ecart || (totalDebit - totalCredit));
   117	
   118	                var balanceEstEquilibree =
   119	                    balance.equilibree === true ||
   120	                    balance.equilibre === true ||
   121	                    Math.abs(ecart) < 0.01;
   122	
   123	                var compteResultat = etats.compte_resultat || {};
   124	                var bilan = etats.bilan_synthetique || {};
   125	
   126	                conteneur.innerHTML =
   127	                    '<div class="fade-in space-y-6">' +
   128	                        '<div class="flex items-center justify-between">' +
   129	                            '<h3 class="text-xl font-bold">Comptabilite</h3>' +
   130	                            '<div class="flex gap-2">' +
   131	                                '<button onclick="exportJournal()" class="bg-accent text-white px-3 py-2 rounded-lg text-xs font-semibold">Journal CSV</button>' +
   132	                                '<button onclick="exportGrandLivre()" class="bg-blue-600 text-white px-3 py-2 rounded-lg text-xs font-semibold">Grand Livre CSV</button>' +
   133	                                '<button onclick="exportEtatsFinanciers()" class="bg-emerald-600 text-white px-3 py-2 rounded-lg text-xs font-semibold">Etats Financiers CSV</button>' +
   134	                            '</div>' +
   135	                        '</div>' +
   136	
   137	                        '<div class="kpi-grid">' +
   138	                            '<div class="kpi-card" style="border-left:3px solid #0066ff">' +
   139	                                '<div class="kpi-label">Total Debit</div>' +
   140	                                '<div class="kpi-value">' + formaterMontant(totalDebit) + '</div>' +
   141	                            '</div>' +
   142	
   143	                            '<div class="kpi-card" style="border-left:3px solid #00c853">' +
   144	                                '<div class="kpi-label">Total Credit</div>' +
   145	                                '<div class="kpi-value">' + formaterMontant(totalCredit) + '</div>' +
   146	                            '</div>' +
   147	
   148	                            '<div class="kpi-card" style="border-left:3px solid ' + (balanceEstEquilibree ? '#00c853' : '#ef4444') + '">' +
   149	                                '<div class="kpi-label">Balance</div>' +
   150	                                '<div class="kpi-value">' + (balanceEstEquilibree ? 'Equilibree' : 'Desequilibree') + '</div>' +
   151	                                '<div class="text-xs text-muted mt-1">Ecart : ' + formaterMontant(ecart) + '</div>' +
   152	                            '</div>' +
   153	                        '</div>' +
   154	
   155	                        '<div class="panel">' +
   156	                            '<h3>Grand Livre</h3>' +
   157	                            '<div class="table-wrapper">' +
   158	                                '<table>' +
   159	                                    '<thead><tr><th>Compte</th><th>Libelle</th><th class="text-right">Debit</th><th class="text-right">Credit</th><th class="text-right">Solde</th></tr></thead>' +
   160	                                    '<tbody>' + construireLignesGrandLivre(balance.comptes && balance.comptes.length ? balance.comptes : grandLivre) + '</tbody>' +
   161	                                '</table>' +
   162	                            '</div>' +
   163	                        '</div>' +
   164	
   165	                        '<div class="panel">' +
   166	                            '<div class="flex items-center justify-between mb-3">' +
   167	                                '<h3>Journal Comptable</h3>' +
   168	                                '<div id="journal-pagination">' + afficherPagination(journal) + '</div>' +
   169	                            '</div>' +
   170	                            '<div class="table-wrapper" style="max-height:450px;overflow:auto">' +
   171	                                '<table>' +
   172	                                    '<thead><tr><th>Date</th><th>N</th><th>Debit</th><th>Credit</th><th>Libelle</th><th class="text-right">Montant</th><th>Reference</th></tr></thead>' +
   173	                                    '<tbody id="journal-table">' + construireLignesJournal(journal.donnees || []) + '</tbody>' +
   174	                                '</table>' +
   175	                            '</div>' +
   176	                        '</div>' +
   177	
   178	                        '<div class="panel">' +
   179	                            '<h3>Etats Financiers</h3>' +
   180	                            '<div class="grid grid-cols-2 gap-4">' +
   181	                                '<div><p class="text-sm text-muted">Chiffre d affaires</p><p class="text-lg font-bold">' + formaterMontant(compteResultat.chiffre_affaires) + '</p></div>' +
   182	                                '<div><p class="text-sm text-muted">Encaissements</p><p class="text-lg font-bold">' + formaterMontant(compteResultat.encaissements) + '</p></div>' +
   183	                                '<div><p class="text-sm text-muted">Charges</p><p class="text-lg font-bold">' + formaterMontant(compteResultat.charges) + '</p></div>' +
   184	                                '<div><p class="text-sm text-muted">Benefice brut</p><p class="text-lg font-bold">' + formaterMontant(compteResultat.benefice_brut) + '</p></div>' +
   185	                                '<div><p class="text-sm text-muted">Valeur stock</p><p class="text-lg font-bold">' + formaterMontant(compteResultat.valeur_stock) + '</p></div>' +
   186	                                '<div><p class="text-sm text-muted">Actif total</p><p class="text-lg font-bold">' + formaterMontant(bilan.actif_total) + '</p></div>' +
   187	                                '<div><p class="text-sm text-muted">Passif total</p><p class="text-lg font-bold">' + formaterMontant(bilan.passif_total) + '</p></div>' +
   188	                            '</div>' +
   189	                        '</div>' +
   190	                    '</div>';
   191	
   192	                var boutonPrecedent = document.getElementById('journal-page-precedente');
   193	                var boutonSuivant = document.getElementById('journal-page-suivante');
   194	
   195	                if (boutonPrecedent) {
   196	                    boutonPrecedent.onclick = function () {
   197	                        if (pageJournal > 1) {
   198	                            pageJournal--;
   199	                            chargerEtAfficher();
   200	                        }
   201	                    };
   202	                }
   203	
   204	                if (boutonSuivant) {
   205	                    boutonSuivant.onclick = function () {
   206	                        var totalPages = Number(journal.total_pages || 1);
   207	                        if (pageJournal < totalPages) {
   208	                            pageJournal++;
   209	                            chargerEtAfficher();
   210	                        }
   211	                    };
   212	                }
   213	            })
   214	            .catch(function (erreur) {
   215	                afficherErreur(conteneur, erreur);
   216	            });
   217	        }
   218	
   219	        chargerEtAfficher();
   220	    }
   221	
   222	    window.loadComptabilite = loadComptabilite;
   223	
   224	    window.exportJournal = function () {
   225	        var token = localStorage.getItem('sourougnon_token') || '';
   226	        window.open('/api/comptabilite/export?token=' + encodeURIComponent(token), '_blank');
   227	    };
   228	
   229	    window.exportGrandLivre = function () {
   230	        var token = localStorage.getItem('sourougnon_token') || '';
   231	        window.open('/api/comptabilite/export-grand-livre?token=' + encodeURIComponent(token), '_blank');
   232	    };
   233	
   234	    window.exportEtatsFinanciers = function () {
   235	        var token = localStorage.getItem('sourougnon_token') || '';
   236	        window.open('/api/comptabilite/export-etats-financiers?token=' + encodeURIComponent(token), '_blank');
   237	    };

     1	function loadComptant(c){apiFetch('/ventes?type_vente=comptant').then(function(v){function f(x){return new Intl.NumberFormat('fr-FR',{style:'currency',currency:'XOF',maximumFractionDigits:0}).format(x);}c.innerHTML='<div class="fade-in space-y-6"><div class="flex items-center justify-between"><h3 class="text-xl font-bold">Ventes au Comptant</h3></div><div class="panel"><div class="table-wrapper"><table><thead><tr><th>Date</th><th>Client</th><th>Telephone</th><th>Produit</th><th>Montant</th><th>Mode</th></tr></thead><tbody>'+(v.length>0?v.map(function(x){var n=x.debiteur?x.debiteur.nom+' '+(x.debiteur.prenom||''):'Client';return '<tr><td>'+x.date_debut+'</td><td class="font-medium">'+n+'</td><td>'+(x.debiteur?x.debiteur.telephone:'--')+'</td><td>'+(x.produit?x.produit.nom:'--')+'</td><td class="font-semibold">'+f(x.montant_total)+'</td><td>Especes</td></tr>';}).join(''):'<tr><td colspan="6" class="text-center text-muted py-4">Aucune vente comptant</td></tr>')+'</tbody></table></div></div></div>';});}

     1	var debStep1Data = {};

     3	function showAddDebiteurModal(){

    13	function closeDebModals(){
    14	    document.getElementById('deb-step1-modal').classList.add('hidden');
    15	    document.getElementById('deb-step2-modal').classList.add('hidden');

    18	function debStep1(e){
    19	    e.preventDefault();
    20	    debStep1Data = {
    21	        nom: document.getElementById('deb-nom').value,
    22	        prenom: document.getElementById('deb-prenom').value,
    23	        telephone: document.getElementById('deb-telephone').value,
    24	        quartier: typeof getLocalisationComplete === 'function' ? getLocalisationComplete() : '',
    25	        adresse: document.getElementById('deb-adresse').value
    26	    };
    27	    document.getElementById('deb-step1-modal').classList.add('hidden');
    28	    document.getElementById('deb-step2-modal').classList.remove('hidden');
    29	
    30	    apiFetch('/agents').then(function(a){
    31	        var agentSelect = document.getElementById('deb-agent');
    32	        if (agentSelect) {
    33	            agentSelect.innerHTML = '<option value="">Agent assigné...</option>' +
    34	                a.filter(function(x){return x.actif;}).map(function(x){
    35	                    return '<option value="'+x.uuid+'">'+x.nom+' '+(x.prenom||'')+'</option>';
    36	                }).join('');
    37	        }
    38	
    39	        if (window.debiteurEnModification && window.debiteurDataData) {
    40	            var d = window.debiteurDataData;
    41	            var fAct = document.getElementById('deb-activite'); if(fAct) fAct.value = d.activite || '';
    42	            var fRefNom = document.getElementById('deb-ref-nom'); if(fRefNom) fRefNom.value = d.personne_reference_nom || '';
    43	            var fRefTel = document.getElementById('deb-ref-tel'); if(fRefTel) fRefTel.value = d.personne_reference_tel || '';
    44	            var fLim = document.getElementById('deb-limite'); if(fLim) fLim.value = d.credits_autorises || 1;
    45	            if (d.agent_id && agentSelect) agentSelect.value = d.agent_id;
    46	        }
    47	    });
    48	}

    50	function debStepBack(){
    51	    document.getElementById('deb-step2-modal').classList.add('hidden');
    52	    document.getElementById('deb-step1-modal').classList.remove('hidden');
    53	}

    55	function debStep2(e){
    56	    e.preventDefault();
    57	    var data = Object.assign(debStep1Data, {
    58	        agent_id: document.getElementById('deb-agent').value,
    59	        activite: document.getElementById('deb-activite').value,
    60	        personne_reference_nom: document.getElementById('deb-ref-nom').value,
    61	        personne_reference_tel: document.getElementById('deb-ref-tel').value,
    62	        credits_autorises: parseInt(document.getElementById('deb-limite').value) || 1
    63	    });
    64	
    65	    var isEdit = !!window.debiteurEnModification;
    66	    var url = isEdit ? '/debiteurs/' + window.debiteurEnModification : '/debiteurs';
    67	    var method = isEdit ? 'PUT' : 'POST';
    68	
    69	    apiFetch(url, {method: method, body: JSON.stringify(data)}).then(function(r){
    70	        if(r.success) {
    71	            closeDebModals();
    72	            window.debiteurEnModification = null;
    73	            window.debiteurDataData = null;
    74	            showAlert(isEdit ? 'Débiteur modifié avec succès.' : 'Débiteur créé avec le code ' + (r.code_client || ''));
    75	            loadPage('debiteurs');
    76	        } else {
    77	            showAlert('Erreur: ' + (r.error || 'inconnue'));
    78	        }
    79	    });
    80	}

    82	function loadDebiteurs(c){
    83	    apiFetch('/debiteurs').then(function(d){
    84	        c.innerHTML='<div class="fade-in space-y-6">'+
    85	            '<div class="flex items-center justify-between"><h3 class="text-xl font-bold">Debiteurs</h3>'+
    86	            '<button onclick="showAddDebiteurModal()" class="bg-accent text-white px-4 py-2 rounded-lg text-sm font-semibold hover:bg-blue-700">+ Nouveau Debiteur</button></div>'+
    87	            '<div class="panel"><div class="table-wrapper"><table><thead><tr><th>Code</th><th>Nom</th><th>Telephone</th><th>Quartier</th><th>Agent</th><th>Score</th><th>Actions</th></tr></thead><tbody>'+
    88	            (d.length>0?d.map(function(x){
    89	                var score = x.score_solvabilite !== undefined && x.score_solvabilite !== null ? x.score_solvabilite : 50;
    90	                var scoreColor = score >= 80 ? '#00c853' : score >= 60 ? '#0066ff' : score >= 40 ? '#f59e0b' : '#ef4444';
    91	                return '<tr>'+
    92	                    '<td class="font-mono text-xs">'+(x.code_client||'--')+'</td>'+
    93	                    '<td class="font-medium">'+x.nom+' '+(x.prenom||'')+'</td>'+
    94	                    '<td>'+(x.telephone||'--')+'</td>'+
    95	                    '<td>'+(x.quartier||'--')+'</td>'+
    96	                    '<td>'+(x.agent?x.agent.nom:'--')+'</td>'+
    97	                    '<td><span class="badge" style="background:'+scoreColor+'20;color:'+scoreColor+';font-weight:bold">'+score+'%</span></td>'+
    98	                    '<td><button onclick="voirDebiteur(\''+x.uuid+'\')" class="text-accent text-xs hover:underline font-semibold">Details</button></td>'+
    99	                '</tr>';
   100	            }).join(''):'<tr><td colspan="7" class="text-center text-muted py-4">Aucun debiteur</td></tr>')+
   101	            '</tbody></table></div></div></div>';
   102	    });
   103	}

   105	function voirDebiteur(uuid){
   106	    var ancienModal = document.getElementById('detail-modal');
   107	    if (ancienModal) ancienModal.remove();
   108	
   109	    Promise.all([
   110	        apiFetch('/debiteurs/'+uuid),
   111	        apiFetch('/debiteurs/'+uuid+'/score').catch(function(){ return {score:50, label:'Neutre', details:{}}; }),
   112	        apiFetch('/debiteurs/'+uuid+'/historique').catch(function(){ return {ventes:[], paiements:[], debiteur:{}}; })
   113	    ]).then(function(resultats){
   114	        var d = resultats[0];
   115	        var scoreData = resultats[1] || {};
   116	        var hist = resultats[2] || {};
   117	
   118	        var score = scoreData.score !== undefined ? scoreData.score : (d.score_solvabilite || 50);
   119	        var label = scoreData.label || 'Neutre';
   120	        var details = scoreData.details || {};
   121	        var scoreColor = score >= 80 ? '#00c853' : score >= 60 ? '#0066ff' : score >= 40 ? '#f59e0b' : '#ef4444';
   122	        var strokeDash = (2 * Math.PI * 40 * score / 100).toFixed(2);
   123	
   124	        var refNom = d.personne_reference_nom || d.reference_nom || d.pers_ref_nom || '--';
   125	        var refTel = d.personne_reference_tel || d.reference_tel || d.pers_ref_tel || '--';
   126	        var creditsAuth = d.credits_autorises !== undefined ? d.credits_autorises : (d.limite_credit || 1);
   127	
   128	        var f = function(v){ return new Intl.NumberFormat('fr-FR',{style:'currency',currency:'XOF',maximumFractionDigits:0}).format(v); };
   129	
   130	        var soldeTotal = (hist.debiteur && hist.debiteur.solde_total !== undefined && hist.debiteur.solde_total !== null)
   131	            ? hist.debiteur.solde_total : null;
   132	
   133	        var html = '<div class="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-start justify-center p-4 bg-black/50 overflow-y-auto py-8" id="detail-modal" onclick="if(event.target===this)this.remove()">';
   134	        html += '<div class="bg-white rounded-2xl p-6 w-full max-w-lg shadow-2xl fade-in my-auto">';
   135	        html += '<div class="flex items-center justify-between mb-4">';
   136	        html += '<h3 class="text-lg font-bold">Fiche Debiteur</h3>';
   137	        html += '<button onclick="document.getElementById(\'detail-modal\').remove()" class="text-slate-400 hover:text-slate-600 font-bold">✕</button>';
   138	        html += '</div>';
   139	
   140	        html += '<div class="flex items-center gap-4 mb-4 p-4 bg-slate-50 rounded-xl">';
   141	        html += '<div style="width:100px;height:100px;position:relative;flex-shrink:0;">';
   142	        html += '<svg viewBox="0 0 100 100" style="width:100%;height:100%;transform:rotate(-90deg)">';
   143	        html += '<circle cx="50" cy="50" r="40" fill="none" stroke="#e2e8f0" stroke-width="10"/>';
   144	        html += '<circle cx="50" cy="50" r="40" fill="none" stroke="'+scoreColor+'" stroke-width="10" stroke-dasharray="'+strokeDash+' 251.33" stroke-linecap="round"/>';
   145	        html += '</svg>';
   146	        html += '<div style="position:absolute;top:50%;left:50%;transform:translate(-50%,-50%);text-align:center;font-weight:bold;font-size:18px;color:'+scoreColor+'">'+score+'%</div>';
   147	        html += '</div>';
   148	        html += '<div><span class="text-sm font-semibold" style="color:'+scoreColor+'">'+label+'</span>';
   149	        html += '<p class="text-xs text-muted mt-1">Score de solvabilité</p>';
   150	        html += '<button onclick="recalculerScore(\''+uuid+'\')" class="mt-2 text-xs text-accent hover:underline font-medium">↺ Recalculer le score</button>';
   151	        html += '</div></div>';
   152	
   153	        if (details && details.comportement_paiement !== undefined) {
   154	            html += '<div class="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2 text-xs mb-4">';
   155	            html += '<div class="p-2 bg-slate-50 rounded-lg"><span class="text-muted">Comportement paiement</span><div class="font-semibold">'+details.comportement_paiement+'%</div></div>';
   156	            html += '<div class="p-2 bg-slate-50 rounded-lg"><span class="text-muted">Sévérité retards</span><div class="font-semibold">'+details.severite_retards+'%</div></div>';
   157	            html += '<div class="p-2 bg-slate-50 rounded-lg"><span class="text-muted">Crédits terminés</span><div class="font-semibold">'+details.credits_termines+'%</div></div>';
   158	            html += '<div class="p-2 bg-slate-50 rounded-lg"><span class="text-muted">Ancienneté</span><div class="font-semibold">'+details.anciennete+'%</div></div>';
   159	            html += '<div class="p-2 bg-slate-50 rounded-lg"><span class="text-muted">Régularité</span><div class="font-semibold">'+details.regularite+'%</div></div>';
   160	            html += '<div class="p-2 bg-slate-50 rounded-lg"><span class="text-muted">Échéances observées</span><div class="font-semibold">'+details.nb_echeances+'</div></div>';
   161	            html += '</div>';
   162	        }
   163	
   164	        html += '<div class="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3 text-sm border-t pt-3">';
   165	        html += '<div><span class="text-muted">Code:</span> <strong>'+(d.code_client||'--')+'</strong></div>';
   166	        html += '<div><span class="text-muted">Nom:</span> <strong>'+d.nom+' '+(d.prenom||'')+'</strong></div>';
   167	        html += '<div><span class="text-muted">Tél:</span> '+(d.telephone||'--')+'</div>';
   168	        html += '<div><span class="text-muted">Quartier:</span> '+(d.quartier||'--')+'</div>';
   169	        html += '<div><span class="text-muted">Adresse:</span> '+(d.adresse||'--')+'</div>';
   170	        html += '<div><span class="text-muted">Activité:</span> '+(d.activite||'--')+'</div>';
   171	        html += '<div><span class="text-muted">Réf.:</span> <strong>'+refNom+'</strong></div>';
   172	        html += '<div><span class="text-muted">Tél réf.:</span> <strong>'+refTel+'</strong></div>';
   173	        html += '<div><span class="text-muted">Agent:</span> '+(d.agent?d.agent.nom:'--')+'</div>';
   174	        html += '<div><span class="text-muted">Crédits autorisés:</span> <strong>'+creditsAuth+'</strong></div>';
   175	        html += '</div>';
   176	
   177	        // Solde restant — affiché même si 0 (correction du bug falsy)
   178	        html += '<div class="mt-3 p-2 rounded-lg text-sm font-semibold '+(soldeTotal > 0 ? 'bg-red-50 text-red-600' : 'bg-green-50 text-green-600')+'">';
   179	        html += 'Solde restant total : ' + (soldeTotal !== null ? f(soldeTotal) : '--');
   180	        html += '</div>';
   181	
   182	        // Historique des achats
   183	        // Situation des échéances
   184	        html += '<div class="mt-3 border-t pt-3">';
   185	        if (hist.ventes && hist.ventes.length > 0) {
   186	            hist.ventes.forEach(function(v) {
   187	                html += '<div class="text-xs">Jours payés : ' + (v.jours_payes || 0) + '/' + (v.jours_total || 0) + '</div>';
   188	                html += '<div class="text-xs">Impayés : ' + (v.jours_impayes || 0) + ' jour(s)</div>';
   189	                html += '<div class="text-xs">Pénalités dues : ' + f(v.penalites_en_attente || 0) + '</div>';
   190	            });
   191	        } else {
   192	            html += '<p class="text-xs text-muted">Aucune échéance</p>';
   193	        }
   194	        html += '</div>';
   195	        
   196	        html += '<div class="border-t pt-3 mt-3">';
   197	        html += '<h4 class="font-semibold text-sm mb-2">Historique des achats</h4>';
   198	        html += (hist.ventes && hist.ventes.length > 0)
   199	            ? '<div class="space-y-1 max-h-32 overflow-y-auto">' + hist.ventes.map(function(v){
   200	                return '<div class="flex justify-between text-xs p-1.5 bg-slate-50 rounded"><span>'+v.date+' | '+(v.produits_resume||v.produit||'--')+'</span><span class="font-semibold">'+f(v.montant_total)+' | '+v.statut+'</span></div>';
   201	              }).join('') + '</div>'
   202	            : '<p class="text-xs text-muted">Aucun achat</p>';
   203	        html += '</div>';
   204	
   205	
   206	        // Historique des paiements
   207	        html += '<div class="mt-3">';
   208	        html += '<h4 class="font-semibold text-sm mb-2">Historique des paiements</h4>';
   209	        html += (hist.paiements && hist.paiements.length > 0)
   210	            ? '<div class="space-y-1 max-h-32 overflow-y-auto">' + hist.paiements.map(function(p){
   211	                return '<div class="flex justify-between text-xs p-1.5 bg-slate-50 rounded"><span>'+p.date+' | '+p.mode+'</span><span class="font-semibold text-green-600">'+f(p.montant)+' | '+p.statut+'</span></div>';
   212	              }).join('') + '</div>'
   213	            : '<p class="text-xs text-muted">Aucun paiement</p>';
   214	        html += '</div>';
   215	
   216	        html += '<div class="mt-4 flex gap-2">';
   217	        html += '<button onclick="modifierDebiteur(\''+uuid+'\',\''+d.nom+'\',\''+(d.telephone||'')+'\')" class="flex-1 px-4 py-2.5 bg-amber-600 text-white rounded-xl text-sm font-semibold">Modifier</button>';
   218	        html += '<button onclick="supprimerDebiteur(\''+uuid+'\',\''+d.nom+'\')" class="flex-1 px-4 py-2.5 bg-red-600 text-white rounded-xl text-sm font-semibold">Supprimer</button>';
   219	        html += '<button onclick="document.getElementById(\'detail-modal\').remove()" class="flex-1 px-4 py-2.5 bg-accent text-white rounded-xl text-sm font-semibold">Fermer</button>';
   220	        html += '</div>';
   221	        html += '</div></div>';
   222	        document.body.insertAdjacentHTML('beforeend', html);
   223	    });
   224	}

   226	function recalculerScore(uuid){
   227	    apiFetch('/debiteurs/'+uuid+'/score/calculer', {method:'POST'}).then(function(r){
   228	        if (r && r.score !== undefined) {
   229	            showAlert('Score de solvabilité recalculé : ' + r.score + '% (' + (r.label || '') + ')');
   230	            voirDebiteur(uuid);
   231	        } else {
   232	            showAlert('Erreur lors du recalcul du score de solvabilité.');
   233	        }
   234	    });
   235	}

   237	function modifierDebiteur(uuid) {
   238	    apiFetch('/debiteurs/' + uuid).then(function(d) {
   239	        window.debiteurEnModification = uuid;
   240	        window.debiteurDataData = d;
   241	
   242	        var fNom = document.getElementById('deb-nom'); if(fNom) fNom.value = d.nom || '';
   243	        var fPrenom = document.getElementById('deb-prenom'); if(fPrenom) fPrenom.value = d.prenom || '';
   244	        var fTel = document.getElementById('deb-telephone'); if(fTel) fTel.value = d.telephone || '';
   245	        var fAdresse = document.getElementById('deb-adresse'); if(fAdresse) fAdresse.value = d.adresse || '';
   246	
   247	        var modal = document.getElementById('detail-modal');
   248	        if (modal) modal.remove();
   249	
   250	        var m1 = document.getElementById('deb-step1-modal');
   251	        if (m1) m1.classList.remove('hidden');
   252	
   253	        if (typeof chargerLocalisations === 'function') {
   254	            setTimeout(function(){ chargerLocalisations(); }, 300);
   255	        }
   256	    }).catch(function(err){
   257	        showAlert('Impossible de charger les données du débiteur.');
   258	    });
   259	}

   261	async function supprimerDebiteur(uuid, nom) {
   262	    if (!(await showConfirm('Supprimer ' + nom + ' ?'))) return;
   263	    apiFetch('/debiteurs/'+uuid, {method:'DELETE'}).then(function(){
   264	        loadPage('debiteurs');
   265	    });

     3	let agentsGlobaux = [];
     4	let debiteursGlobaux = [];
     5	let chartEpargneEvolution = null;

    10	function formatMoney(v) {
    11	    return new Intl.NumberFormat('fr-FR', { style: 'currency', currency: 'XOF', maximumFractionDigits: 0 }).format(parseFloat(v || 0));
    12	}
    13	
    14	function formatDate(iso) {
    15	    if (!iso) return '-';
    16	    return new Date(iso).toLocaleDateString('fr-FR', { day: '2-digit', month: 'short', year: 'numeric' });
    17	}
    18	
    19	function destroyChart(chart) {
    20	    if (chart) { chart.destroy(); }
    21	    return null;
    22	}

    24	async function chargerFiltres() {
    25	    if (agentsGlobaux.length && debiteursGlobaux.length) return;
    26	    try {
    27	        const agents = await apiFetch('/agents');
    28	        const debiteurs = await apiFetch('/debiteurs');
    29	        agentsGlobaux = Array.isArray(agents) ? agents : [];
    30	        debiteursGlobaux = Array.isArray(debiteurs) ? debiteurs : [];
    31	    } catch (e) {
    32	        console.warn('Erreur filtres', e);
    33	    }
    34	}
    35	
    36	function remplirSelects(type) {
    37	    const agentSelect = document.getElementById('filtre-agent-' + type);
    38	    const debiteurSelect = document.getElementById('filtre-debiteur-' + type);
    39	    if (!agentSelect || !debiteurSelect) return;
    40	
    41	    agentSelect.innerHTML = '<option value="">Tous les agents</option>' +
    42	        agentsGlobaux.map(a => `<option value="${a.uuid}">${a.nom} ${a.prenom || ''}</option>`).join('');
    43	
    44	    debiteurSelect.innerHTML = '<option value="">Tous les débiteurs</option>' +
    45	        debiteursGlobaux.map(d => `<option value="${d.uuid}">${d.nom} ${d.prenom || ''}</option>`).join('');
    46	}
    47	
    48	async function initFiltres(type) {
    49	    await chargerFiltres();
    50	    remplirSelects(type);
    51	}
    52	
    53	function getUrlFiltre(type, endpoint) {
    54	    const agentSelect = document.getElementById('filtre-agent-' + type);
    55	    const debiteurSelect = document.getElementById('filtre-debiteur-' + type);
    56	    let url = endpoint + '?';
    57	    if (agentSelect && agentSelect.value) url += 'agent_uuid=' + encodeURIComponent(agentSelect.value) + '&';
    58	    if (debiteurSelect && debiteurSelect.value) url += 'debiteur_uuid=' + encodeURIComponent(debiteurSelect.value) + '&';
    59	    return url;
    60	}
    61	
    62	async function exportEpargnes() {
    63	    const url = getUrlFiltre('epargnes', '/epargnes/export');
    64	    if (typeof apiDownload === 'function') apiDownload(url, 'epargnes.csv');
    65	    else window.location.href = url;
    66	}

    68	async function exportPenalites() {
    69	    const url = getUrlFiltre('penalites', '/penalites/export');
    70	    if (typeof apiDownload === 'function') apiDownload(url, 'penalites.csv');
    71	    else window.location.href = url;
    72	}
    73	
    74	function groupByDate(list, dateKey, montantKey) {
    75	    const map = {};
    76	    list.forEach(item => {
    77	        const date = item[dateKey] ? new Date(item[dateKey]).toLocaleDateString('fr-FR', { day: '2-digit', month: 'short' }) : 'Inconnu';
    78	        map[date] = (map[date] || 0) + parseFloat(item[montantKey] || 0);
    79	    });
    80	    return map;
    81	}
    82	
    83	async function loadEpargnes(c) {
    84	    const data = await apiFetch('/epargnes');
    85	    if (!Array.isArray(data)) {
    86	        c.innerHTML = '<div class="panel text-center py-12"><p class="text-muted">Erreur chargement épargnes</p></div>';
    87	        return;
    88	    }
    89	
    90	    const totalCollecte = data.filter(e => e.statut === 'collecte').reduce((s, e) => s + parseFloat(e.montant || 0), 0);
    91	    const totalRecupere = data.filter(e => e.statut === 'recuperee').reduce((s, e) => s + parseFloat(e.montant || 0), 0);
    92	    const nbCollectes = data.length;
    93	    const clientsUniques = new Set(data.map(e => e.debiteur_id)).size;
    94	    const collectesParJour = groupByDate(data, 'date_collecte', 'montant');
    95	    const labels = Object.keys(collectesParJour).sort();
    96	    const values = labels.map(l => collectesParJour[l]);
    97	
    98	    const rows = data.slice(0, 50).map(e => {
    99	        const deb = e.debiteur ? (e.debiteur.nom + ' ' + (e.debiteur.prenom || '')) : '-';
   100	        const statut = e.statut === 'recuperee' ? '<span class="badge badge-success">Récupérée</span>' : '<span class="badge badge-info">Collectée</span>';
   101	        return `<tr><td>${deb}</td><td>${formatMoney(e.montant)}</td><td>${formatDate(e.date_collecte)}</td><td>${statut}</td></tr>`;
   102	    }).join('');
   103	
   104	    c.innerHTML = `
   105	        <div class="fade-in space-y-6">
   106	            <div class="flex items-center justify-between">
   107	                <h3 class="text-xl font-bold">Épargne tontine</h3>
   108	            </div>
   109	
   110	            <div class="flex flex-wrap gap-2 items-end">
   111	                <div>
   112	                    <label class="block text-xs text-muted mb-1">Agent</label>
   113	                    <select id="filtre-agent-epargnes" class="small-btn small-btn--secondary"><option value="">Tous les agents</option></select>
   114	                </div>

   116	                    <label class="block text-xs text-muted mb-1">Débiteur</label>
   117	                    <select id="filtre-debiteur-epargnes" class="small-btn small-btn--secondary"><option value="">Tous les débiteurs</option></select>
   118	                </div>
   119	                <button class="small-btn small-btn--primary" onclick="exportEpargnes()">📥 Export CSV</button>
   120	            </div>
   121	
   122	            <div class="grid grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-5 gap-4">
   123	                <div class="kpi-card" style="border-left:3px solid #10b981"><div class="kpi-label">Épargne collectée en cours</div><div class="kpi-value">${formatMoney(totalCollecte)}</div></div>
   124	                <div class="kpi-card" style="border-left:3px solid #3b82f6"><div class="kpi-label">Épargne restituée (client)</div><div class="kpi-value">${formatMoney(totalRecupere)}</div></div>
   125	                <div class="kpi-card" style="border-left:3px solid #8b5cf6"><div class="kpi-label">Collectes</div><div class="kpi-value">${nbCollectes}</div></div>
   126	                <div class="kpi-card" style="border-left:3px solid #f59e0b"><div class="kpi-label">Clients actifs</div><div class="kpi-value">${clientsUniques}</div></div>
   127	                <div class="kpi-card" style="border-left:3px solid #ef4444"><div class="kpi-label">Moyenne/jour</div><div class="kpi-value">${formatMoney(totalCollecte / Math.max(1, labels.length))}</div></div>
   128	            </div>
   129	
   130	            <div class="grid lg:grid-cols-2 gap-6">
   131	                <div class="panel p-4">
   132	                    <h4 class="font-semibold mb-3">Évolution des collectes</h4>
   133	                    <div style="position:relative; height:200px;"><canvas id="chartEpargneEvolution"></canvas></div>
   134	                </div>
   135	                <div class="panel p-4">
   136	                    <h4 class="font-semibold mb-3">Répartition</h4>
   137	                    <div style="position:relative; height:200px;"><canvas id="chartEpargneRepartition"></canvas></div>
   138	                </div>
   139	            </div>
   140	
   141	            <div class="panel">
   142	                <div class="table-wrapper">
   143	                    <table>
   144	                        <thead><tr><th>Débiteur</th><th>Montant</th><th>Date</th><th>Statut</th></tr></thead>
   145	                        <tbody>${rows || '<tr><td colspan="4" class="text-center">Aucune épargne</td></tr>'}</tbody>
   146	                    </table>
   147	                </div>
   148	            </div>
   149	        </div>`;
   150	
   151	    initFiltres('epargnes');
   152	
   153	    destroyChart(chartEpargneEvolution);
   154	    if (window.Chart) {
   155	        chartEpargneEvolution = new Chart(document.getElementById('chartEpargneEvolution'), {
   156	            type: 'line',
   157	            data: {
   158	                labels: labels,
   159	                datasets: [{
   160	                    label: 'Montant collecté (FCFA)',
   161	                    data: values,
   162	                    borderColor: '#10b981',
   163	                    backgroundColor: 'rgba(16,185,129,0.1)',
   164	                    fill: true,
   165	                    tension: 0.4,
   166	                    pointRadius: 3,
   167	                    pointBackgroundColor: '#10b981'
   168	                }]
   169	            },
   170	            options: { responsive: true, maintainAspectRatio: false }
   171	        });
   172	    }
   173	
   174	    destroyChart(chartEpargneRepartition);
   175	    if (window.Chart) {
   176	        chartEpargneRepartition = new Chart(document.getElementById('chartEpargneRepartition'), {
   177	            type: 'doughnut',
   178	            data: {
   179	                labels: ['Collectée', 'Récupérée'],
   180	                datasets: [{

   183	                }]
   184	            },

   190	async function loadPenalites(c) {
   191	    const data = await apiFetch('/penalites');
   192	    if (!Array.isArray(data)) {
   193	        c.innerHTML = '<div class="panel text-center py-12"><p class="text-muted">Erreur chargement pénalités</p></div>';
   194	        return;
   195	    }
   196	
   197	    const enAttente = data.filter(p => p.statut === 'en_attente');
   198	    const payees = data.filter(p => p.statut === 'payee');
   199	    const refusees = data.filter(p => p.statut === 'refusee');
   200	    const totalEnAttente = enAttente.reduce((s, p) => s + parseFloat(p.montant || 0), 0);
   201	    const totalPaye = payees.reduce((s, p) => s + parseFloat(p.montant || 0), 0);
   202	    const totalRefuse = refusees.reduce((s, p) => s + parseFloat(p.montant || 0), 0);
   203	    const total = totalEnAttente + totalPaye + totalRefuse;
   204	
   205	    const parJour = groupByDate(data, 'date_appliquee', 'montant');
   206	    const labels = Object.keys(parJour).sort();
   207	    const values = labels.map(l => parJour[l]);
   208	
   209	    const rows = enAttente.slice(0, 50).map(p => {
   210	        const deb = p.debiteur ? (p.debiteur.nom + ' ' + (p.debiteur.prenom || '')) : '-';
   211	        return `<tr><td>${deb}</td><td>${formatMoney(p.montant)}</td><td>${p.jours_retard || 0} j</td><td><span class="badge badge-warning">En attente</span></td></tr>`;
   212	    }).join('');
   213	
   214	    c.innerHTML = `
   215	        <div class="fade-in space-y-6">
   216	            <div class="flex items-center justify-between">
   217	                <h3 class="text-xl font-bold">Pénalités à régulariser</h3>
   218	            </div>
   219	
   220	            <div class="flex flex-wrap gap-2 items-end">
   221	                <div>
   222	                    <label class="block text-xs text-muted mb-1">Agent</label>
   223	                    <select id="filtre-agent-penalites" class="small-btn small-btn--secondary"><option value="">Tous les agents</option></select>
   224	                </div>
   225	                <div>
   226	                    <label class="block text-xs text-muted mb-1">Débiteur</label>
   227	                    <select id="filtre-debiteur-penalites" class="small-btn small-btn--secondary"><option value="">Tous les débiteurs</option></select>
   228	                </div>
   229	                <button class="small-btn small-btn--primary" onclick="exportPenalites()">📥 Export CSV</button>
   230	            </div>
   231	
   232	            <div class="grid grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-4 gap-4">
   233	                <div class="kpi-card" style="border-left:3px solid #ef4444"><div class="kpi-label">Total dû</div><div class="kpi-value">${formatMoney(total)}</div></div>
   234	                <div class="kpi-card" style="border-left:3px solid #f59e0b"><div class="kpi-label">En attente</div><div class="kpi-value">${formatMoney(totalEnAttente)}</div></div>
   235	                <div class="kpi-card" style="border-left:3px solid #10b981"><div class="kpi-label">Recouvré</div><div class="kpi-value">${formatMoney(totalPaye)}</div></div>
   236	                <div class="kpi-card" style="border-left:3px solid #6b7280"><div class="kpi-label">Refusé / compensé</div><div class="kpi-value">${formatMoney(totalRefuse)}</div></div>
   237	            </div>
   238	
   239	            <div class="grid lg:grid-cols-2 gap-6">
   240	                <div class="panel p-4">
   241	                    <h4 class="font-semibold mb-3">Pénalités par jour</h4>
   242	                    <div style="position:relative; height:200px;"><canvas id="chartPenaliteEvolution"></canvas></div>
   243	                </div>
   244	                <div class="panel p-4">
   245	                    <h4 class="font-semibold mb-3">Répartition</h4>
   246	                    <div style="position:relative; height:200px;"><canvas id="chartPenaliteRepartition"></canvas></div>
   247	                </div>
   248	            </div>
   249	
   250	            <div class="panel">
   251	                <h4 class="font-semibold p-4 pb-0">Pénalités en attente</h4>
   252	                <div class="table-wrapper">
   253	                    <table>
   254	                        <thead><tr><th>Débiteur</th><th>Montant</th><th>Retard</th><th>Statut</th></tr></thead>
   255	                        <tbody>${rows || '<tr><td colspan="4" class="text-center">Aucune pénalité</td></tr>'}</tbody>
   256	                    </table>
   257	                </div>
   258	            </div>
   259	        </div>`;
   260	
   261	    initFiltres('penalites');
   262	
   263	    destroyChart(chartPenaliteEvolution);
   264	    if (window.Chart) {
   265	        chartPenaliteEvolution = new Chart(document.getElementById('chartPenaliteEvolution'), {
   266	            type: 'line',
   267	            data: {
   268	                labels: labels,
   269	                datasets: [{
   270	                    label: 'Montant pénalités (FCFA)',
   271	                    data: values,
   272	                    borderColor: '#ef4444',
   273	                    backgroundColor: 'rgba(239,68,68,0.1)',
   274	                    fill: true,
   275	                    tension: 0.4,
   276	                    pointRadius: 3,
   277	                    pointBackgroundColor: '#ef4444'
   278	                }]
   279	            },
   280	            options: { responsive: true, maintainAspectRatio: false }
   281	        });
   282	    }
   283	
   284	    destroyChart(chartPenaliteRepartition);
   285	    if (window.Chart) {
   286	        chartPenaliteRepartition = new Chart(document.getElementById('chartPenaliteRepartition'), {
   287	            type: 'doughnut',
   288	            data: {
   289	                labels: ['En attente', 'Recouvrée', 'Refusée'],
   290	                datasets: [{
   291	                    data: [totalEnAttente, totalPaye, totalRefuse],
   292	                    backgroundColor: ['#f59e0b', '#10b981', '#6b7280'],
   293	                }]
   294	            },
   295	            options: { responsive: true, maintainAspectRatio: false }
   296	        });
   297	    }
   298	}
===== public/dashboard/chef/js/fix-modals.js =====
     1	(function () {
     2	    'use strict';
     3	
     4	    function deplacerModales() {
     5	        var ids = [
     6	            'stock-entree-modal',
     7	            'encaisser-modal',
     8	            'stock-sortie-modal',
     9	            'inventaire-modal',
    10	            'caisse-decaissement-modal',
    11	            'caisse-cloture-modal'
    12	        ];
    13	
    14	        ids.forEach(function (id) {
    15	            var modale = document.getElementById(id);
    16	
    17	            if (modale && modale.parentElement !== document.body) {
    18	                document.body.appendChild(modale);
    19	                modale.classList.add('hidden');
    20	            }
    21	        });
    22	    }
    23	
    24	    if (document.readyState === 'loading') {
    25	        document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', deplacerModales);
    26	    } else {
    27	        deplacerModales();
    28	    }
    29	})();
===== public/dashboard/chef/js/localisation-loader.js =====
     1	// === CHARGEUR DE LOCALISATION SIMPLE ET ROBUSTE ===
     2	
     3	function chargerLocalisations() {
     4	    console.log('[LOC] Chargement des localisations...');
     5	    
     6	    var depSelect = document.getElementById('deb-departement');
     7	    var comSelect = document.getElementById('deb-commune');
     8	    var arrSelect = document.getElementById('deb-arrondissement');
     9	    var vilSelect = document.getElementById('deb-village');
    10	    
    11	    if (!depSelect) {
    12	        console.warn('[LOC] #deb-departement introuvable');
    13	        return;
    14	    }
    15	    
    16	    // Charger les départements
    17	    fetch('/api/localisations?type=departement')
    18	        .then(r => r.json())
    19	        .then(data => {
    20	            depSelect.innerHTML = '<option value="">-- Departement --</option>' +
    21	                data.map(d => '<option value="' + d.nom + '">' + d.nom + '</option>').join('');
    22	            console.log('[LOC] ' + data.length + ' departements charges');
    23	        });
    24	    
    25	    // Quand on change de département
    26	    depSelect.onchange = function() {
    27	        comSelect.innerHTML = '<option value="">-- Commune --</option>';
    28	        arrSelect.innerHTML = '<option value="">-- Arrondissement --</option>';
    29	        vilSelect.innerHTML = '<option value="">-- Village/Quartier --</option>';
    30	        
    31	        if (!this.value) return;
    32	        
    33	        fetch('/api/localisations?type=commune&parent=' + encodeURIComponent(this.value))
    34	            .then(r => r.json())
    35	            .then(data => {
    36	                comSelect.innerHTML = '<option value="">-- Commune --</option>' +
    37	                    data.map(c => '<option value="' + c.nom + '">' + c.nom + '</option>').join('');
    38	                console.log('[LOC] ' + data.length + ' communes charges');
    39	            });
    40	    };
    41	    
    42	    // Quand on change de commune
    43	    if (comSelect) comSelect.onchange = function() {
    44	        arrSelect.innerHTML = '<option value="">-- Arrondissement --</option>';
    45	        vilSelect.innerHTML = '<option value="">-- Village/Quartier --</option>';
    46	        
    47	        if (!this.value) return;
    48	        
    49	        fetch('/api/localisations?type=arrondissement&parent=' + encodeURIComponent(this.value))
    50	            .then(r => r.json())
    51	            .then(data => {
    52	                arrSelect.innerHTML = '<option value="">-- Arrondissement --</option>' +
    53	                    data.map(a => '<option value="' + a.nom + '">' + a.nom + '</option>').join('');
    54	                console.log('[LOC] ' + data.length + ' arrondissements charges');
    55	            });
    56	    };
    57	    
    58	    // Quand on change d'arrondissement
    59	    if (arrSelect) arrSelect.onchange = function() {
    60	        vilSelect.innerHTML = '<option value="">-- Village/Quartier --</option>';
    61	        
    62	        if (!this.value) return;
    63	        
    64	        fetch('/api/localisations?type=quartier&parent=' + encodeURIComponent(this.value))
    65	            .then(r => r.json())
    66	            .then(data => {
    67	                vilSelect.innerHTML = '<option value="">-- Village/Quartier --</option>' +
    68	                    data.map(q => '<option value="' + q.nom + '">' + q.nom + '</option>').join('');
    69	                console.log('[LOC] ' + data.length + ' quartiers charges');
    70	            });
    71	    };
    72	}
    73	
    74	function getLocalisationComplete() {
    75	    var parts = [];
    76	    var ids = ['deb-departement', 'deb-commune', 'deb-arrondissement', 'deb-village'];
    77	    ids.forEach(function(id) {
    78	        var el = document.getElementById(id);
    79	        if (el && el.value) parts.push(el.value);
    80	    });
    81	    return parts.join(' > ') || '';
    82	}
    83	
    84	// Exposer globalement
    85	window.chargerLocalisations = chargerLocalisations;
    86	window.getLocalisationComplete = getLocalisationComplete;
