     1	function loadNotificationsPage(c){apiFetch('/notifications').then(function(n){var typeIcon={echeance:'📅',retard:'⚠️',stock:'📦',vente:'💰',paiement:'💳'};c.innerHTML='<div class="fade-in space-y-6"><div class="flex items-center justify-between"><h3 class="text-xl font-bold">Notifications</h3><button onclick="marquerNotifsLues()" class="bg-slate-200 text-slate-700 px-4 py-2 rounded-lg text-sm font-semibold hover:bg-slate-300">Tout marquer lu</button></div><div class="space-y-3">'+(n.length>0?n.map(function(x){return '<div class="panel '+(x.lue?'':'border-l-4 border-accent')+'"><div class="flex items-start justify-between"><div><span class="text-xl mr-2">'+(typeIcon[x.type]||'📌')+'</span><span class="font-semibold">'+x.titre+'</span><p class="text-sm text-muted mt-1">'+x.message+'</p></div><div class="text-xs text-muted">'+new Date(x.created_at).toLocaleDateString('fr-FR')+'</div></div></div>';}).join(''):'<div class="panel text-center py-12"><p class="text-muted">Aucune notification</p></div>')+'</div></div>';}).catch(function(e){c.innerHTML='<div class="panel text-center py-12"><p class="text-muted">Erreur de chargement.</p></div>';});}
     2	function marquerNotifsLues(){apiFetch('/notifications/toutes-lues',{method:'PUT',body:'{}'}).then(function(){loadPage('notifications');});}

    27	    loadParametresData();

    30	function loadParametresData(){
    31	    apiFetch('/parametres').then(function(d){
    32	        var t=document.getElementById('agences-tbody');
    33	        if(t&&d.agences)t.innerHTML=d.agences.length>0?d.agences.map(function(a){return '<tr><td class="font-medium">'+a.nom+'</td><td>'+(a.adresse||'--')+'</td><td>'+(a.telephone||'--')+'</td><td><span class="badge '+(a.actif?'badge-success':'badge-danger')+'">'+(a.actif?'Actif':'Inactif')+'</span></td></tr>';}).join(''):'<tr><td colspan="4" class="text-center text-muted py-4">Aucune agence</td></tr>';
    34	        
    35	        var u=document.getElementById('users-tbody');
    36	        if(u&&d.utilisateurs)u.innerHTML=d.utilisateurs.map(function(x){
    37	            var actions = '<div class="flex gap-1">';
    38	            actions += '<button onclick="openPermissionsModal(\''+x.uuid+'\',\''+(x.nom||'')+'\','+JSON.stringify(x.permissions||{})+')" class="px-2 py-1 bg-blue-100 text-blue-700 rounded text-xs hover:bg-blue-200">Permissions</button>';
    39	            actions += '<button onclick="openPasswordModal(\''+x.uuid+'\',\''+(x.nom||'')+'\')" class="px-2 py-1 bg-amber-100 text-amber-700 rounded text-xs hover:bg-amber-200">MDP</button>';
    40	            if(x.role!=='gerante') actions += '<button onclick="openSuspendreModal(\''+x.uuid+'\',\''+(x.nom||'')+'\','+x.actif+')" class="px-2 py-1 rounded text-xs font-medium '+(x.actif?'bg-red-100 text-red-700 hover:bg-red-200':'bg-green-100 text-green-700 hover:bg-green-200')+'">'+(x.actif?'Suspendre':'Reactivation')+'</button>';
    41	            actions += '</div>';
    42	            
    43	            return '<tr>'+
    44	                '<td class="font-medium text-xs">'+x.nom+' '+(x.prenom||'')+'</td>'+
    45	                '<td class="text-xs">'+x.email+'</td>'+
    46	                '<td><select onchange="updateUser(\''+x.uuid+'\',\'role\',this.value)" class="px-1 py-0.5 border rounded text-xs"><option value="gerante" '+(x.role==='gerante'?'selected':'')+'>Gerante</option><option value="chef_agence" '+(x.role==='chef_agence'?'selected':'')+'>Chef agence</option><option value="agent" '+(x.role==='agent'?'selected':'')+'>Agent</option></select></td>'+
    47	                '<td>'+actions+'</td>'+
    48	                '</tr>';
    49	        }).join('');
    50	        
    51	        var m=document.getElementById('magasins-tbody');
    52	        if(m&&d.magasins)m.innerHTML=d.magasins.length>0?d.magasins.map(function(x){return '<tr><td class="font-medium">'+x.nom+'</td><td>'+(x.adresse||'--')+'</td><td>'+(x.responsable||'--')+'</td><td><span class="badge '+(x.actif?'badge-success':'badge-danger')+'">'+(x.actif?'Actif':'Inactif')+'</span></td></tr>';}).join(''):'<tr><td colspan="4" class="text-center text-muted py-4">Aucun magasin</td></tr>';
    53	    });
    54	}

    56	// === MODAL PERMISSIONS ===
    57	function openPermissionsModal(uuid, nom, permissions) {
    58	    var perms = permissions || {};
    59	    var html = '<div class="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-center justify-center p-4 bg-black/50" id="perm-modal-overlay" onclick="if(event.target===this)closePermissionsModal()">';
    60	    html += '<div class="bg-white rounded-2xl p-6 w-full max-w-md shadow-2xl fade-in max-h-96 overflow-y-auto">';
    61	    html += '<h3 class="text-lg font-bold mb-4">Permissions de ' + nom + '</h3>';
    62	    html += '<div class="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2">';
    63	    allPages.forEach(function(p) {
    64	        var checked = perms[p.id] !== undefined ? perms[p.id] : true;
    65	        html += '<label class="flex items-center gap-2 p-2 bg-slate-50 rounded-lg cursor-pointer hover:bg-slate-100 text-sm"><input type="checkbox" '+(checked?'checked':'')+' onchange="togglePermission(\''+uuid+'\',\''+p.id+'\',this.checked)" class="w-4 h-4 text-accent">'+p.label+'</label>';
    66	    });
    67	    html += '</div>';
    68	    html += '<div class="flex gap-3 pt-4"><button onclick="closePermissionsModal()" class="flex-1 px-4 py-2.5 bg-accent text-white rounded-xl text-sm font-semibold">Fermer</button></div>';
    69	    html += '</div></div>';
    70	    
    71	    var existing = document.getElementById('perm-modal-overlay');
    72	    if(existing) existing.remove();
    73	    document.body.insertAdjacentHTML('beforeend', html);
    74	}

    77	    var el = document.getElementById('perm-modal-overlay');
    78	    if(el) el.remove();
    79	}

   120	    html += '<h3 class="text-lg font-bold mb-2">' + action + ' l\'utilisateur ?</h3>';
   121	    html += '<p class="text-sm text-muted mb-6"><strong>' + nom + '</strong> sera ' + (actif ? 'bloque et ne pourra plus se connecter' : 'debloque et pourra se reconnecter') + '.</p>';

   124	    html += '</div></div>';
   125

   127	    if(existing) existing.remove();
   128	    document.body.insertAdjacentHTML('beforeend', html);
   129	}

===== public/dashboard/chef/js/produits-functions.js =====
     1	function loadProduits(c){
     2	    apiFetch('/produits').then(function(p){
     3	        function f(v){return new Intl.NumberFormat('fr-FR',{style:'currency',currency:'XOF',maximumFractionDigits:0}).format(v||0);}
     4	        
     5	        c.innerHTML='<div class="fade-in space-y-6">'+
     6	            '<div class="flex items-center justify-between">'+
     7	                '<h3 class="text-xl font-bold">Produits ('+p.length+')</h3>'+
     8	                '<button onclick="showAddProduitModal()" class="bg-accent text-white px-4 py-2 rounded-lg text-sm font-semibold hover:bg-blue-700">+ Nouveau Produit</button>'+
     9	            '</div>'+
    10	            '<div class="panel">'+
    11	                '<div class="flex items-center justify-between mb-4">'+
    12	                    '<h3>Liste des produits</h3>'+
    13	                    '<input type="text" id="prod-search" placeholder="Rechercher..." onkeyup="filterProduits()" class="px-4 py-2 border border-slate-200 rounded-xl text-sm w-64">'+
    14	                '</div>'+
    15	                '<div class="table-wrapper">'+
    16	                    '<table><thead><tr><th>Produit</th><th>Prix Achat</th><th>Prix Vente</th><th>Benefice</th><th>Marge</th><th>Stock</th><th>Actions</th></tr></thead>'+
    17	                    '<tbody id="produits-tbody">'+
    18	                        p.map(function(x){
    19	                            var benefice = (x.prix_vente||0) - (x.prix_achat||0);
    20	                            var marge = (x.prix_achat > 0) ? ((benefice / x.prix_achat) * 100).toFixed(1) + '%' : '--';
    21	                            return '<tr>'+
    22	                                '<td class="font-medium">'+x.nom+'</td>'+
    23	                                '<td><input type="number" value="'+(x.prix_achat||0)+'" onchange="updateProduitPrix(\''+x.uuid+'\',\'prix_achat\',this.value)" class="w-24 px-2 py-1 border rounded text-xs text-right"></td>'+
    24	                                '<td><input type="number" value="'+(x.prix_vente||0)+'" onchange="updateProduitPrix(\''+x.uuid+'\',\'prix_vente\',this.value)" class="w-24 px-2 py-1 border rounded text-xs text-right"></td>'+
    25	                                '<td class="font-semibold text-green-600">'+f(benefice)+'</td>'+
    26	                                '<td class="text-xs">'+marge+'</td>'+
    27	                                '<td><span class="badge '+(x.stock<=x.seuil_alerte?'badge-danger':'badge-success')+'">'+(x.stock||0)+'</span></td>'+
    28	                                '<td><div class="flex gap-1">'+
    29	                                    '<button onclick="categoriserProduit(\''+x.uuid+'\',\''+x.nom+'\')" class="text-blue-600 text-xs">Cat.</button>'+
    30	                                    '<button onclick="supprimerProduit(\''+x.uuid+'\',\''+x.nom+'\')" class="text-red-600 text-xs">Suppr.</button>'+
    31	                                '</div></td>'+
    32	                            '</tr>';
    33	                        }).join('')+
    34	                    '</tbody></table>'+
    35	                '</div>'+
    36	            '</div>'+
    37	            '<div class="panel max-w-md">'+
    38	                '<h3>Resume des benefices</h3>'+
    39	                '<div class="grid grid-cols-3 gap-3 mt-4">'+
    40	                    '<div class="text-center"><span class="text-xs text-muted">Valeur achat</span><div class="font-bold">'+f(p.reduce((s,x)=>s+(x.prix_achat||0)*(x.stock||0),0))+'</div></div>'+
    41	                    '<div class="text-center"><span class="text-xs text-muted">Valeur vente</span><div class="font-bold text-green-600">'+f(p.reduce((s,x)=>s+(x.prix_vente||0)*(x.stock||0),0))+'</div></div>'+
    42	                    '<div class="text-center"><span class="text-xs text-muted">Benefice potentiel</span><div class="font-bold text-accent">'+f(p.reduce((s,x)=>s+((x.prix_vente||0)-(x.prix_achat||0))*(x.stock||0),0))+'</div></div>'+
    43	                '</div>'+
    44	            '</div>'+
    45	        '</div>';
    46	    });
    47	}
    48	
    49	function filterProduits(){
    50	    var q = document.getElementById('prod-search').value.toLowerCase();
    51	    document.querySelectorAll('#produits-tbody tr').forEach(function(row){
    52	        var nom = row.cells[0].textContent.toLowerCase();
    53	        row.style.display = nom.includes(q) ? '' : 'none';
    54	    });
    55	}
    56	
    57	function updateProduitPrix(uuid, champ, valeur){
    58	    var data = {};
    59	    data[champ] = parseFloat(valeur) || 0;
    60	    apiFetch('/produits/'+uuid, {method:'PUT', body:JSON.stringify(data)}).then(function(){
    61	        loadPage('produits');
    62	    });
    63	}
    64	
    65	async function categoriserProduit(uuid, nom){
    66	    // Ouvrir un prompt avec les catégories existantes ou créer
    67	    var categories = ['Riz','Sucre','Huile','Tomate','Boisson','Lait','Sardine','Pates','Farine','Savon','Autre'];
    68	    var choix = await showPrompt('Catégorie pour ' + nom + ' :\n' + categories.join(', '), ''); if(!choix) return;
    69	    if(!choix) return;
    70	    apiFetch('/produits/'+uuid, {method:'PUT', body:JSON.stringify({categorie_nom:choix})}).then(function(){
    71	        loadPage('produits');
    72	    });
    73	}
    74	
    75	async function supprimerProduit(uuid, nom){
    76	    if (!(await showConfirm('Supprimer ' + nom + ' ?'))) return;
    77	    apiFetch('/produits/'+uuid, {method:'DELETE'}).then(function(){
    78	        loadPage('produits');
    79	    });
    80	}

    82	function showAddProduitModal(){document.getElementById('add-produit-modal').classList.remove('hidden');}
    83	function closeAddProduitModal(){document.getElementById('add-produit-modal').classList.add('hidden');}
    84	function createProduit(e){
    85	    e.preventDefault();
    86	    var data = {
    87	        nom: document.getElementById('prod-nom').value,
    88	        prix_achat: parseFloat(document.getElementById('prod-prix-achat').value)||0,
    89	        prix_vente: parseFloat(document.getElementById('prod-prix-vente').value)||0,
    90	        seuil_alerte: parseInt(document.getElementById('prod-seuil').value)||5
    91	    };
    92	    apiFetch('/produits', {method:'POST', body:JSON.stringify(data)}).then(function(){
    93	        closeAddProduitModal();
    94	        loadPage('produits');
    95	    });
    96	}

     1	function loadRapports(c){c.innerHTML='<div class="fade-in space-y-6"><h3 class="text-xl font-bold">Rapports et Exports</h3><div class="row-grid"><div class="panel text-center py-8"><div class="text-4xl mb-3">📊</div><h3>Rapport Ventes</h3><p class="text-muted text-sm mb-4">Exportez la liste complete des ventes</p><a href="/api/rapports/ventes?format=csv" onclick="this.href=this.href+&#39;&token=&#39;+localStorage.getItem(&#39;sourougnon_token&#39;)" target="_blank" class="inline-block bg-accent text-white px-6 py-3 rounded-xl text-sm font-semibold hover:bg-blue-700">Export CSV Ventes</a></div><div class="panel text-center py-8"><div class="text-4xl mb-3">💳</div><h3>Rapport Paiements</h3><p class="text-muted text-sm mb-4">Exportez l historique des encaissements</p><a href="/api/rapports/recouvrements?format=csv" onclick="this.href=this.href+&#39;&token=&#39;+localStorage.getItem(&#39;sourougnon_token&#39;)" target="_blank" class="inline-block bg-accent text-white px-6 py-3 rounded-xl text-sm font-semibold hover:bg-blue-700">Export CSV Paiements</a></div><div class="panel text-center py-8"><div class="text-4xl mb-3">📋</div><h3>Resume Mensuel</h3><p class="text-muted text-sm mb-4">Synthese des performances du mois</p><a href="/api/rapports/resume" onclick="event.preventDefault();loadResume(this)" class="inline-block bg-accent text-white px-6 py-3 rounded-xl text-sm font-semibold hover:bg-blue-700">Voir le resume</a></div></div><div id="resume-content"></div></div>';}
     2	function loadResume(b){b.textContent='Chargement...';apiFetch('/rapports/resume').then(function(r){function f(v){return new Intl.NumberFormat('fr-FR',{style:'currency',currency:'XOF',maximumFractionDigits:0}).format(v);}var c=document.getElementById('resume-content');c.innerHTML='<div class="fade-in space-y-6 mt-6"><div class="panel"><h3>Aujourd hui</h3><div class="kpi-grid"><div class="kpi-card"><div class="kpi-label">Encaissements</div><div class="kpi-value">'+f(r.aujourdhui.encaissements)+'</div></div><div class="kpi-card"><div class="kpi-label">Nb Paiements</div><div class="kpi-value">'+r.aujourdhui.nb_paiements+'</div></div><div class="kpi-card"><div class="kpi-label">Ventes</div><div class="kpi-value">'+r.aujourdhui.ventes_jour+'</div></div><div class="kpi-card"><div class="kpi-label">CA Jour</div><div class="kpi-value">'+f(r.aujourdhui.ca_jour)+'</div></div></div></div><div class="panel"><h3>Ce mois</h3><div class="kpi-grid"><div class="kpi-card"><div class="kpi-label">Encaissements</div><div class="kpi-value">'+f(r.mois.encaissements)+'</div></div><div class="kpi-card"><div class="kpi-label">Nb Paiements</div><div class="kpi-value">'+r.mois.nb_paiements+'</div></div><div class="kpi-card"><div class="kpi-label">Ventes</div><div class="kpi-value">'+r.mois.ventes_mois+'</div></div><div class="kpi-card"><div class="kpi-label">Creances</div><div class="kpi-value">'+f(r.mois.creances)+'</div></div></div></div><div class="panel"><h3>Top Agents du Mois</h3><div class="table-wrapper"><table><thead><tr><th>Agent</th><th class="text-right">Total Encaisse</th></tr></thead><tbody>'+r.top_agents_mois.map(function(a){return '<tr><td class="font-medium">'+(a.agent?a.agent.nom:'--')+' '+(a.agent?a.agent.prenom:'')+'</td><td class="text-right font-semibold">'+f(a.total)+'</td></tr>';}).join('')+'</tbody></table></div></div></div>';b.textContent='Rafraichir le resume';});}
===== public/dashboard/chef/js/recherche-globale.js =====
     1	// === MOTEUR DE RECHERCHE GLOBALE ===
     2	var rechercheTimeout;
     3	
     4	function rechercheGlobale(query) {
     5	    clearTimeout(rechercheTimeout);
     6	    var resultsDiv = document.getElementById('search-results');
     7	    if (!resultsDiv) return;
     8	    
     9	    if (!query || query.length < 2) {
    10	        resultsDiv.classList.add('hidden');
    11	        return;
    12	    }
    13	    
    14	    rechercheTimeout = setTimeout(function() {
    15	        apiFetch('/recherche?q=' + encodeURIComponent(query)).then(function(data) {
    16	            if (!data || (!data.debiteurs.length && !data.ventes.length && !data.agents.length && !data.produits.length)) {
    17	                resultsDiv.innerHTML = '<div class="p-4 text-sm text-muted text-center">Aucun resultat pour "' + query + '"</div>';
    18	                resultsDiv.classList.remove('hidden');
    19	                return;
    20	            }
    21	            
    22	            var html = '';
    23	            
    24	            if (data.debiteurs && data.debiteurs.length > 0) {
    25	                html += '<div class="p-2 text-xs font-semibold text-muted uppercase">Debiteurs (' + data.debiteurs.length + ')</div>';
    26	                data.debiteurs.forEach(function(d) {
    27	                    html += '<div class="px-4 py-2 hover:bg-slate-50 cursor-pointer text-sm" onclick="loadPage(\'debiteurs\');document.getElementById(\'search-results\').classList.add(\'hidden\')">' +
    28	                        '<span class="font-medium">' + d.nom + ' ' + (d.prenom||'') + '</span> ' +
    29	                        '<span class="text-muted text-xs">' + (d.quartier||'') + ' | ' + (d.telephone||'') + '</span></div>';
    30	                });
    31	            }
    32	            
    33	            if (data.agents && data.agents.length > 0) {
    34	                html += '<div class="p-2 text-xs font-semibold text-muted uppercase border-t">Agents (' + data.agents.length + ')</div>';
    35	                data.agents.forEach(function(a) {
    36	                    html += '<div class="px-4 py-2 hover:bg-slate-50 cursor-pointer text-sm" onclick="loadPage(\'agents\');document.getElementById(\'search-results\').classList.add(\'hidden\')">' +
    37	                        '<span class="font-medium">' + a.nom + ' ' + (a.prenom||'') + '</span> ' +
    38	                        '<span class="text-muted text-xs">' + a.email + ' | ' + a.role + '</span></div>';
    39	                });
    40	            }
    41	            
    42	            if (data.ventes && data.ventes.length > 0) {
    43	                html += '<div class="p-2 text-xs font-semibold text-muted uppercase border-t">Ventes (' + data.ventes.length + ')</div>';
    44	                data.ventes.forEach(function(v) {
    45	                    html += '<div class="px-4 py-2 hover:bg-slate-50 cursor-pointer text-sm" onclick="loadPage(\'ventes\');document.getElementById(\'search-results\').classList.add(\'hidden\')">' +
    46	                        '<span class="font-medium">' + (v.debiteur_nom||'Client') + '</span> ' +
    47	                        '<span class="text-muted text-xs">' + v.type_vente + ' | ' + new Intl.NumberFormat('fr-FR',{style:'currency',currency:'XOF'}).format(v.montant_total||0) + '</span></div>';
    48	                });
    49	            }
    50	            
    51	            if (data.produits && data.produits.length > 0) {
    52	                html += '<div class="p-2 text-xs font-semibold text-muted uppercase border-t">Produits (' + data.produits.length + ')</div>';
    53	                data.produits.forEach(function(p) {
    54	                    html += '<div class="px-4 py-2 hover:bg-slate-50 cursor-pointer text-sm" onclick="loadPage(\'produits\');document.getElementById(\'search-results\').classList.add(\'hidden\')">' +
    55	                        '<span class="font-medium">' + p.nom + '</span> ' +
    56	                        '<span class="text-muted text-xs">Stock: ' + (p.stock||0) + ' | Prix: ' + new Intl.NumberFormat('fr-FR',{style:'currency',currency:'XOF'}).format(p.prix_vente||0) + '</span></div>';
    57	                });
    58	            }
    59	            
    60	            resultsDiv.innerHTML = html;
    61	            resultsDiv.classList.remove('hidden');
    62	        }).catch(function(e) {
    63	            console.error('Erreur recherche:', e);
    64	        });
    65	    }, 300);
    66	}
    67	
    68	// Fermer les resultats si on clique ailleurs
    69	document.addEventListener('click', function(e) {
    70	    var resultsDiv = document.getElementById('search-results');
    71	    var searchInput = document.getElementById('search-input');
    72	    if (resultsDiv && !resultsDiv.contains(e.target) && e.target !== searchInput) {
    73	        resultsDiv.classList.add('hidden');
    74	    }
    75	});

===== public/dashboard/chef/js/stocks-functions.js =====
     1	function loadStocks(c){
     2	    apiFetch('/stocks').then(function(d){
     3	        function f(v){return new Intl.NumberFormat('fr-FR',{style:'currency',currency:'XOF',maximumFractionDigits:0}).format(v);}
     4	        var p=d.produits;
     5	        var produitsEnStock = p.filter(function(x){return (x.stock || 0) > 0;});
     6	        var nbEnStock = produitsEnStock.length;
     7	        var al=produitsEnStock.filter(function(x){return x.stock<=x.seuil_alerte;});
     8	        
     9	        c.innerHTML='<div class="fade-in space-y-6">'+
    10	            '<div class="flex items-center justify-between"><h3 class="text-xl font-bold">Stock</h3>'+
    11	            '<div class="flex gap-2">'+
    12	                '<button onclick="showStockEntreeModal()" class="bg-green-600 text-white px-4 py-2 rounded-lg text-sm font-semibold hover:bg-green-700">+ Entree</button>'+
    13	                '<button onclick="showStockSortieModal()" class="bg-amber-600 text-white px-4 py-2 rounded-lg text-sm font-semibold hover:bg-amber-700">- Sortie</button>'+
    14	                '<button onclick="showInventaireModal()" class="bg-purple-600 text-white px-4 py-2 rounded-lg text-sm font-semibold hover:bg-purple-700">Inventaire</button>'+
    15	            '</div></div>'+
    16	            '<div class="kpi-grid">'+
    17	                '<div class="kpi-card" style="border-left:3px solid #0066ff"><div class="kpi-label">Produits en stock</div><div class="kpi-value">'+nbEnStock+' / '+p.length+'</div></div>'+
    18	                '<div class="kpi-card" style="border-left:3px solid #059669"><div class="kpi-label">Valeur Achat</div><div class="kpi-value">'+f(d.valeur_totale_achat)+'</div></div>'+
    19	                '<div class="kpi-card" style="border-left:3px solid #8b5cf6"><div class="kpi-label">Valeur Vente</div><div class="kpi-value">'+f(d.valeur_totale_vente)+'</div></div>'+
    20	                '<div class="kpi-card" style="border-left:3px solid #00c853"><div class="kpi-label">CA Attendu</div><div class="kpi-value">'+f(d.ca_attendu)+'</div></div>'+
    21	            '</div>'+
    22	            '<div class="panel">'+
    23	                '<div class="flex items-center justify-between mb-4"><h3 class="text-lg font-semibold">Mouvements de stock</h3></div>'+
    24	                '<div id="mouvements-stock-content"><p class="text-center text-muted py-4">Chargement...</p></div>'+
    25	            '</div>'+
    26	        '</div>';
    27	        loadMouvementsStock();
    28	    }).catch(function(e){c.innerHTML='<div class="panel text-center py-12"><p class="text-muted">Erreur de chargement.</p></div>';});
    29	}
    30	
    31	function loadMouvementsStock() {
    32	    apiFetch('/stocks/mouvements').then(function(m) {
    33	        window.allMouvementsStock = m;
    34	        renderMouvementsStock(m);
    35	    });
    36	}
    37	
    38	function renderMouvementsStock(m) {
    39	    function f(v) {
    40	        return new Intl.NumberFormat('fr-FR', { style: 'currency', currency: 'XOF', maximumFractionDigits: 0 }).format(v);
    41	    }
    42	    var c = document.getElementById('mouvements-stock-content');
    43	    if (!c) return;
    44	    var tb = { entree: 'badge-success', vente: 'badge-danger', sortie: 'badge-danger', perte: 'badge-danger', retour: 'badge-warning', inventaire: 'badge-info' };
    45	    var actionBadge = { creation: 'badge-success', modification: 'badge-warning', suppression: 'badge-danger', inventaire: 'badge-info' };
    46	    
    47	    c.innerHTML = '<div class="table-wrapper"><table><thead><tr><th>Date</th><th>Type</th><th>Action</th><th>Produit</th><th>Qte</th><th>Prix Unit.</th><th>Fournisseur</th><th>Actions</th></tr></thead><tbody>' +
    48	    (m.length > 0 ? m.map(function(x) {
    49	        return '<tr>' +
    50	        '<td class="text-xs">' + new Date(x.created_at).toLocaleDateString('fr-FR') + ' ' + new Date(x.created_at).toLocaleTimeString('fr-FR', {hour:'2-digit',minute:'2-digit'}) + '</td>' +
    51	        '<td><span class="badge ' + (tb[x.type] || 'badge-info') + '">' + x.type + '</span></td>' +
    52	        '<td><span class="badge ' + (actionBadge[x.action] || 'badge-success') + '">' + (x.action || 'creation') + '</span></td>' +
    53	        '<td class="font-medium">' + (x.produit ? x.produit.nom : '-') + '</td>' +
    54	        '<td>' + x.quantite + '</td>' +
    55	        '<td>' + f(x.prix_unitaire) + '</td>' +
    56	        '<td>' + (x.fournisseur || '-') + '</td>' +
    57	        '<td><div class="flex gap-1">' +
    58	        '<button onclick="openModifierMouvementModal(' + x.id + ',' + x.quantite + ',' + x.prix_unitaire + ',\'' + (x.fournisseur || '').replace(/'/g, "\\'") + '\')" class="text-amber-600 text-xs font-semibold px-2 py-1 bg-amber-50 rounded hover:bg-amber-100">Mod.</button>' +
    59	        '<button onclick="openSupprimerMouvementModal(' + x.id + ')" class="text-red-600 text-xs font-semibold px-2 py-1 bg-red-50 rounded hover:bg-red-100">Suppr.</button>' +
    60	        '</div></td></tr>';
    61	    }).join('') : '<tr><td colspan="8" class="text-center text-muted py-4">Aucun mouvement trouve</td></tr>') +
    62	    '</tbody></table></div>';
    63	}

    77	function submitModifierMouvement(e) {
    78	    e.preventDefault();

    83	            quantite: parseFloat(document.getElementById('modif-quantite').value),
    84	            prix_unitaire: parseFloat(document.getElementById('modif-prix').value),
    85	            fournisseur: document.getElementById('modif-fournisseur').value
    86	        }
    87	    }).then(function() {
    88	        closeModifierMouvementModal();
    89	        loadPage('stocks');
    90	    });
    91	}

     1	function loadSuivi(c){apiFetch('/suivi').then(function(d){function f(v){return new Intl.NumberFormat('fr-FR',{style:'currency',currency:'XOF',maximumFractionDigits:0}).format(v);}var s=d.stats;var taux=s.montantAttendu>0?Math.round((s.montantPaye/s.montantAttendu)*100):0;c.innerHTML='<div class="fade-in space-y-6"><h3 class="text-xl font-bold">Suivi Quotidien</h3><div class="kpi-grid"><div class="kpi-card" style="border-left:3px solid #0066ff"><div class="kpi-label">Attendu</div><div class="kpi-value">'+f(s.montantAttendu)+'</div></div><div class="kpi-card" style="border-left:3px solid #00c853"><div class="kpi-label">Encaisse</div><div class="kpi-value">'+f(s.montantPaye)+'</div></div><div class="kpi-card" style="border-left:3px solid #f59e0b"><div class="kpi-label">Restant</div><div class="kpi-value">'+f(s.montantAttendu-s.montantPaye)+'</div></div><div class="kpi-card" style="border-left:3px solid #8b5cf6"><div class="kpi-label">Taux du jour</div><div class="kpi-value">'+taux+'%</div></div></div><div class="panel"><h3>Performance Agents du jour</h3><div class="table-wrapper"><table><thead><tr><th>Agent</th><th>Echeances</th><th>Attendu</th><th>Paye</th><th>Taux</th></tr></thead><tbody>'+d.agents.map(function(a){return '<tr><td class="font-medium">'+a.nom+'</td><td>'+a.nb_echeances+'</td><td>'+f(a.montant_attendu)+'</td><td>'+f(a.montant_paye)+'</td><td><span class="badge '+(a.taux>=50?'badge-success':'badge-danger')+'">'+a.taux+'%</span></td></tr>';}).join('')+'</tbody></table></div></div></div>';});}
===== public/dashboard/chef/js/tournees-functions.js =====
     1	// === PAGE TOURNÉES ===
     2	function loadTournees(c) {
     3	    apiFetch('/tournees').then(function(list) {
     4	        if (!Array.isArray(list)) list = [];
     5	        const rows = list.map(function(t) {
     6	            const agent = t.agent ? (t.agent.nom + ' ' + t.agent.prenom) : '-';
     7	            const debut = t.heure_demarrage ? new Date(t.heure_demarrage).toLocaleTimeString('fr-FR') : '-';
     8	            const fin = t.heure_fin ? new Date(t.heure_fin).toLocaleTimeString('fr-FR') : '-';
     9	            let duree = '-';
    10	            if (t.heure_demarrage && t.heure_fin) {
    11	                const ms = new Date(t.heure_fin) - new Date(t.heure_demarrage);
    12	                const mins = Math.round(ms / 60000);
    13	                duree = Math.floor(mins / 60) + 'h ' + (mins % 60) + 'm';
    14	            }
    15	            const statut = t.statut === 'en_cours' ? '<span class="badge badge-info">En cours</span>' : '<span class="badge badge-success">Terminée</span>';
    16	            return `<tr>
    17	                <td>${agent}</td>
    18	                <td>${t.date_tournee}</td>
    19	                <td>${debut}</td>
    20	                <td>${fin}</td>
    21	                <td>${duree}</td>
    22	                <td>${statut}</td>
    23	            </tr>`;
    24	        }).join('');
    25	        c.innerHTML = `<div class="fade-in space-y-6">
    26	            <h3 class="text-xl font-bold">Tournées des agents</h3>
    27	            <div class="panel">
    28	                <div class="table-wrapper">
    29	                    <table>
    30	                        <thead><tr><th>Agent</th><th>Date</th><th>Démarrage</th><th>Fin</th><th>Durée</th><th>Statut</th></tr></thead>
    31	                        <tbody>${rows || '<tr><td colspan="6" class="text-center">Aucune tournée</td></tr>'}</tbody>
    32	                    </table>
    33	                </div>
    34	            </div>
    35	        </div>`;
    36	    }).catch(function() {
    37	        c.innerHTML = '<div class="panel text-center py-12"><p class="text-muted">Erreur chargement.</p></div>';
    38	    });
    39	}

     1	function showVenteComptantModal(){

     5	    var dIn=document.getElementById('vc-date'); if(dIn){dIn.value=todayStr; dIn.max=todayStr;}
     6	document.getElementById('vente-comptant-modal').classList.remove('hidden');apiFetch('/produits').then(function(p){document.getElementById('vc-produit').innerHTML='<option value="">-- Produit (optionnel) --</option>'+p.map(function(x){return '<option value="'+x.uuid+'" data-prix="'+x.prix_vente+'">'+x.nom+' - '+new Intl.NumberFormat('fr-FR',{style:'currency',currency:'XOF',maximumFractionDigits:0}).format(x.prix_vente)+'</option>';}).join('');});}

     9	function submitVenteComptant(e){e.preventDefault();var data={nom:document.getElementById('vc-nom').value,telephone:document.getElementById('vc-telephone').value,produit_id:document.getElementById('vc-produit').value||null,montant:parseFloat(document.getElementById('vc-montant').value),mode_paiement:document.getElementById('vc-mode').value};apiFetch('/chef-agence/vente-comptant',{method:'POST',body:JSON.stringify(data)}).then(function(r){if(r.success){closeVenteComptantModal();var confElem=document.getElementById('conf-journalier');if(confElem)confElem.parentElement.style.display='none';var modalElem=document.getElementById('confirmation-vente-modal');if(modalElem)modalElem.classList.remove('hidden');if(typeof loadDashboard==='function')loadDashboard();}});}
    10	function showVenteCreditModal(){
    11	    var todayStr = new Date().toISOString().split('T')[0]; var dIn = document.getElementById('vcr-date-achat'); if(dIn){ dIn.value = todayStr; dIn.readOnly = true; }
    12	    if(typeof vcrPanier !== 'undefined') { vcrPanier = []; renderPanier(); }
    13	
    14	    var todayStr = new Date().toISOString().split('T')[0];
    15	    var dIn=document.getElementById('vcr-date-achat'); if(dIn){dIn.value=todayStr; dIn.max=todayStr;}
    16	
    17	    setTimeout(function(){
    18	        var debSelect=document.getElementById("vcr-debiteur");
    19	        if(debSelect) debSelect.onchange=onDebiteurChangeCredit;
    20	    }, 200);document.getElementById('vente-credit-modal').classList.remove('hidden');apiFetch('/debiteurs').then(function(d){document.getElementById('vcr-debiteur').innerHTML='<option value="">-- Debiteur --</option>'+d.map(function(x){return '<option value="'+x.uuid+'">'+x.nom+' '+(x.prenom||'')+'</option>';}).join('');});apiFetch('/produits').then(function(p){document.getElementById('vcr-produit').innerHTML='<option value="">-- Produit (optionnel) --</option>'+p.map(function(x){return '<option value="'+x.uuid+'" data-prix="'+x.prix_vente+'">'+x.nom+' - '+new Intl.NumberFormat('fr-FR',{style:'currency',currency:'XOF',maximumFractionDigits:0}).format(x.prix_vente)+'</option>';}).join('');});}
    21	function closeVenteCreditModal(){document.getElementById('vente-credit-modal').classList.add('hidden');}
    22	function onProduitChangeCredit(){var s=document.getElementById('vcr-produit');var opt=s.options[s.selectedIndex];var p=opt.getAttribute('data-prix');if(p){document.getElementById('vcr-montant').value=p;document.getElementById('vcr-prix-vente').value=p;}calculerJournalier();}
    23	function calculerJournalier(){var m=parseFloat(document.getElementById('vcr-montant').value)||0;var e=parseFloat(document.getElementById('vcr-epargne').value)||0;var j=parseInt(document.getElementById('vcr-jours').value)||20;var epargneTotal=e*j;var total=m+epargneTotal;var jr=Math.round(total/j);document.getElementById('vcr-journalier').textContent=new Intl.NumberFormat('fr-FR',{style:'currency',currency:'XOF',maximumFractionDigits:0}).format(jr);var recap=document.getElementById('vcr-recap');if(recap){recap.classList.remove('hidden');document.getElementById('vcr-recap-epargne').textContent=new Intl.NumberFormat('fr-FR',{style:'currency',currency:'XOF',maximumFractionDigits:0}).format(epargneTotal);document.getElementById('vcr-recap-total').textContent=new Intl.NumberFormat('fr-FR',{style:'currency',currency:'XOF',maximumFractionDigits:0}).format(total);}}

    27	function submitVenteCredit(e){e.preventDefault();var motifEl=document.getElementById('vcr-motif');var data={debiteur_id:document.getElementById('vcr-debiteur').value,produit_id:document.getElementById('vcr-produit').value||null,montant:parseFloat(document.getElementById('vcr-montant').value),epargne_par_jour:parseFloat(document.getElementById('vcr-epargne').value)||300,jours:parseInt(document.getElementById('vcr-jours').value),penalite_par_jour:parseFloat(document.getElementById('vcr-penalite').value)||1000,motif_derogation:motifEl?motifEl.value.trim():'',produits:(typeof vcrPanier!=='undefined'&&vcrPanier.length>0)?vcrPanier.map(function(p){return {produit_id:p.produit_id,quantite:1};}):null};apiFetch('/chef-agence/vente-credit',{method:'POST',body:JSON.stringify(data)}).then(function(r){if(r.error){afficherErreur(r.error, document.getElementById('vcr-debiteur').value);return;}closeVenteCreditModal();loadPage('agent-space');if(r.success){closeVenteCreditModal();window.venteEnCours = {vente_id: r.vente_id || r.uuid, journalier: r.journalier || r.montant_journalier};

    31	if (modalElem) modalElem.classList.remove('hidden');}});}

    36	        if (!r.success) return showAlert('Une erreur est survenue. Veuillez réessayer.');

#000;padding-top:5px;text-align:center}</style></head><body><h1>SOUROUGNON</h1><h2>Contrat de Vente a Credit N°' + c.numero + '</h2><p>Date : ' + c.date + '</p><table><tr><th>Debiteur</th><td>' + specifie') + '</td></tr><tr><th>Prix de vente</th><td>' + new Intl.NumberFormat("fr-FR",{style:"currency",currency:"XOF"}).format(c.prix_vente) + '</td></tr><tr><th>Epargne/jour</th><td>' + new Intl.NumberFormat("fr-FR",{style:"currency",currency:"XOF"}).format(c.montant_journalier) + '</strong></td></tr><tr><th>Duree</th><td>' + c.nombre_jours + ' jours</td></tr><tr><th>Penalite/jour</th><td>' + new Intl.NumberFormat("fr-FR",{style:"currency",currency:"XOF"}).format(c.penalite_par_jour) + '</td></tr><tr><th>Date fin</th><td>' + c.date_fin + '</td></tr><tr><th>Agent</th><td>' + c.agent_nom + '</td></tr></table><div class="signature"><div>La Gerante</div><div>Le Client<br>(lu et approuve)</div></div></body></html>';
    39	        var w = window.open('', '_blank');
    40	        w.document.write(html);
    41	        w.document.close();
    42	        setTimeout(function() { w.print(); }, 500);

    47	function onDebiteurChangeCredit() {
    48	    var debiteurId = document.getElementById('vcr-debiteur').value;
    49	    var scoreBadge = document.getElementById('vcr-score-badge');
    50	    var motifField = document.getElementById('vcr-motif');
    51	    
    52	    if (!debiteurId) {
    53	        if (scoreBadge) scoreBadge.innerHTML = '';
    54	        if (motifField) motifField.required = false;
    55	        return;
    56	    }
    57	    
    58	    apiFetch('/debiteurs/' + debiteurId + '/score').then(function(r) {
    59	        if (scoreBadge && r.score !== undefined) {
    60	            var color = r.score >= 80 ? '#00c853' : r.score >= 60 ? '#0066ff' : r.score >= 40 ? '#f59e0b' : '#ef4444';
    61	            scoreBadge.innerHTML = '<span class="badge" style="background:'+color+'20;color:'+color+';font-weight:bold;font-size:12px;padding:4px 8px;border-radius:6px;display:inline-block;">Score: ' + r.score + '% (' + (r.label || '') + ')</span>';
    62	            
    63	            // Si le score est < 40, le motif devient obligatoire
    64	            if (motifField) {
    65	                if (r.score < 40) {
    66	                    motifField.required = true;
    67	                    motifField.placeholder = 'MOTIF OBLIGATOIRE - Score insuffisant (' + r.score + '%)';
    68	                    motifField.classList.add('border-red-500');
    69	                } else {
    70	                    motifField.required = false;
    71	                    motifField.placeholder = 'Ex: Client fidèle, situation exceptionnelle...';
    72	                    motifField.classList.remove('border-red-500');
    73	                }
    74	            }
    75	        }
    76	    }).catch(function() {
    77	        if (scoreBadge) scoreBadge.innerHTML = '';
    78	    });
    79	}
    80	
    81	function fermerConfirmationVente() {
    82	    var modalElem = document.getElementById('confirmation-vente-modal');
    83	    if (modalElem) modalElem.classList.add('hidden');
    84	    window.venteEnCours = null;
    85	    if (typeof loadDashboard === 'function') loadDashboard();
    86	    else if (typeof loadPage === 'function') loadPage('chef-agence');
    87	}

    89	function telechargerContratDirect() {
    90	    if (!window.venteEnCours || !window.venteEnCours.vente_id) return;
    91	    var venteId = window.venteEnCours.vente_id;
    92	    apiFetch('/ventes/' + venteId + '/contrat').then(function(r) {
    93	        if (!r || !r.success) return;
    94	        var c = r.contrat;
    95	        var html = '<html><head><meta charset="utf-8"><title>Contrat ' + c.numero + '</title><style>body{font-family:sans-serif;max-width:700px;margin:40px auto;padding:20px;line-height:1.6}h1{text-align:center;color:#059669}h2{color:#333}table{width:100%;border-collapse:collapse;margin:20px 0}td,th{border:1px solid #ddd;padding:8px;text-align:left}th{background:#f8fafc}.signature{margin-top:60px;display:flex;justify-content:space-between}.signature div{width:200px;border-top:1px solid #000;padding-top:5px;text-align:center}</style></head><body><h1>SOUROUGNON</h1><h2>Contrat de Vente à Crédit N°' + c.numero + '</h2><p>Date : ' + c.date + '</p><table><tr><th>Débiteurs</th><td>' + c.debiteur_nom + '</td></tr><tr><th>Téléphone</th><td>' + c.debiteur_tel + '</td></tr><tr><th>Quartier</th><td>' + c.debiteur_quartier + '</td></tr><tr><th>Produit</th><td>' + (c.produit || 'Non spécifié') + '</td></tr><tr><th>Total à rembourser</th><td><strong>' + new Intl.NumberFormat("fr-FR",{style:"currency",currency:"XOF"}).format(c.montant_total) + '</strong></td></tr><tr><th>Journalier</th><td><strong>' + new Intl.NumberFormat("fr-FR",{style:"currency",currency:"XOF"}).format(c.montant_journalier) + '</strong></td></tr><tr><th>Durée</th><td>' + c.nombre_jours + ' jours</td></tr><tr><th>Date fin</th><td>' + c.date_fin + '</td></tr></table><div class="signature"><div>La Gérante</div><div>Le Client<br>(lu et approuvé)</div></div></body></html>';
    96	        var blob = new Blob([html], {type: 'text/html'});
    97	        var url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
    98	        var a = document.createElement('a');
    99	        a.href = url;
   100	        a.download = 'Contrat_' + c.numero + '.html';
   101	        document.body.appendChild(a);
   102	        a.click();
   103	        document.body.removeChild(a);
   104	        URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
   105	        fermerConfirmationVente();
   106	    });
   107	}
   108	
   109	function afficherErreur(message, debiteurId){
   110	    var msgEl=document.getElementById('erreur-message');
   111	    if(msgEl)msgEl.textContent=message;
   112	    var actionsEl=document.getElementById('erreur-actions');
   113	    if(actionsEl)actionsEl.innerHTML='';
   114	    
   115	    // Détecter le cas "crédit en cours" pour proposer l'augmentation directe
   116	    if(message.indexOf('crédit(s) en cours') > -1 || message.indexOf('credit(s) en cours') > -1){
   117	        var maxMatch = message.match(/maximum autorisé : (\d+)/);
   118	        var currentMatch = message.match(/déjà (\d+) crédit/);
   119	        var debiteur = debiteurId || window.debiteurSelectionne || document.getElementById('vcr-debiteur')?.value;
   120	        
   121	        if(actionsEl && debiteur){
   122	            var nouveauMax = maxMatch ? parseInt(maxMatch[1]) + 1 : 2;
   123	            actionsEl.innerHTML = '<button onclick="augmenterCreditsAutorises(\''+debiteur+'\','+nouveauMax+')" class="w-full py-2.5 px-4 bg-emerald-600 hover:bg-emerald-700 text-white rounded-xl text-sm font-semibold transition-colors mb-2">✅ Autoriser '+nouveauMax+' crédits maintenant</button>';
   124	        }
   125	    }
   126	    
   127	    var modalEl=document.getElementById('erreur-modal');
   128	    if(modalEl)modalEl.classList.remove('hidden');
   129	}
   130	
   131	function augmenterCreditsAutorises(debiteurId, nouveauNombre){
   132	    apiFetch('/debiteurs/'+debiteurId, {method:'PUT', body:JSON.stringify({credits_autorises:nouveauNombre})}).then(function(r){
   133	        if(r.success){
   134	            fermerErreurModal();
   135	            // Recharger la page des débiteurs ou rester sur la vente
   136	            if(typeof loadPage === 'function') loadPage('debiteurs');
   137	        } else {
   138	            afficherErreur('Impossible d\'augmenter les crédits. Essayez via la fiche débiteur.');
   139	        }
   140	    });
   141	}
   142	function fermerErreurModal(){var modalEl=document.getElementById('erreur-modal');if(modalEl)modalEl.classList.add('hidden');}

   145	var vcrPanier = [];
   146	
   147	function ajouterAuPanier() {
   148	    var select = document.getElementById('vcr-produit');
   149	    if (!select || !select.value) return;
   150	    
   151	    var opt = select.options[select.selectedIndex];
   152	    var produitId = select.value;
   153	    var nom = opt.text.split(' - ')[0] || 'Produit';
   154	    var prix = parseFloat(opt.getAttribute('data-prix')) || 0;
   155	    
   156	    vcrPanier.push({produit_id: produitId, nom: nom, prix: prix});
   157	    renderPanierUI();
   158	}
   159	
   160	function retirerDuPanier(index) {
   161	    vcrPanier.splice(index, 1);
   162	    renderPanierUI();
   163	}
   164	
   165	function renderPanierUI() {
   166	    var itemsEl = document.getElementById('vcr-panier-items');
   167	    var countEl = document.getElementById('vcr-panier-count');
   168	    if (countEl) countEl.textContent = vcrPanier.length;
   169	    if (!itemsEl) return;
   170	    
   171	    if (vcrPanier.length === 0) {
   172	        itemsEl.innerHTML = '<p class="text-xs text-slate-400 italic">Aucun produit dans le panier</p>';
   173	        document.getElementById('vcr-montant').value = '';
   174	        document.getElementById('vcr-prix-vente').value = '';
   175	        if (typeof calculerJournalier === 'function') calculerJournalier();
   176	        return;
   177	    }
   178	    
   179	    var total = vcrPanier.reduce(function(sum, item) { return sum + item.prix; }, 0);
   180	    
   181	    itemsEl.innerHTML = vcrPanier.map(function(item, idx) {
   182	        return '<div class="flex justify-between items-center p-2 bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-lg text-xs shadow-sm">' +
   183	               '<span class="font-medium text-slate-800">' + item.nom + '</span>' +
   184	               '<div class="flex items-center gap-2">' +
   185	               '<span class="font-bold">' + new Intl.NumberFormat('fr-FR', {style: 'currency', currency: 'XOF'}).format(item.prix) + '</span>' +
   186	               '<button type="button" onclick="retirerDuPanier(' + idx + ')" class="px-2 py-1 bg-rose-50 hover:bg-rose-100 text-rose-600 rounded font-semibold text-xs">✕ Retirer</button>' +
   187	               '</div></div>';
   188	    }).join('');
   189	    
   190	    document.getElementById('vcr-montant').value = total;
   191	    document.getElementById('vcr-prix-vente').value = total;
   192	    if (typeof calculerJournalier === 'function') calculerJournalier();
   193	}

     1	function loadVentes(c){apiFetch('/ventes').then(function(v){function f(x){return new Intl.NumberFormat('fr-FR',{style:'currency',currency:'XOF',maximumFractionDigits:0}).format(x);}c.innerHTML='<div class="fade-in space-y-6"><div class="flex items-center justify-between"><h3 class="text-xl font-bold">Ventes</h3></div><div class="panel"><div class="table-wrapper"><table><thead><tr><th>N</th><th>Debiteur</th><th>Type</th><th>Montant</th><th>Journalier</th><th>Restant</th><th>Statut</th><th>Penalite</th><th>Actions</th></tr></thead><tbody>'+(v.length>0?v.map(function(x){return '<tr><td class="font-mono text-xs">'+x.uuid.substring(0,8)+'</td><td class="font-medium">'+(x.debiteur?x.debiteur.nom+' '+(x.debiteur.prenom||''):'--')+'</td><td><span class="badge '+(x.type_vente==="comptant"?"badge-info":"badge-warning")+'">'+(x.type_vente==="comptant"?"Comptant":"Credit")+'</span></td><td class="font-semibold">'+f(x.montant_total)+'</td><td>'+f(x.montant_journalier)+'</td><td>'+f(x.montant_total-(x.total_paye||0))+'</td><td><span class="badge '+(x.statut==="en_cours"?"badge-success":x.statut==="termine"?"badge-info":"badge-danger")+'">'+x.statut+'</span></td><td>'+(x.statut==="en_cours"?'<button onclick="annulerVente(\''+x.uuid+'\')" class="text-red-600 text-xs">Annuler</button>':'--')+'</td></tr>';}).join(''):'<tr><td colspan="9" class="text-center text-muted py-4">Aucune vente</td></tr>')+'</tbody></table></div></div></div>';});}
     2	async function annulerVente(u){if(!(await showConfirm('Annuler cette vente ?')))return;apiFetch('/ventes/'+u+'/annuler',{method:'PUT'}).then(function(){loadPage('ventes');});}

     4	function openPenaliteModal(uuid, debiteur, montant) {
     5	    document.getElementById('pen-vente-id').value = uuid;
     6	    document.getElementById('pen-debiteur').textContent = debiteur;
     7	    document.getElementById('pen-montant').textContent = new Intl.NumberFormat('fr-FR').format(montant || 1000);
     8	    document.getElementById('pen-nouveau-montant').value = montant || 1000;
     9	    document.getElementById('pen-motif').value = '';
    10	    document.getElementById('penalite-modal').classList.remove('hidden');
    11	}
    12	function closePenaliteModal() {
    13	    document.getElementById('penalite-modal').classList.add('hidden');
    14	}
    15	function submitPenalite(e) {
    16	    e.preventDefault();
    17	    var data = {
    18	        penalite_par_jour: parseFloat(document.getElementById('pen-nouveau-montant').value) || 0,
    19	        penalite_active: (parseFloat(document.getElementById('pen-nouveau-montant').value) > 0),
    20	        motif_penalite: document.getElementById('pen-motif').value
    21	    };
    22	    var uuid = document.getElementById('pen-vente-id').value;
    23	    apiFetch('/ventes/' + uuid, {method:'PUT', body:JSON.stringify(data)}).then(function(r) {
    24	        if (r.success) {
    25	            closePenaliteModal();
    26	            showAlert('Pénalité mise à jour avec succès.');
    27	            loadPage('ventes');
    28	        }
    29	    });
    30	}

     3	function loadAgentSpace(c){apiFetch('/agent-space/tournee').then(function(d){function f(v){return new Intl.NumberFormat('fr-FR',{style:'currency',currency:'XOF',maximumFractionDigits:0}).format(v||0);}var s=d.stats;var tabs=['a_visiter','paye','absent','refus','promesse'];var labels={'a_visiter':'A visiter','paye':'Paye','absent':'Absent','refus':'Refus','promesse':'Promesse'};var currentTab='a_visiter';function renderTab(tab){var list=d[tab]||[];return list.map(function(v){var badgeRetard=v.statut_global==='en_retard'?'<span class=\"badge badge-danger\">En retard</span>':'<span class=\"badge badge-success\">A jour</span>';var onclick='openEncaisserModal(&#39;'+v.uuid+'&#39;,&#39;'+v.debiteur.replace(/'/g,'')+'&#39;,&#39;'+(v.quartier||'').replace(/'/g,'')+'&#39;,&#39;'+(v.telephone||'').replace(/'/g,'')+'&#39;,'+v.montant_journalier+','+v.reste_a_payer+',&#39;'+v.statut_global+'&#39;,&#39;'+v.statut_visite+'&#39;)';return '<tr><td class=\"font-medium\">'+v.debiteur+'</td><td>'+(v.quartier||'-')+'</td><td>'+(v.telephone||'-')+'</td><td>'+f(v.montant_journalier)+'</td><td>'+f(v.reste_a_payer)+'</td><td>'+(v.jours_payes||0)+'/'+(v.jours_total||0)+' j</td><td>'+badgeRetard+'</td><td><button onclick=\"'+onclick+'\" class=\"bg-green-600 text-white px-3 py-1 rounded-lg text-xs font-semibold hover:bg-green-700\">Encaisser</button></td></tr>';}).join('');}c.innerHTML='<div class=\"fade-in space-y-6\"><h3 class=\"text-xl font-bold\">Mon Espace Agent</h3><div class=\"kpi-grid\"><div class=\"kpi-card\" style=\"border-left:3px solid #0066ff\"><div class=\"kpi-label\">Total</div><div class=\"kpi-value\">'+s.nb_total+'</div></div><div class=\"kpi-card\" style=\"border-left:3px solid #00c853\"><div class=\"kpi-label\">Paye</div><div class=\"kpi-value\">'+s.nb_paye+'</div></div><div class=\"kpi-card\" style=\"border-left:3px solid #ef4444\"><div class=\"kpi-label\">Absent</div><div class=\"kpi-value\">'+s.nb_absent+'</div></div><div class=\"kpi-card\" style=\"border-left:3px solid #f59e0b\"><div class=\"kpi-label\">Attendu</div><div class=\"kpi-value\">'+f(s.montant_attendu)+'</div></div></div><div class=\"flex gap-2 overflow-x-auto pb-2\">'+tabs.map(function(t){return '<button onclick=\"switchAgentTab(&#39;'+t+'&#39;)\" class=\"agent-tab px-4 py-2 rounded-lg text-sm font-semibold whitespace-nowrap '+(t===currentTab?'bg-accent text-white':'bg-slate-200 text-slate-700')+'\" data-tab=\"'+t+'\">'+labels[t]+' ('+(d[t]||[]).length+')</button>';}).join('')+'</div><div class=\"panel\"><div class=\"table-wrapper\"><table><thead><tr><th>Debiteur</th><th>Quartier</th><th>Tel</th><th>Journalier</th><th>Restant</th><th>Jours</th><th>Statut</th><th>Action</th></tr></thead><tbody id=\"agent-table-body\">'+renderTab(currentTab)+'</tbody></table></div></div></div>';window.switchAgentTab=function(tab){currentTab=tab;document.querySelectorAll('.agent-tab').forEach(function(b){b.className='agent-tab px-4 py-2 rounded-lg text-sm font-semibold whitespace-nowrap '+(b.getAttribute('data-tab')===tab?'bg-accent text-white':'bg-slate-200 text-slate-700');});document.getElementById('agent-table-body').innerHTML=renderTab(tab);};}).catch(function(){c.innerHTML='<div class=\"panel text-center py-12\"><p class=\"text-muted\">Erreur chargement.</p></div>';});}

     1	function loadAlertes(c){apiFetch('/alertes').then(function(a){var nbNonLues=a.filter(function(x){return!x.lue;}).length;var badge=document.getElementById('alert-badge');if(badge)badge.classList.toggle('hidden',nbNonLues===0);var typeBadge={retard:'badge-danger',echeance_jour:'badge-info',stock_faible:'badge-warning',credit_finissant:'badge-warning'};var niveauBadge={info:'badge-info',avertissement:'badge-warning',critique:'badge-danger'};c.innerHTML='<div class="fade-in space-y-6"><div class="flex items-center justify-between"><h3 class="text-xl font-bold">Alertes</h3><button onclick="marquerToutesLues()" class="bg-slate-200 text-slate-700 px-4 py-2 rounded-lg text-sm font-semibold hover:bg-slate-300">Tout marquer lu</button></div><div class="panel"><div class="table-wrapper"><table><thead><tr><th>Date</th><th>Type</th><th>Niveau</th><th>Message</th><th>Etat</th></tr></thead><tbody>'+(a.length>0?a.map(function(x){return '<tr class="'+(x.lue?'':'bg-amber-50')+'"><td class="text-xs">'+x.date_alerte.substring(0,10)+'</td><td><span class="badge '+(typeBadge[x.type]||'badge-info')+'">'+x.type+'</span></td><td><span class="badge '+(niveauBadge[x.niveau]||'badge-info')+'">'+x.niveau+'</span></td><td>'+x.message+'</td><td>'+(x.lue?'<span class="text-green-600 text-xs">Lu</span>':'<span class="text-amber-600 text-xs font-semibold">Nouveau</span>')+'</td></tr>';}).join(''):'<tr><td colspan="5" class="text-center text-muted py-4">Aucune alerte</td></tr>')+'</tbody></table></div></div></div>';});}

    12	function esc(s) { var d = document.createElement('div'); d.textContent = s == null ? '' : String(s); return d.innerHTML; }

    14	function loadAlertesGroupees(c) {
    15	    apiFetch('/alertes').then(function(a) {
    16	        var groupes = { retard: [], echeance_jour: [], stock_faible: [], autre: [] };
    17	        a.forEach(function(x) { (groupes[x.type] || groupes.autre).push(x); });
    18	        
    19	        var niv = { info: 'badge-info', avertissement: 'badge-warning', critique: 'badge-danger' };
    20	        
    21	        function tableau(titre, lignes) {
    22	            if (!lignes.length) return '';
    23	            return '<div class="panel mb-4"><h3 class="text-lg font-semibold mb-2">' + esc(titre) + ' (' + lignes.length + ')</h3>' +
    24	                '<div class="table-wrapper" style="max-height:400px;overflow:auto"><table><thead><tr>' +
    25	                '<th>Date</th><th>Niveau</th><th>Message</th><th>Etat</th></tr></thead><tbody>' +
    26	                lignes.map(function(x) {
    27	                    return '<tr>' +
    28	                        '<td class="text-xs">' + esc((x.date_alerte || '').substring(0, 10)) + '</td>' +
    29	                        '<td><span class="badge ' + (niv[x.niveau] || 'badge-info') + '">' + esc(x.niveau) + '</span></td>' +
    30	                        '<td>' + esc(x.message) + '</td>' +
    31	                        '<td>' + (x.lue ? '<span class="text-green-600 text-xs">Lu</span>' : '<span class="text-amber-600 text-xs font-semibold">Nouveau</span>') + '</td></tr>';
    32	                }).join('') + '</tbody></table></div></div>';
    33	        }
    34	        
    35	        c.innerHTML = '<div class="fade-in space-y-4">' +
    36	            '<div class="flex items-center justify-between"><h3 class="text-xl font-bold">Alertes</h3>' +
    37	            '<button onclick="marquerToutesLues()" class="bg-slate-200 text-slate-700 px-4 py-2 rounded-lg text-sm font-semibold">Tout marquer lu</button></div>' +
    38	            tableau('Retards de paiement', groupes.retard) +
    39	            tableau('Échéances du jour', groupes.echeance_jour) +
    40	            tableau('Stock faible', groupes.stock_faible) +
    41	            tableau('Autres', groupes.autre) +
    42	            '</div>';
    43	    }).catch(function(e) {
    44	        c.innerHTML = '<div class="panel text-center py-12"><p class="text-muted">Erreur : ' + esc(e.message) + '</p></div>';
    45	    });
    46	}

     5	var currentUser = {};

    10	try { authToken = localStorage.getItem('sourougnon_token') || ''; } catch (e) {}
    11	try { var b = localStorage.getItem('sourougnon_user'); currentUser = b ? JSON.parse(b) : {}; } catch (e) { localStorage.removeItem('sourougnon_user'); currentUser = {}; }
    12	if (!authToken) window.location.href = '/dashboard/login.html';

    16	    if (currentUser && currentUser.nom) {
    17	        S('user-name', currentUser.nom);
    18	        S('user-initials', (currentUser.prenom || currentUser.nom).charAt(0).toUpperCase());
    19	        var g = document.getElementById('greeting'); if (g) g.innerHTML = 'Bonjour ' + (currentUser.prenom || currentUser.nom);

    35	function toggleSidebar() { var s = document.getElementById('sidebar'); var o = document.getElementById('sidebar-overlay'); if (s) s.classList.toggle('-translate-x-full'); if (o) o.classList.toggle('hidden'); }

    37	function apiFetch(endpoint, options) {
    38	    options = options || {};
    39	    options.headers = Object.assign({
    40	        'Authorization': 'Bearer ' + authToken,
    41	        'Content-Type': 'application/json',
    42	        'Accept': 'application/json'
    43	    }, options.headers || {});
    44	
    45	    if (options.body && typeof options.body === 'object') {
    46	        options.body = JSON.stringify(options.body);
    47	    }
    48	
    49	    return fetch(API_BASE + endpoint, options).then(function (r) {
    50	        if (r.status === 401) {
    51	            localStorage.clear();
    52	            window.location.href = '/dashboard/login.html';
    53	            return Promise.reject(new Error('Session expirée'));
    54	        }
    55	        return r.text().then(function (txt) {
    56	            var data;
    57	            try { data = txt ? JSON.parse(txt) : {}; }
    58	            catch (e) {
    59	                console.error('Réponse non-JSON', r.status, txt.slice(0, 500));
    60	                return Promise.reject(new Error('Erreur serveur ' + r.status));
    61	            }
    62	            if (!r.ok) {
    63	                var msg = data.message || data.error ||
    64	                    (data.errors ? Object.values(data.errors).flat().join('\n') : 'Erreur ' + r.status);
    65	                return Promise.reject(Object.assign(new Error(msg), {status: r.status, data: data}));
    66	            }
    67	            return data;
    68	        });
    69	    });
    70	}
    71

    74	async function apiDownload(endpoint, filename) {
    75	    try {
    76	        const res = await fetch(API_BASE + endpoint, {
    77	            headers: {'Authorization': 'Bearer ' + authToken}
    78	        });
    79	        if (!res.ok) throw new Error('Erreur ' + res.status);
    80	        const blob = await res.blob();
    81	        const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
    82	        const a = document.createElement('a');
    83	        a.href = url;
    84	        a.download = filename;
    85	        document.body.appendChild(a);
    86	        a.click();
    87	        a.remove();
    88	        URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
    89	    } catch (e) {
    90	        showAlert('Erreur export : ' + e.message);
    91	    }
    92	}
    93	function showAlert(message) {
    94	    return new Promise(function(resolve) {
    95	        var overlay = document.createElement('div');
    96	        overlay.className = 'fixed inset-0 z-[100] flex items-center justify-center bg-black/50 p-4';
    97	        overlay.innerHTML = '<div class="bg-white rounded-2xl p-6 w-full max-w-sm shadow-2xl"><p class="text-slate-800 text-sm mb-4"></p><button class="w-full py-2.5 bg-blue-600 text-white rounded-xl text-sm font-semibold">OK</button></div>';
    98	        document.body.appendChild(overlay);
    99	        overlay.querySelector('p').textContent = message;
   100	        overlay.querySelector('button').onclick = function(){ overlay.remove(); resolve(); };
   101	    });
   102	}

   105	function showPrompt(message, valeurParDefaut) {
   106	    return new Promise(function(resolve) {
   107	        var overlay = document.createElement('div');
   108	        overlay.className = 'fixed inset-0 z-[100] flex items-center justify-center bg-black/50 p-4';
   109	        overlay.innerHTML = '<div class="bg-white rounded-2xl p-6 w-full max-w-sm shadow-2xl"><p class="text-slate-800 text-sm mb-4"></p><input type="text" class="w-full px-4 py-2.5 border border-slate-200 rounded-xl text-sm mb-4" /><div class="flex gap-2"><button data-act="cancel" class="flex-1 py-2.5 bg-slate-200 text-slate-700 rounded-xl text-sm font-semibold">Annuler</button><button data-act="ok" class="flex-1 py-2.5 bg-blue-600 text-white rounded-xl text-sm font-semibold">Valider</button></div></div>';
   110	        document.body.appendChild(overlay);
   111	        overlay.querySelector('p').textContent = message;
   112	        var input = overlay.querySelector('input');
   113	        input.value = valeurParDefaut || '';
   114	        function close(val) { overlay.remove(); resolve(val); }
   115	        overlay.querySelector('[data-act="cancel"]').onclick = function(){ close(null); };
   116	        overlay.querySelector('[data-act="ok"]').onclick = function(){ close(input.value); };
   117	    });
   118	}
   119	function showConfirm(message) {
   120	    return new Promise(function(resolve) {
   121	        var overlay = document.createElement('div');
   122	        overlay.className = 'fixed inset-0 z-[100] flex items-center justify-center bg-black/50 p-4';
   123	        overlay.innerHTML = '<div class="bg-white rounded-2xl p-6 w-full max-w-sm shadow-2xl"><p class="text-slate-800 text-sm mb-4"></p><div class="flex gap-2"><button data-act="cancel" class="flex-1 py-2.5 bg-slate-200 text-slate-700 rounded-xl text-sm font-semibold">Annuler</button><button data-act="ok" class="flex-1 py-2.5 bg-blue-600 text-white rounded-xl text-sm font-semibold">Confirmer</button></div></div>';
   124	        document.body.appendChild(overlay);
   125	        overlay.querySelector('p').textContent = message;
   126	        function close(val) { overlay.remove(); resolve(val); }
   127	        overlay.querySelector('[data-act="cancel"]').onclick = function(){ close(false); };
   128	        overlay.querySelector('[data-act="ok"]').onclick = function(){ close(true); };
   129	    });
   130	}

   170	function confirmModal(message, opts) {
   171	    opts = opts || {};
   172	    return new Promise(function(resolve) {
   173	        var el = document.createElement('div');
   174	        el.className = 'fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-center justify-center bg-black/50';
   175	        el.innerHTML =
   176	            '<div class="bg-white rounded-2xl p-6 w-full max-w-sm shadow-2xl">' +
   177	            '<p class="text-sm mb-4"></p>' +
   178	            '<div class="flex gap-2">' +
   179	            '<button data-act="cancel" class="flex-1 px-4 py-2 bg-slate-200 rounded-xl text-sm font-semibold">Annuler</button>' +
   180	            '<button data-act="ok" class="flex-1 px-4 py-2 ' + (opts.danger === false ? 'bg-blue-600' : 'bg-red-600') + ' text-white rounded-xl text-sm font-semibold">Confirmer</button>' +
   181	            '</div></div>';
   182	        el.querySelector('p').textContent = message;
   183	        function close(v) { el.remove(); document.removeEventListener('keydown', onKey); resolve(v); }
   184	        function onKey(e) { if (e.key === 'Escape') close(false); }
   185	        el.addEventListener('click', function(e) {
   186	            var a = e.target.getAttribute && e.target.getAttribute('data-act');
   187	            if (a) close(a === 'ok');
   188	            else if (e.target === el) close(false);
   189	        });
   190	        document.addEventListener('keydown', onKey);
   191	        document.body.appendChild(el);
   192	        el.querySelector('[data-act="ok"]').focus();
   193	    });
   194	}
===== public/dashboard/js/benin-localisation.js =====
     1	(function () {
     2	    'use strict';
     3	
     4	    const SELECT_IDS = {
     5	        departement: 'deb-departement',
     6	        commune: 'deb-commune',
     7	        arrondissement: 'deb-arrondissement',
     8	        quartier: 'deb-village'
     9	    };

    12	
    13	    function getSelect(id) {
    14	        return document.getElementById(id);
    15	    }
    16	
    17	    function resetSelect(id, placeholder, disabled) {
    18	        const select = getSelect(id);
    19	        if (!select) return;
    20	        select.innerHTML = '';
    21	        select.appendChild(new Option(placeholder, ''));
    22	        select.disabled = Boolean(disabled);
    23	    }
    24	
    25	    function setLoading(id, placeholder) {
    26	        const select = getSelect(id);
    27	        if (!select) return;
    28	        select.innerHTML = '';
    29	        select.appendChild(new Option(placeholder, ''));
    30	        select.disabled = true;
    31	    }
    32	
    33	    async function chargerOptions(type, parent, selectId, placeholder) {
    34	        const select = getSelect(selectId);
    35	        if (!select) {
    36	            console.warn('[Localisation] Select introuvable : #' + selectId);
    37	            return;
    38	        }
    39	
    40	        setLoading(selectId, 'Chargement...');
    41	
    42	        const params = new URLSearchParams({ type: type });
    43	        if (parent) params.set('parent', parent);
    44	
    45	        try {
    46	            const response = await fetch(API_URL + '?' + params.toString(), {
    47	                headers: { 'Accept': 'application/json' },
    48	                cache: 'no-store'
    49	            });
    50	
    51	            const contentType = response.headers.get('content-type') || '';
    52	            const data = contentType.includes('application/json')
    53	                ? await response.json()
    54	                : null;
    55	
    56	            if (!response.ok) throw new Error('Erreur HTTP ' + response.status);
    57	            if (!Array.isArray(data)) throw new Error('Reponse non JSON');
    58	
    59	            select.innerHTML = '';
    60	            select.appendChild(new Option(placeholder, ''));
    61	            data.forEach(item => select.appendChild(new Option(item.nom, item.nom)));
    62	            select.disabled = false;
    63	            console.log('[Localisation]', type, ':', data.length, 'element(s) charge(s)');
    64	        } catch (error) {
    65	            console.error('[Localisation] Echec chargement', { type, parent, erreur: error });
    66	            select.innerHTML = '';
    67	            select.appendChild(new Option('Erreur de chargement', ''));
    68	            select.disabled = true;
    69	        }
    70	    }
    71	
    72	    function initSelects(root) {
    73	        const scope = root || document;
    74	        const dep = scope.querySelector('#' + SELECT_IDS.departement);
    75	        const com = scope.querySelector('#' + SELECT_IDS.commune);
    76	        const arr = scope.querySelector('#' + SELECT_IDS.arrondissement);
    77	        const quartier = scope.querySelector('#' + SELECT_IDS.quartier);
    78	
    79	        if (!dep) {
    80	            console.warn('[Localisation] #deb-departement introuvable');
    81	            return false;
    82	        }
    83	
    84	        if (dep.dataset.localisationReady === '1') return true;
    85	        dep.dataset.localisationReady = '1';
    86	
    87	        if (com) com.disabled = true;
    88	        if (arr) arr.disabled = true;
    89	        if (quartier) quartier.disabled = true;
    90	
    91	        chargerOptions('departement', '', SELECT_IDS.departement, '-- Departement --');
    92	
    93	        dep.addEventListener('change', function () {
    94	            resetSelect(SELECT_IDS.commune, '-- Commune --', true);
    95	            resetSelect(SELECT_IDS.arrondissement, '-- Arrondissement --', true);
    96	            resetSelect(SELECT_IDS.quartier, '-- Quartier --', true);
    97	            if (this.value) chargerOptions('commune', this.value, SELECT_IDS.commune, '-- Commune --');
    98	        });
    99	
   100	        if (com) com.addEventListener('change', function () {
   101	            resetSelect(SELECT_IDS.arrondissement, '-- Arrondissement --', true);
   102	            resetSelect(SELECT_IDS.quartier, '-- Quartier --', true);
   103	            if (this.value) chargerOptions('arrondissement', this.value, SELECT_IDS.arrondissement, '-- Arrondissement --');
   104	        });
   105	
   106	        if (arr) arr.addEventListener('change', function () {
   107	            resetSelect(SELECT_IDS.quartier, '-- Quartier --', true);
   108	            if (this.value) chargerOptions('quartier', this.value, SELECT_IDS.quartier, '-- Quartier --');
   109	        });
   110	
   111	        return true;
   112	    }
   113	
   114	    function initWhenAvailable() {
   115	        if (initSelects()) return;
   116	        const observer = new MutationObserver(function () {
   117	            if (initSelects()) observer.disconnect();
   118	        });
   119	        observer.observe(document.body, { childList: true, subtree: true });
   120	    }
   121	
   122	    window.initLocalisationBenin = initSelects;
   123	window.initSelects = initSelects;
   124	
   125	    if (document.readyState === 'loading') {
   126	        document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', initWhenAvailable);
   127	    } else {
   128	        initWhenAvailable();
   129	    }
   130	})();

    11	function renderCaisseCalendrier(c) {
    12	    const selector = `
    13	        <div class="flex gap-2 overflow-x-auto pb-2">
    14	            ${['jour','semaine','mois','annee'].map(p => `
    15	                <button class="chip ${p===currentCaissePeriod?'chip--active':''}" onclick="setCaissePeriod('${p}')">
    16	                    ${p.charAt(0).toUpperCase() + p.slice(1)}
    17	                </button>`).join('')}
    18	        </div>
    19	    `;
    20	
    21	    const nav = `
    22	        <div class="flex items-center justify-between mb-3">
    23	            <button class="text-btn" onclick="changeCaisseMonth(-1)">◀</button>
    24	            <h4 class="font-semibold">${new Intl.DateTimeFormat('fr-FR', {month:'long', year:'numeric'}).format(currentCaisseDate)}</h4>
    25	            <button class="text-btn" onclick="changeCaisseMonth(1)">▶</button>
    26	        </div>
    27	    `;
    28	
    29	    c.innerHTML = `<div class="fade-in space-y-6">
    30	        <div class="flex items-center justify-between">
    31	            <h3 class="text-xl font-bold">Caisse</h3>
    32	            <div class="flex gap-2">
    33	                <button onclick="showDecaissementModal()" class="bg-red-600 text-white px-4 py-2 rounded-lg text-sm font-semibold hover:bg-red-700">- Dépense</button>
    34	                <button onclick="showClotureModal()" class="bg-purple-600 text-white px-4 py-2 rounded-lg text-sm font-semibold hover:bg-purple-700">Clôturer</button>
    35	                <button onclick="exportCaissePeriode()" class="bg-slate-600 text-white px-4 py-2 rounded-lg text-sm font-semibold hover:bg-slate-700">Export CSV</button>
    36	            </div>
    37	        </div>
    38	        ${selector}
    39	        <div class="panel p-4">
    40	            ${nav}
    41	            <div id="caisse-calendrier" class="grid grid-cols-7 gap-1"></div>
    42	        </div>
    43	        <div id="caisse-kpis" class="grid grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-5 gap-3"></div>
    44	        <div id="remises-attente" class="panel p-4"></div>
    45	        <div class="panel">
    46	            <h4 class="font-semibold p-4 pb-0">Mouvements</h4>
    47	            <div class="table-wrapper" id="caisse-mouvements">
    48	                <p class="text-center text-muted py-4">Sélectionnez un jour</p>
    49	            </div>
    50	        </div>
    51	    </div>`;
    52	
    53	    drawCaisseMonth();
    54	    loadCaisseKPIs();
    55	}

    57	function setCaissePeriod(period) {
    58	    currentCaissePeriod = period;
    59	    document.querySelectorAll('.chip').forEach(ch => ch.classList.remove('chip--active'));
    60	    event.target.classList.add('chip--active');
    61	    renderCaisseCalendrier(document.getElementById('page-content'));
    62	    loadRemisesEnAttente();
    63	}

    65	function changeCaisseMonth(delta) {
    66	    currentCaisseDate.setMonth(currentCaisseDate.getMonth() + delta);
    67	    renderCaisseCalendrier(document.getElementById('page-content'));
    68	}
    69	
    70	function drawCaisseMonth() {
    71	    const year = currentCaisseDate.getFullYear();
    72	    const month = currentCaisseDate.getMonth();
    73	    const firstDay = new Date(year, month, 1);
    74	    const lastDay = new Date(year, month + 1, 0);
    75	    const startOffset = (firstDay.getDay() + 6) % 7;
    76	    const daysInMonth = lastDay.getDate();
    77	    const today = new Date();
    78	
    79	    const container = document.getElementById('caisse-calendrier');
    80	    container.innerHTML = '';
    81	
    82	    for (let i = 0; i < startOffset; i++) {
    83	        container.innerHTML += '<div></div>';
    84	    }
    85	
    86	    for (let d = 1; d <= daysInMonth; d++) {
    87	        const dateStr = `${year}-${String(month+1).padStart(2,'0')}-${String(d).padStart(2,'0')}`;
    88	        const isToday = d === today.getDate() && month === today.getMonth() && year === today.getFullYear();
    89	        const dayBtn = document.createElement('button');
    90	        dayBtn.className = `p-2 rounded-lg text-sm border ${isToday ? 'border-blue-500' : 'border-slate-200'} hover:bg-slate-50`;
    91	        dayBtn.innerHTML = `<div class="font-medium">${d}</div><div id="solde-${dateStr}" class="text-xs mt-1">...</div><div id="pastille-${dateStr}" class="mt-1"><span class="w-2 h-2 inline-block rounded-full bg-gray-300"></span></div>`;
    92	        dayBtn.onclick = () => selectCaisseDay(dateStr);
    93	        container.appendChild(dayBtn);
    94	    }
    95	
    96	    loadCaissePeriode(year, month);
    97	}
    98	
    99	async function loadCaissePeriode(year, month) {
   100	    const debut = `${year}-${String(month+1).padStart(2,'0')}-01`;
   101	    const fin = new Date(year, month+1, 0).toISOString().split('T')[0];
   102	
   103	    try {
   104	        const res = await apiFetch(`/caisse/periode?debut=${debut}&fin=${fin}`);
   105	        if (!Array.isArray(res)) return;
   106	
   107	        res.forEach(item => {
   108	            const soldeEl = document.getElementById(`solde-${item.jour}`);
   109	            const pastilleEl = document.getElementById(`pastille-${item.jour}`);
   110	
   111	            const encaissements = Number(item.encaissements || 0);
   112	            const decaissements = Number(item.decaissements || 0);
   113	            const epargnes = Number(item.epargnes || 0);
   114	            const penalites = Number(item.penalites || 0);
   115	            const ecart = Number(item.ecart || 0);
   116	            const nbMouvements = Number(item.nb_mouvements || 0);
   117	            const solde = encaissements + epargnes + penalites - decaissements;
   118	
   119	            if (soldeEl) {
   120	                soldeEl.textContent = nbMouvements > 0
   121	                    ? new Intl.NumberFormat('fr-FR', { maximumFractionDigits: 0 }).format(solde) + ' F'
   122	                    : '';
   123	            }
   124	
   125	            if (!pastilleEl) return;
   126	
   127	            let couleur = 'bg-gray-300';
   128	            let titre = 'Aucun mouvement';
   129	
   130	            if (item.non_cloture) {
   131	                couleur = 'bg-red-500';
   132	                titre = 'Caisse non clôturée';
   133	            } else if (Math.abs(ecart) > 0.009) {
   134	                couleur = 'bg-orange-500';
   135	                titre = 'Écart de caisse : ' + ecart;
   136	            } else if (nbMouvements > 0) {
   137	                couleur = 'bg-green-500';
   138	                titre = nbMouvements + ' mouvement(s)';
   139	            }
   140	
   141	            pastilleEl.innerHTML = `<span title="${titre}" class="w-2 h-2 inline-block rounded-full ${couleur}"></span>`;
   142	        });
   143	    } catch (e) {
   144	        console.error('loadCaissePeriode', e);
   145	    }
   146	}
   147	
   148	function selectCaisseDay(dateStr) {
   149	    currentCaissePeriod = 'jour';
   150	    currentCaisseDate = new Date(dateStr + 'T00:00:00');
   151	    document.querySelectorAll('.chip').forEach(ch => ch.classList.remove('chip--active'));
   152	    const jourChip = [...document.querySelectorAll('.chip')].find(ch => ch.textContent.trim() === 'Jour');
   153	    if (jourChip) jourChip.classList.add('chip--active');
   154	
   155	    loadCaisseKPIs(dateStr);
   156	    loadCaisseMouvements(dateStr);
   157	}

   159	async function loadCaisseKPIs(date = null) {
   160	    const debut = date || new Date(currentCaisseDate.getFullYear(), currentCaisseDate.getMonth(), 1).toISOString().split('T')[0];
   161	    const fin = date || new Date(currentCaisseDate.getFullYear(), currentCaisseDate.getMonth()+1, 0).toISOString().split('T')[0];
   162	
   163	    try {
   164	        const data = await apiFetch(`/caisse/consolidee?debut=${debut}&fin=${fin}`);
   165	        const f = v => new Intl.NumberFormat('fr-FR',{style:'currency',currency:'XOF',maximumFractionDigits:0}).format(v||0);
   166	
   167	        document.getElementById('caisse-kpis').innerHTML = `
   168	            <div class="kpi-card" style="border-left:3px solid #10b981"><div class="kpi-label">Encaissements</div><div class="kpi-value">${f(data.encaissements)}</div></div>
   169	            <div class="kpi-card" style="border-left:3px solid #ef4444"><div class="kpi-label">Dépenses</div><div class="kpi-value">${f(data.decaissements)}</div></div>
   170	            <div class="kpi-card" style="border-left:3px solid #3b82f6"><div class="kpi-label">Épargnes</div><div class="kpi-value">${f(data.epargnes)}</div></div>
   171	            <div class="kpi-card" style="border-left:3px solid #f59e0b"><div class="kpi-label">Pénalités</div><div class="kpi-value">${f(data.penalites)}</div></div>
   172	            <div class="kpi-card" style="border-left:3px solid #8b5cf6"><div class="kpi-label">Solde</div><div class="kpi-value">${f(data.solde)}</div></div>
   173	        `;
   174	    } catch(e) {
   175	        console.warn('Erreur KPI caisse', e);
   176	    }
   177	}

   179	async function loadCaisseMouvements(date) {
   180	    try {

   188	                ${mouvements.map(m => `<tr><td class="text-xs">${new Date(m.date_mouvement).toLocaleTimeString('fr-FR')}</td><td><span class="badge ${m.type==='encaissement'?'badge-success':'badge-danger'}">${m.type}</span></td><td class="font-semibold">${f(m.montant)}</td><td>${m.mode_paiement||''}</td><td>${m.motif||''}</td></tr>`).join('')}
   189	            </tbody></table>`;
   190	    } catch(e) {
   191	        console.warn('Erreur mouvements', e);
