   195	async function loadRemisesEnAttente() {
   196	    try {
   197	        const remises = await apiFetch('/caisse/remises-en-attente');
   198	        const f = v => new Intl.NumberFormat('fr-FR',{style:'currency',currency:'XOF',maximumFractionDigits:0}).format(v||0);
   199	        const container = document.getElementById('remises-attente');
   200	        if (!container) return;

   204	            return;
   205	        }
   206	
   207	        container.innerHTML = `<h4 class="font-semibold mb-2">Remises en attente de validation</h4>
   208	        <div class="table-wrapper"><table class="min-w-full">
   209	            <thead><tr><th>Agent</th><th>Montant déclaré</th><th>Référence</th><th>Actions</th></tr></thead>
   210	            <tbody>${remises.map(r => `<tr>
   211	                <td>${r.user ? r.user.nom + ' ' + r.user.prenom : '-'}</td>
   212	                <td>${f(r.montant_declare || r.montant)}</td>
   213	                <td class="text-xs">${r.reference}</td>
   214	                <td>
   215	                    <button class="small-btn small-btn--primary" onclick="validerRemiseAction('${r.reference}')">Valider</button>
   216	                    <button class="small-btn small-btn--secondary" onclick="rejeterRemiseAction('${r.reference}')">Rejeter</button>
   217	                </td></tr>`).join('')}
   218	            </tbody></table></div>`;
   219	    } catch(e) {
   220	        console.warn('Erreur remises attente', e);
   221	    }
   222	}

   224	async function validerRemiseAction(reference) {
   225	    if (!(await showConfirm('Valider cette remise ?'))) return;
   226	    apiFetch(`/caisse/remise/${reference}/valider`, {method:'POST', body: JSON.stringify({statut:'valide'})}).then(() => {
   227	        showAlert('Remise validée.');
   228	        loadPage('caisse');
   229	    }).catch(e => showAlert('Erreur : ' + e.message));
   230	}
   231	
   232	async function rejeterRemiseAction(reference) {
   233	    const motif = await showPrompt('Motif du rejet :');
   234	    if (!motif) return;
   235	    apiFetch(`/caisse/remise/${reference}/valider`, {method:'POST', body: JSON.stringify({statut:'rejete', motif_rejet:motif})}).then(() => {
   236	        showAlert('Remise rejetée.');
   237	        loadPage('caisse');
   238	    }).catch(e => showAlert('Erreur : ' + e.message));
   239	}

Écart : '+new Intl.NumberFormat('fr-FR',{style:'currency',currency:'XOF',maximumFractionDigits:0}).format(r.ecart));loadPage('caisse');});}

   248	function exportCaissePeriode() {
   249	    const y = currentCaisseDate.getFullYear();
   250	    const m = currentCaisseDate.getMonth();
   251	    const debut = `${y}-${String(m+1).padStart(2,'0')}-01`;
   252	    const fin = new Date(y, m+1, 0).toISOString().split('T')[0];
   253	    if (typeof apiDownload === 'function') {
   254	        apiDownload(`/caisse/export-periode?debut=${debut}&fin=${fin}`, `caisse_${debut}_au_${fin}.csv`);
   255	    } else {
   256	        window.location.href = `/api/caisse/export-periode?debut=${debut}&fin=${fin}`;
   257	    }
   258	}

===== public/dashboard/js/comptabilite-functions.js =====
     1	function loadComptabilite(c) {
     2	    var journalPage = 1;
     3	    var journalCompte = '';
     4	    var journalDebut = '';
     5	    var journalFin = '';
     6	
     7	    function chargerJournal() {
     8	        var params = '?page=' + journalPage + '&par_page=50';
     9	        if (journalCompte) params += '&compte=' + journalCompte;
    10	        if (journalDebut) params += '&date_debut=' + journalDebut;
    11	        if (journalFin) params += '&date_fin=' + journalFin;
    12	
    13	        apiFetch('/comptabilite/journal' + params).then(function(jr) {
    14	            window._journal = jr.donnees || [];
    15	            var pagination = '<div class="flex gap-2 items-center">' +
    16	                '<button onclick="journalPagePrecedente()" class="px-3 py-1 border rounded-lg text-sm" ' + (journalPage <= 1 ? 'disabled' : '') + '>←</button>' +
    17	                '<span class="text-sm">Page ' + jr.page + ' / ' + jr.total_pages + '</span>' +
    18	                '<button onclick="journalPageSuivante()" class="px-3 py-1 border rounded-lg text-sm" ' + (journalPage >= jr.total_pages ? 'disabled' : '') + '>→</button>' +
    19	                '</div>';
    20	            var paginationEl = document.getElementById('journal-pagination');
    21	            if (paginationEl) paginationEl.innerHTML = pagination;
    22	            var tableEl = document.getElementById('journal-table');
    23	            if (tableEl) {
    24	                tableEl.innerHTML = window._journal.map(function(j) { return '<tr><td>' + j.date_ecriture + '</td><td>' + j.numero_ecriture + '</td><td>' + j.compte_debit + '</td><td>' + j.compte_credit + '</td><td>' + j.libelle + '</td><td class="text-right">' + f(j.montant) + '</td><td>' + j.reference + '</td></tr>'; }).join('');
    25	            }
    26	        });
    27	    }
    28	
    29	    window.journalPagePrecedente = function() { if (journalPage > 1) { journalPage--; chargerJournal(); } };
    30	    window.journalPageSuivante = function() { journalPage++; chargerJournal(); };
    31	
    32	    chargerJournal();
    33	    apiFetch('/comptabilite/balance').then(function(b) {
    34	        apiFetch('/comptabilite/grand-livre').then(function(g) {
    35	            apiFetch('/comptabilite/etats-financiers').then(function(e) {
    36	                function f(v) { return new Intl.NumberFormat('fr-FR', {style:'currency', currency:'XOF', maximumFractionDigits:0}).format(v); }
    37	                
    38	                c.innerHTML = '<div class="fade-in space-y-6">' +
    39	                    '<div class="flex items-center justify-between"><h3 class="text-xl font-bold">Comptabilite</h3>' +
    40	                    '<div class="flex gap-2">' +
    41	'<button onclick="exportJournal()" class="bg-accent text-white px-3 py-2 rounded-lg text-xs font-semibold">Journal CSV</button>' +
    42	'<button onclick="exportGrandLivre()" class="bg-blue-600 text-white px-3 py-2 rounded-lg text-xs font-semibold">Grand Livre CSV</button>' +
    43	'<button onclick="exportEtatsFinanciers()" class="bg-emerald-600 text-white px-3 py-2 rounded-lg text-xs font-semibold">États Financiers CSV</button>' +
    44	'</div></div>' +
    45	                    
    46	                    '<div class="kpi-grid">' +
    47	                        '<div class="kpi-card" style="border-left:3px solid #0066ff"><div class="kpi-label">Total Debit</div><div class="kpi-value">' + f(b.total_debit) + '</div></div>' +
    48	                        '<div class="kpi-card" style="border-left:3px solid #00c853"><div class="kpi-label">Total Credit</div><div class="kpi-value">' + f(b.total_credit) + '</div></div>' +
    49	                        '<div class="kpi-card" style="border-left:3px solid ' + (b.equilibree ? '#00c853' : '#ef4444') + '"><div class="kpi-label">Balance</div><div class="kpi-value">' + (b.equilibree ? 'Equilibree' : 'Desequilibree') + '</div></div>' +
    50	                    '</div>' +
    51	                    
    52	                    '<div class="panel"><h3>Grand Livre</h3><div class="table-wrapper"><table><thead><tr><th>Compte</th><th>Libelle</th><th class="text-right">Debit</th><th class="text-right">Credit</th><th class="text-right">Solde</th></tr></thead><tbody>' +
    53	                    g.map(function(x) { return '<tr><td>' + x.compte + '</td><td>' + x.libelle + '</td><td class="text-right">' + f(x.debit) + '</td><td class="text-right">' + f(x.credit) + '</td><td class="text-right font-semibold">' + f(x.solde) + '</td></tr>'; }).join('') +
    54	                    '</tbody></table></div></div>' +
    55	                    
    56	                    '<div class="panel"><h3>Journal Comptable</h3><div class="table-wrapper" style="max-height:400px;overflow:auto"><table><thead><tr><th>Date</th><th>N°</th><th>Débit</th><th>Crédit</th><th>Libellé</th><th class="text-right">Montant</th><th>Référence</th></tr></thead><tbody>' +
    57	                    (window._journal || []).map(function(j) { return '<tr><td>' + j.date_ecriture + '</td><td>' + j.numero_ecriture + '</td><td>' + j.compte_debit + '</td><td>' + j.compte_credit + '</td><td>' + j.libelle + '</td><td class="text-right">' + f(j.montant) + '</td><td>' + j.reference + '</td></tr>'; }).join('') +
    58	                    '</tbody></table></div></div>' +
    59	                    
    60	                    '<div class="panel"><h3>États Financiers</h3><div class="grid grid-cols-2 gap-4">' +
    61	                        '<div><p class="text-sm text-muted">Chiffre d\'affaires</p><p class="text-lg font-bold">' + f(e.compte_resultat.chiffre_affaires) + '</p></div>' +
    62	                        '<div><p class="text-sm text-muted">Encaissements</p><p class="text-lg font-bold">' + f(e.compte_resultat.encaissements) + '</p></div>' +
    63	                        '<div><p class="text-sm text-muted">Charges</p><p class="text-lg font-bold">' + f(e.compte_resultat.charges) + '</p></div>' +
    64	                        '<div><p class="text-sm text-muted">Bénéfice brut</p><p class="text-lg font-bold">' + f(e.compte_resultat.benefice_brut) + '</p></div>' +
    65	                        '<div><p class="text-sm text-muted">Valeur stock</p><p class="text-lg font-bold">' + f(e.compte_resultat.valeur_stock) + '</p></div>' +
    66	                        '<div><p class="text-sm text-muted">Actif total</p><p class="text-lg font-bold">' + f(e.bilan_synthetique.actif_total) + '</p></div>' +
    67	                    '</div></div>' +
    68	                '</div>';
    69	            }).catch(function() { c.innerHTML = '<div class="panel text-center py-12"><p class="text-muted">Erreur états financiers.</p></div>'; });
    70	        });
    71	    });
    72	}

     1	var debStep1Data = {};

     3	function showAddDebiteurModal(){
     4	    window.debiteurEnModification = null;
     5	    window.debiteurDataData = null;
     6	    var form = document.querySelector("#deb-step1-modal form"); if(form) form.reset();
     7	    document.getElementById('deb-step1-modal').classList.remove('hidden');
     8	    setTimeout(function() {
     9	        if (typeof chargerLocalisations === 'function') chargerLocalisations();
    10	    }, 300);
    11	}

    13	function closeDebModals(){
    14	    document.getElementById('deb-step1-modal').classList.add('hidden');
    15	    document.getElementById('deb-step2-modal').classList.add('hidden');
    16	}

    18	function debStep1(e){
    19	    e.preventDefault();
    20	    debStep1Data = {
    21	        nom: document.getElementById('deb-nom').value,
    22	        prenom: document.getElementById('deb-prenom').value,
    23	        telephone: document.getElementById('deb-telephone').value,
    24	        quartier: typeof getLocalisationComplete === 'function' ? getLocalisationComplete() : '',
    25	        adresse: document.getElementById('deb-adresse').value
    26	    };
    27	    document.getElementById('deb-step1-modal').classList.add('hidden');
    28	    document.getElementById('deb-step2-modal').classList.remove('hidden');
    29	
    30	    apiFetch('/agents').then(function(a){
    31	        var agentSelect = document.getElementById('deb-agent');
    32	        if (agentSelect) {
    33	            agentSelect.innerHTML = '<option value="">Agent assigné...</option>' +
    34	                a.filter(function(x){return x.actif;}).map(function(x){
    35	                    return '<option value="'+x.uuid+'">'+x.nom+' '+(x.prenom||'')+'</option>';
    36	                }).join('');
    37	        }
    38	
    39	        if (window.debiteurEnModification && window.debiteurDataData) {
    40	            var d = window.debiteurDataData;
    41	            var fAct = document.getElementById('deb-activite'); if(fAct) fAct.value = d.activite || '';
    42	            var fRefNom = document.getElementById('deb-ref-nom'); if(fRefNom) fRefNom.value = d.personne_reference_nom || '';
    43	            var fRefTel = document.getElementById('deb-ref-tel'); if(fRefTel) fRefTel.value = d.personne_reference_tel || '';
    44	            var fLim = document.getElementById('deb-limite'); if(fLim) fLim.value = d.credits_autorises || 1;
    45	            if (d.agent_id && agentSelect) agentSelect.value = d.agent_id;
    46	        }
    47	    });
    48	}

    50	function debStepBack(){
    51	    document.getElementById('deb-step2-modal').classList.add('hidden');
    52	    document.getElementById('deb-step1-modal').classList.remove('hidden');

    55	function debStep2(e){
    56	    e.preventDefault();
    57	    var data = Object.assign(debStep1Data, {
    58	        agent_id: document.getElementById('deb-agent').value,
    59	        activite: document.getElementById('deb-activite').value,
    60	        personne_reference_nom: document.getElementById('deb-ref-nom').value,
    61	        personne_reference_tel: document.getElementById('deb-ref-tel').value,
    62	        credits_autorises: parseInt(document.getElementById('deb-limite').value) || 1
    63	    });
    64	
    65	    var isEdit = !!window.debiteurEnModification;
    66	    var url = isEdit ? '/debiteurs/' + window.debiteurEnModification : '/debiteurs';
    67	    var method = isEdit ? 'PUT' : 'POST';
    68	
    69	    apiFetch(url, {method: method, body: JSON.stringify(data)}).then(function(r){
    70	        if(r.success) {
    71	            closeDebModals();
    72	            window.debiteurEnModification = null;
    73	            window.debiteurDataData = null;
    74	            showAlert(isEdit ? 'Débiteur modifié avec succès.' : 'Débiteur créé avec le code ' + (r.code_client || ''));
    75	            loadPage('debiteurs');
    76	        } else {
    77	            showAlert('Erreur: ' + (r.error || 'inconnue'));
    78	        }
    79	    });
    80	}

    82	function loadDebiteurs(c){
    83	    apiFetch('/debiteurs').then(function(d){
    84	        c.innerHTML='<div class="fade-in space-y-6">'+
    85	            '<div class="flex items-center justify-between"><h3 class="text-xl font-bold">Debiteurs</h3>'+
    86	            '<button onclick="showAddDebiteurModal()" class="bg-accent text-white px-4 py-2 rounded-lg text-sm font-semibold hover:bg-blue-700">+ Nouveau Debiteur</button></div>'+
    87	            '<div class="panel"><div class="table-wrapper"><table><thead><tr><th>Code</th><th>Nom</th><th>Telephone</th><th>Quartier</th><th>Agent</th><th>Score</th><th>Actions</th></tr></thead><tbody>'+
    88	            (d.length>0?d.map(function(x){
    89	                var score = x.score_solvabilite !== undefined && x.score_solvabilite !== null ? x.score_solvabilite : 50;
    90	                var scoreColor = score >= 80 ? '#00c853' : score >= 60 ? '#0066ff' : score >= 40 ? '#f59e0b' : '#ef4444';
    91	                return '<tr>'+
    92	                    '<td class="font-mono text-xs">'+(x.code_client||'--')+'</td>'+
    93	                    '<td class="font-medium">'+x.nom+' '+(x.prenom||'')+'</td>'+
    94	                    '<td>'+(x.telephone||'--')+'</td>'+
    95	                    '<td>'+(x.quartier||'--')+'</td>'+
    96	                    '<td>'+(x.agent?x.agent.nom:'--')+'</td>'+
    97	                    '<td><span class="badge" style="background:'+scoreColor+'20;color:'+scoreColor+';font-weight:bold">'+score+'%</span></td>'+
    98	                    '<td><button onclick="voirDebiteur(\''+x.uuid+'\')" class="text-accent text-xs hover:underline font-semibold">Details</button></td>'+
    99	                '</tr>';
   100	            }).join(''):'<tr><td colspan="7" class="text-center text-muted py-4">Aucun debiteur</td></tr>')+
   101	            '</tbody></table></div></div></div>';
   102	    });
   103	}
   104	
   105	function voirDebiteur(uuid){
   106	    var ancienModal = document.getElementById('detail-modal');
   107	    if (ancienModal) ancienModal.remove();
   108	
   109	    Promise.all([
   110	        apiFetch('/debiteurs/'+uuid),
   111	        apiFetch('/debiteurs/'+uuid+'/score').catch(function(){ return {score:50, label:'Neutre', details:{}}; }),
   112	        apiFetch('/debiteurs/'+uuid+'/historique').catch(function(){ return {ventes:[], paiements:[], debiteur:{}}; })
   113	    ]).then(function(resultats){
   114	        var d = resultats[0];
   115	        var scoreData = resultats[1] || {};
   116	        var hist = resultats[2] || {};
   117	
   118	        var score = scoreData.score !== undefined ? scoreData.score : (d.score_solvabilite || 50);
   119	        var label = scoreData.label || 'Neutre';
   120	        var details = scoreData.details || {};
   121	        var scoreColor = score >= 80 ? '#00c853' : score >= 60 ? '#0066ff' : score >= 40 ? '#f59e0b' : '#ef4444';
   122	        var strokeDash = (2 * Math.PI * 40 * score / 100).toFixed(2);
   123	
   124	        var refNom = d.personne_reference_nom || d.reference_nom || d.pers_ref_nom || '--';
   125	        var refTel = d.personne_reference_tel || d.reference_tel || d.pers_ref_tel || '--';
   126	        var creditsAuth = d.credits_autorises !== undefined ? d.credits_autorises : (d.limite_credit || 1);
   127	
   128	        var f = function(v){ return new Intl.NumberFormat('fr-FR',{style:'currency',currency:'XOF',maximumFractionDigits:0}).format(v); };
   129	
   130	        var soldeTotal = (hist.debiteur && hist.debiteur.solde_total !== undefined && hist.debiteur.solde_total !== null)
   131	            ? hist.debiteur.solde_total : null;
   132	
   133	        var html = '<div class="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-start justify-center p-4 bg-black/50 overflow-y-auto py-8" id="detail-modal" onclick="if(event.target===this)this.remove()">';
   134	        html += '<div class="bg-white rounded-2xl p-6 w-full max-w-lg shadow-2xl fade-in my-auto">';
   135	        html += '<div class="flex items-center justify-between mb-4">';
   136	        html += '<h3 class="text-lg font-bold">Fiche Debiteur</h3>';
   137	        html += '<button onclick="document.getElementById(\'detail-modal\').remove()" class="text-slate-400 hover:text-slate-600 font-bold">✕</button>';
   138	        html += '</div>';
   139	
   140	        html += '<div class="flex items-center gap-4 mb-4 p-4 bg-slate-50 rounded-xl">';
   141	        html += '<div style="width:100px;height:100px;position:relative;flex-shrink:0;">';
   142	        html += '<svg viewBox="0 0 100 100" style="width:100%;height:100%;transform:rotate(-90deg)">';
   143	        html += '<circle cx="50" cy="50" r="40" fill="none" stroke="#e2e8f0" stroke-width="10"/>';
   144	        html += '<circle cx="50" cy="50" r="40" fill="none" stroke="'+scoreColor+'" stroke-width="10" stroke-dasharray="'+strokeDash+' 251.33" stroke-linecap="round"/>';
   145	        html += '</svg>';
   146	        html += '<div style="position:absolute;top:50%;left:50%;transform:translate(-50%,-50%);text-align:center;font-weight:bold;font-size:18px;color:'+scoreColor+'">'+score+'%</div>';
   147	        html += '</div>';
   148	        html += '<div><span class="text-sm font-semibold" style="color:'+scoreColor+'">'+label+'</span>';
   149	        html += '<p class="text-xs text-muted mt-1">Score de solvabilité</p>';
   150	        html += '<button onclick="recalculerScore(\''+uuid+'\')" class="mt-2 text-xs text-accent hover:underline font-medium">↺ Recalculer le score</button>';
   151	        html += '</div></div>';
   152	
   153	        if (details && details.comportement_paiement !== undefined) {
   154	            html += '<div class="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2 text-xs mb-4">';
   155	            html += '<div class="p-2 bg-slate-50 rounded-lg"><span class="text-muted">Comportement paiement</span><div class="font-semibold">'+details.comportement_paiement+'%</div></div>';
   156	            html += '<div class="p-2 bg-slate-50 rounded-lg"><span class="text-muted">Sévérité retards</span><div class="font-semibold">'+details.severite_retards+'%</div></div>';
   157	            html += '<div class="p-2 bg-slate-50 rounded-lg"><span class="text-muted">Crédits terminés</span><div class="font-semibold">'+details.credits_termines+'%</div></div>';
   158	            html += '<div class="p-2 bg-slate-50 rounded-lg"><span class="text-muted">Ancienneté</span><div class="font-semibold">'+details.anciennete+'%</div></div>';
   159	            html += '<div class="p-2 bg-slate-50 rounded-lg"><span class="text-muted">Régularité</span><div class="font-semibold">'+details.regularite+'%</div></div>';
   160	            html += '<div class="p-2 bg-slate-50 rounded-lg"><span class="text-muted">Échéances observées</span><div class="font-semibold">'+details.nb_echeances+'</div></div>';
   161	            html += '</div>';
   162	        }
   163	
   164	        html += '<div class="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3 text-sm border-t pt-3">';
   165	        html += '<div><span class="text-muted">Code:</span> <strong>'+(d.code_client||'--')+'</strong></div>';
   166	        html += '<div><span class="text-muted">Nom:</span> <strong>'+d.nom+' '+(d.prenom||'')+'</strong></div>';
   167	        html += '<div><span class="text-muted">Tél:</span> '+(d.telephone||'--')+'</div>';
   168	        html += '<div><span class="text-muted">Quartier:</span> '+(d.quartier||'--')+'</div>';
   169	        html += '<div><span class="text-muted">Adresse:</span> '+(d.adresse||'--')+'</div>';
   170	        html += '<div><span class="text-muted">Activité:</span> '+(d.activite||'--')+'</div>';
   171	        html += '<div><span class="text-muted">Réf.:</span> <strong>'+refNom+'</strong></div>';
   172	        html += '<div><span class="text-muted">Tél réf.:</span> <strong>'+refTel+'</strong></div>';
   173	        html += '<div><span class="text-muted">Agent:</span> '+(d.agent?d.agent.nom:'--')+'</div>';
   174	        html += '<div><span class="text-muted">Crédits autorisés:</span> <strong>'+creditsAuth+'</strong></div>';
   175	        html += '</div>';
   176	
   177	        // Solde restant — affiché même si 0 (correction du bug falsy)
   178	        html += '<div class="mt-3 p-2 rounded-lg text-sm font-semibold '+(soldeTotal > 0 ? 'bg-red-50 text-red-600' : 'bg-green-50 text-green-600')+'">';
   179	        html += 'Solde restant total : ' + (soldeTotal !== null ? f(soldeTotal) : '--');
   180	        html += '</div>';
   181	
   182	        // Historique des achats
   183	        // Situation des échéances
   184	        html += '<div class="mt-3 border-t pt-3">';
   185	        if (hist.ventes && hist.ventes.length > 0) {
   186	            hist.ventes.forEach(function(v) {
   187	                html += '<div class="text-xs">Jours payés : ' + (v.jours_payes || 0) + '/' + (v.jours_total || 0) + '</div>';
   188	                html += '<div class="text-xs">Impayés : ' + (v.jours_impayes || 0) + ' jour(s)</div>';
   189	                html += '<div class="text-xs">Pénalités dues : ' + f(v.penalites_en_attente || 0) + '</div>';
   190	            });
   191	        } else {
   192	            html += '<p class="text-xs text-muted">Aucune échéance</p>';
   193	        }
   194	        html += '</div>';
   195	        
   196	        html += '<div class="border-t pt-3 mt-3">';
   197	        html += '<h4 class="font-semibold text-sm mb-2">Historique des achats</h4>';
   198	        html += (hist.ventes && hist.ventes.length > 0)
   199	            ? '<div class="space-y-1 max-h-32 overflow-y-auto">' + hist.ventes.map(function(v){
   200	                return '<div class="flex justify-between text-xs p-1.5 bg-slate-50 rounded"><span>'+v.date+' | '+(v.produits_resume||v.produit||'--')+'</span><span class="font-semibold">'+f(v.montant_total)+' | '+v.statut+'</span></div>';
   201	              }).join('') + '</div>'
   202	            : '<p class="text-xs text-muted">Aucun achat</p>';
   203	        html += '</div>';
   204	
   205	
   206	        // Historique des paiements
   207	        html += '<div class="mt-3">';
   208	        html += '<h4 class="font-semibold text-sm mb-2">Historique des paiements</h4>';
   209	        html += (hist.paiements && hist.paiements.length > 0)
   210	            ? '<div class="space-y-1 max-h-32 overflow-y-auto">' + hist.paiements.map(function(p){
   211	                return '<div class="flex justify-between text-xs p-1.5 bg-slate-50 rounded"><span>'+p.date+' | '+p.mode+'</span><span class="font-semibold text-green-600">'+f(p.montant)+' | '+p.statut+'</span></div>';
   212	              }).join('') + '</div>'
   213	            : '<p class="text-xs text-muted">Aucun paiement</p>';
   214	        html += '</div>';
   215	
   216	        html += '<div class="mt-4 flex gap-2">';
   217	        html += '<button onclick="modifierDebiteur(\''+uuid+'\',\''+d.nom+'\',\''+(d.telephone||'')+'\')" class="flex-1 px-4 py-2.5 bg-amber-600 text-white rounded-xl text-sm font-semibold">Modifier</button>';
   218	        html += '<button onclick="supprimerDebiteur(\''+uuid+'\',\''+d.nom+'\')" class="flex-1 px-4 py-2.5 bg-red-600 text-white rounded-xl text-sm font-semibold">Supprimer</button>';
   219	        html += '<button onclick="document.getElementById(\'detail-modal\').remove()" class="flex-1 px-4 py-2.5 bg-accent text-white rounded-xl text-sm font-semibold">Fermer</button>';
   220	        html += '</div>';
   221	        html += '</div></div>';
   222	        document.body.insertAdjacentHTML('beforeend', html);
   223	    });
   224	}
   225	
   226	function recalculerScore(uuid){
   227	    apiFetch('/debiteurs/'+uuid+'/score/calculer', {method:'POST'}).then(function(r){
   228	        if (r && r.score !== undefined) {
   229	            showAlert('Score de solvabilité recalculé : ' + r.score + '% (' + (r.label || '') + ')');
   230	            voirDebiteur(uuid);
   231	        } else {
   232	            showAlert('Erreur lors du recalcul du score de solvabilité.');
   233	        }
   234	    });
   235	}
   236	
   237	function modifierDebiteur(uuid) {
   238	    apiFetch('/debiteurs/' + uuid).then(function(d) {
   239	        window.debiteurEnModification = uuid;
   240	        window.debiteurDataData = d;
   241	
   242	        var fNom = document.getElementById('deb-nom'); if(fNom) fNom.value = d.nom || '';
   243	        var fPrenom = document.getElementById('deb-prenom'); if(fPrenom) fPrenom.value = d.prenom || '';
   244	        var fTel = document.getElementById('deb-telephone'); if(fTel) fTel.value = d.telephone || '';
   245	        var fAdresse = document.getElementById('deb-adresse'); if(fAdresse) fAdresse.value = d.adresse || '';
   246	
   247	        var modal = document.getElementById('detail-modal');
   248	        if (modal) modal.remove();
   249	
   250	        var m1 = document.getElementById('deb-step1-modal');
   251	        if (m1) m1.classList.remove('hidden');
   252	
   253	        if (typeof chargerLocalisations === 'function') {
   254	            setTimeout(function(){ chargerLocalisations(); }, 300);
   255	        }
   256	    }).catch(function(err){
   257	        showAlert('Impossible de charger les données du débiteur.');
   258	    });
   259	}
   260	
   261	function supprimerDebiteur(uuid, nom) {
   262	    confirmModal('Supprimer ' + nom + ' ?').then(function(ok){
   263	        if(!ok) return;
   264	        apiFetch('/debiteurs/'+uuid, {method:'DELETE'}).then(function(){
   265	            loadPage('debiteurs');
   266	        });
   267	    });

     3	let agentsGlobaux = [];
     4	let debiteursGlobaux = [];
     5	let chartEpargneEvolution = null;
     6	let chartEpargneRepartition = null;
     7	let chartPenaliteEvolution = null;

    10	function formatMoney(v) {
    11	    return new Intl.NumberFormat('fr-FR', { style: 'currency', currency: 'XOF', maximumFractionDigits: 0 }).format(parseFloat(v || 0));
    12	}
    13	
    14	function formatDate(iso) {
    15	    if (!iso) return '-';
    16	    return new Date(iso).toLocaleDateString('fr-FR', { day: '2-digit', month: 'short', year: 'numeric' });
    17	}
    18	
    19	function destroyChart(chart) {
    20	    if (chart) { chart.destroy(); }
    21	    return null;
    22	}
    23	
    24	async function chargerFiltres() {
    25	    if (agentsGlobaux.length && debiteursGlobaux.length) return;
    26	    try {
    27	        const agents = await apiFetch('/agents');
    28	        const debiteurs = await apiFetch('/debiteurs');
    29	        agentsGlobaux = Array.isArray(agents) ? agents : [];
    30	        debiteursGlobaux = Array.isArray(debiteurs) ? debiteurs : [];
    31	    } catch (e) {
    32	        console.warn('Erreur filtres', e);
    33	    }
    34	}
    35	
    36	function remplirSelects(type) {
    37	    const agentSelect = document.getElementById('filtre-agent-' + type);
    38	    const debiteurSelect = document.getElementById('filtre-debiteur-' + type);
    39	    if (!agentSelect || !debiteurSelect) return;
    40	
    41	    agentSelect.innerHTML = '<option value="">Tous les agents</option>' +
    42	        agentsGlobaux.map(a => `<option value="${a.uuid}">${a.nom} ${a.prenom || ''}</option>`).join('');
    43	
    44	    debiteurSelect.innerHTML = '<option value="">Tous les débiteurs</option>' +
    45	        debiteursGlobaux.map(d => `<option value="${d.uuid}">${d.nom} ${d.prenom || ''}</option>`).join('');
    46	}
    47	
    48	async function initFiltres(type) {
    49	    await chargerFiltres();
    50	    remplirSelects(type);
    51	}
    52	
    53	function getUrlFiltre(type, endpoint) {
    54	    const agentSelect = document.getElementById('filtre-agent-' + type);
    55	    const debiteurSelect = document.getElementById('filtre-debiteur-' + type);
    56	    let url = endpoint + '?';
    57	    if (agentSelect && agentSelect.value) url += 'agent_uuid=' + encodeURIComponent(agentSelect.value) + '&';
    58	    if (debiteurSelect && debiteurSelect.value) url += 'debiteur_uuid=' + encodeURIComponent(debiteurSelect.value) + '&';
    59	    return url;
    60	}
    61	
    62	async function exportEpargnes() {
    63	    const url = getUrlFiltre('epargnes', '/epargnes/export');
    64	    if (typeof apiDownload === 'function') apiDownload(url, 'epargnes.csv');
    65	    else window.location.href = url;
    66	}
    67	
    68	async function exportPenalites() {
    69	    const url = getUrlFiltre('penalites', '/penalites/export');
    70	    if (typeof apiDownload === 'function') apiDownload(url, 'penalites.csv');
    71	    else window.location.href = url;
    72	}
    73	
    74	function groupByDate(list, dateKey, montantKey) {
    75	    const map = {};
    76	    list.forEach(item => {
    77	        const date = item[dateKey] ? new Date(item[dateKey]).toLocaleDateString('fr-FR', { day: '2-digit', month: 'short' }) : 'Inconnu';
    78	        map[date] = (map[date] || 0) + parseFloat(item[montantKey] || 0);
    79	    });
    80	    return map;
    81	}
    82	
    83	async function loadEpargnes(c) {
    84	    const data = await apiFetch('/epargnes');
    85	    if (!Array.isArray(data)) {
    86	        c.innerHTML = '<div class="panel text-center py-12"><p class="text-muted">Erreur chargement épargnes</p></div>';
    87	        return;
    88	    }
    89	
    90	    const totalCollecte = data.filter(e => e.statut === 'collecte').reduce((s, e) => s + parseFloat(e.montant || 0), 0);
    91	    const totalRecupere = data.filter(e => e.statut === 'recuperee').reduce((s, e) => s + parseFloat(e.montant || 0), 0);
    92	    const nbCollectes = data.length;
    93	    const clientsUniques = new Set(data.map(e => e.debiteur_id)).size;
    94	    const collectesParJour = groupByDate(data, 'date_collecte', 'montant');
    95	    const labels = Object.keys(collectesParJour).sort();
    96	    const values = labels.map(l => collectesParJour[l]);
    97	
    98	    const rows = data.slice(0, 50).map(e => {
    99	        const deb = e.debiteur ? (e.debiteur.nom + ' ' + (e.debiteur.prenom || '')) : '-';
   100	        const statut = e.statut === 'recuperee' ? '<span class="badge badge-success">Récupérée</span>' : '<span class="badge badge-info">Collectée</span>';
   101	        return `<tr><td>${deb}</td><td>${formatMoney(e.montant)}</td><td>${formatDate(e.date_collecte)}</td><td>${statut}</td></tr>`;
   102	    }).join('');
   103	
   104	    c.innerHTML = `
   105	        <div class="fade-in space-y-6">
   106	            <div class="flex items-center justify-between">
   107	                <h3 class="text-xl font-bold">Épargne tontine</h3>
   108	            </div>
   109	
   110	            <div class="flex flex-wrap gap-2 items-end">
   111	                <div>
   112	                    <label class="block text-xs text-muted mb-1">Agent</label>
   113	                    <select id="filtre-agent-epargnes" class="small-btn small-btn--secondary"><option value="">Tous les agents</option></select>
   114	                </div>
   115	                <div>
   116	                    <label class="block text-xs text-muted mb-1">Débiteur</label>
   117	                    <select id="filtre-debiteur-epargnes" class="small-btn small-btn--secondary"><option value="">Tous les débiteurs</option></select>
   118	                </div>
   119	                <button class="small-btn small-btn--primary" onclick="exportEpargnes()">📥 Export CSV</button>
   120	            </div>
   121	
   122	            <div class="grid grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-5 gap-4">
   123	                <div class="kpi-card" style="border-left:3px solid #10b981"><div class="kpi-label">Épargne collectée en cours</div><div class="kpi-value">${formatMoney(totalCollecte)}</div></div>
   124	                <div class="kpi-card" style="border-left:3px solid #3b82f6"><div class="kpi-label">Épargne restituée (client)</div><div class="kpi-value">${formatMoney(totalRecupere)}</div></div>
   125	                <div class="kpi-card" style="border-left:3px solid #8b5cf6"><div class="kpi-label">Collectes</div><div class="kpi-value">${nbCollectes}</div></div>
   126	                <div class="kpi-card" style="border-left:3px solid #f59e0b"><div class="kpi-label">Clients actifs</div><div class="kpi-value">${clientsUniques}</div></div>
   127	                <div class="kpi-card" style="border-left:3px solid #ef4444"><div class="kpi-label">Moyenne/jour</div><div class="kpi-value">${formatMoney(totalCollecte / Math.max(1, labels.length))}</div></div>
   128	            </div>
   129	
   130	            <div class="grid lg:grid-cols-2 gap-6">
   131	                <div class="panel p-4">
   132	                    <h4 class="font-semibold mb-3">Évolution des collectes</h4>
   133	                    <div style="position:relative; height:200px;"><canvas id="chartEpargneEvolution"></canvas></div>
   134	                </div>
   135	                <div class="panel p-4">
   136	                    <h4 class="font-semibold mb-3">Répartition</h4>
   137	                    <div style="position:relative; height:200px;"><canvas id="chartEpargneRepartition"></canvas></div>
   138	                </div>
   139	            </div>
   140	
   141	            <div class="panel">
   142	                <div class="table-wrapper">
   143	                    <table>
   144	                        <thead><tr><th>Débiteur</th><th>Montant</th><th>Date</th><th>Statut</th></tr></thead>
   145	                        <tbody>${rows || '<tr><td colspan="4" class="text-center">Aucune épargne</td></tr>'}</tbody>
   146	                    </table>
   147	                </div>
   148	            </div>
   149	        </div>`;
   150	
   151	    initFiltres('epargnes');
   152	
   153	    destroyChart(chartEpargneEvolution);
   154	    if (window.Chart) {
   155	        chartEpargneEvolution = new Chart(document.getElementById('chartEpargneEvolution'), {
   156	            type: 'line',
   157	            data: {
   158	                labels: labels,
   159	                datasets: [{
   160	                    label: 'Montant collecté (FCFA)',
   161	                    data: values,
   162	                    borderColor: '#10b981',
   163	                    backgroundColor: 'rgba(16,185,129,0.1)',
   164	                    fill: true,
   165	                    tension: 0.4,
   166	                    pointRadius: 3,
   167	                    pointBackgroundColor: '#10b981'
   168	                }]
   169	            },
   170	            options: { responsive: true, maintainAspectRatio: false }
   171	        });
   172	    }
   173	
   174	    destroyChart(chartEpargneRepartition);
   175	    if (window.Chart) {
   176	        chartEpargneRepartition = new Chart(document.getElementById('chartEpargneRepartition'), {
   177	            type: 'doughnut',
   178	            data: {
   179	                labels: ['Collectée', 'Récupérée'],
   180	                datasets: [{
   181	                    data: [totalCollecte, totalRecupere],
   182	                    backgroundColor: ['#10b981', '#3b82f6'],
   183	                }]
   184	            },
   185	            options: { responsive: true, maintainAspectRatio: false }
   186	        });
   187	    }
   188	}

   190	async function loadPenalites(c) {
   191	    const data = await apiFetch('/penalites');
   192	    if (!Array.isArray(data)) {
   193	        c.innerHTML = '<div class="panel text-center py-12"><p class="text-muted">Erreur chargement pénalités</p></div>';
   194	        return;
   195	    }
   196	
   197	    const enAttente = data.filter(p => p.statut === 'en_attente');
   198	    const payees = data.filter(p => p.statut === 'payee');
   199	    const refusees = data.filter(p => p.statut === 'refusee');
   200	    const totalEnAttente = enAttente.reduce((s, p) => s + parseFloat(p.montant || 0), 0);
   201	    const totalPaye = payees.reduce((s, p) => s + parseFloat(p.montant || 0), 0);
   202	    const totalRefuse = refusees.reduce((s, p) => s + parseFloat(p.montant || 0), 0);
   203	    const total = totalEnAttente + totalPaye + totalRefuse;
   204	
   205	    const parJour = groupByDate(data, 'date_appliquee', 'montant');
   206	    const labels = Object.keys(parJour).sort();
   207	    const values = labels.map(l => parJour[l]);
   208	
   209	    const rows = enAttente.slice(0, 50).map(p => {
   210	        const deb = p.debiteur ? (p.debiteur.nom + ' ' + (p.debiteur.prenom || '')) : '-';
   211	        return `<tr><td>${deb}</td><td>${formatMoney(p.montant)}</td><td>${p.jours_retard || 0} j</td><td><span class="badge badge-warning">En attente</span></td></tr>`;
   212	    }).join('');
   213	
   214	    c.innerHTML = `
   215	        <div class="fade-in space-y-6">
   216	            <div class="flex items-center justify-between">
   217	                <h3 class="text-xl font-bold">Pénalités à régulariser</h3>
   218	            </div>
   219	
   220	            <div class="flex flex-wrap gap-2 items-end">
   221	                <div>
   222	                    <label class="block text-xs text-muted mb-1">Agent</label>
   223	                    <select id="filtre-agent-penalites" class="small-btn small-btn--secondary"><option value="">Tous les agents</option></select>
   224	                </div>
   225	                <div>
   226	                    <label class="block text-xs text-muted mb-1">Débiteur</label>
   227	                    <select id="filtre-debiteur-penalites" class="small-btn small-btn--secondary"><option value="">Tous les débiteurs</option></select>
   228	                </div>
   229	                <button class="small-btn small-btn--primary" onclick="exportPenalites()">📥 Export CSV</button>
   230	            </div>
   231	
   232	            <div class="grid grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-4 gap-4">
   233	                <div class="kpi-card" style="border-left:3px solid #ef4444"><div class="kpi-label">Total dû</div><div class="kpi-value">${formatMoney(total)}</div></div>
   234	                <div class="kpi-card" style="border-left:3px solid #f59e0b"><div class="kpi-label">En attente</div><div class="kpi-value">${formatMoney(totalEnAttente)}</div></div>
   235	                <div class="kpi-card" style="border-left:3px solid #10b981"><div class="kpi-label">Recouvré</div><div class="kpi-value">${formatMoney(totalPaye)}</div></div>
   236	                <div class="kpi-card" style="border-left:3px solid #6b7280"><div class="kpi-label">Refusé / compensé</div><div class="kpi-value">${formatMoney(totalRefuse)}</div></div>
   237	            </div>
   238	
   239	            <div class="grid lg:grid-cols-2 gap-6">
   240	                <div class="panel p-4">
   241	                    <h4 class="font-semibold mb-3">Pénalités par jour</h4>
   242	                    <div style="position:relative; height:200px;"><canvas id="chartPenaliteEvolution"></canvas></div>
   243	                </div>
   244	                <div class="panel p-4">
   245	                    <h4 class="font-semibold mb-3">Répartition</h4>
   246	                    <div style="position:relative; height:200px;"><canvas id="chartPenaliteRepartition"></canvas></div>
   247	                </div>
   248	            </div>
   249	
   250	            <div class="panel">
   251	                <h4 class="font-semibold p-4 pb-0">Pénalités en attente</h4>
   252	                <div class="table-wrapper">
   253	                    <table>
   254	                        <thead><tr><th>Débiteur</th><th>Montant</th><th>Retard</th><th>Statut</th></tr></thead>
   255	                        <tbody>${rows || '<tr><td colspan="4" class="text-center">Aucune pénalité</td></tr>'}</tbody>
   256	                    </table>
   257	                </div>
   258	            </div>
   259	        </div>`;
   260	
   261	    initFiltres('penalites');
   262	
   263	    destroyChart(chartPenaliteEvolution);
   264	    if (window.Chart) {
   265	        chartPenaliteEvolution = new Chart(document.getElementById('chartPenaliteEvolution'), {
   266	            type: 'line',
   267	            data: {
   268	                labels: labels,
   269	                datasets: [{
   270	                    label: 'Montant pénalités (FCFA)',
   271	                    data: values,
   272	                    borderColor: '#ef4444',
   273	                    backgroundColor: 'rgba(239,68,68,0.1)',
   274	                    fill: true,
   275	                    tension: 0.4,
   276	                    pointRadius: 3,
   277	                    pointBackgroundColor: '#ef4444'
   278	                }]
   279	            },
   280	            options: { responsive: true, maintainAspectRatio: false }
   281	        });
   282	    }
   283	
   284	    destroyChart(chartPenaliteRepartition);
   285	    if (window.Chart) {
   286	        chartPenaliteRepartition = new Chart(document.getElementById('chartPenaliteRepartition'), {
   287	            type: 'doughnut',
   288	            data: {
   289	                labels: ['En attente', 'Recouvrée', 'Refusée'],
   290	                datasets: [{
   291	                    data: [totalEnAttente, totalPaye, totalRefuse],
   292	                    backgroundColor: ['#f59e0b', '#10b981', '#6b7280'],
   293	                }]
   294	            },
   295	            options: { responsive: true, maintainAspectRatio: false }
   296	        });
   297	    }
   298	}
===== public/dashboard/js/localisation-loader.js =====
     1	// === CHARGEUR DE LOCALISATION SIMPLE ET ROBUSTE ===
     2	
     3	function chargerLocalisations() {
     4	    console.log('[LOC] Chargement des localisations...');
     5	    
     6	    var depSelect = document.getElementById('deb-departement');
     7	    var comSelect = document.getElementById('deb-commune');
     8	    var arrSelect = document.getElementById('deb-arrondissement');
     9	    var vilSelect = document.getElementById('deb-village');
    10	    
    11	    if (!depSelect) {
    12	        console.warn('[LOC] #deb-departement introuvable');
    13	        return;
    14	    }
    15	    
    16	    // Charger les départements
    17	    fetch('/api/localisations?type=departement')
    18	        .then(r => r.json())
    19	        .then(data => {
    20	            depSelect.innerHTML = '<option value="">-- Departement --</option>' +
    21	                data.map(d => '<option value="' + d.nom + '">' + d.nom + '</option>').join('');
    22	            console.log('[LOC] ' + data.length + ' departements charges');
    23	        });
    24	    
    25	    // Quand on change de département
    26	    depSelect.onchange = function() {
    27	        comSelect.innerHTML = '<option value="">-- Commune --</option>';
    28	        arrSelect.innerHTML = '<option value="">-- Arrondissement --</option>';
    29	        vilSelect.innerHTML = '<option value="">-- Village/Quartier --</option>';
    30	        
    31	        if (!this.value) return;
    32	        
    33	        fetch('/api/localisations?type=commune&parent=' + encodeURIComponent(this.value))
    34	            .then(r => r.json())
    35	            .then(data => {
    36	                comSelect.innerHTML = '<option value="">-- Commune --</option>' +
    37	                    data.map(c => '<option value="' + c.nom + '">' + c.nom + '</option>').join('');
    38	                console.log('[LOC] ' + data.length + ' communes charges');
    39	            });
    40	    };
    41	    
    42	    // Quand on change de commune
    43	    if (comSelect) comSelect.onchange = function() {
    44	        arrSelect.innerHTML = '<option value="">-- Arrondissement --</option>';
    45	        vilSelect.innerHTML = '<option value="">-- Village/Quartier --</option>';
    46	        
    47	        if (!this.value) return;
    48	        
    49	        fetch('/api/localisations?type=arrondissement&parent=' + encodeURIComponent(this.value))
    50	            .then(r => r.json())
    51	            .then(data => {
    52	                arrSelect.innerHTML = '<option value="">-- Arrondissement --</option>' +
    53	                    data.map(a => '<option value="' + a.nom + '">' + a.nom + '</option>').join('');
    54	                console.log('[LOC] ' + data.length + ' arrondissements charges');
    55	            });
    56	    };
    57	    
    58	    // Quand on change d'arrondissement
    59	    if (arrSelect) arrSelect.onchange = function() {
    60	        vilSelect.innerHTML = '<option value="">-- Village/Quartier --</option>';
    61	        
    62	        if (!this.value) return;
    63	        
    64	        fetch('/api/localisations?type=quartier&parent=' + encodeURIComponent(this.value))
    65	            .then(r => r.json())
    66	            .then(data => {
    67	                vilSelect.innerHTML = '<option value="">-- Village/Quartier --</option>' +
    68	                    data.map(q => '<option value="' + q.nom + '">' + q.nom + '</option>').join('');
    69	                console.log('[LOC] ' + data.length + ' quartiers charges');
    70	            });
    71	    };
    72	}
    73	
    74	function getLocalisationComplete() {
    75	    var parts = [];
    76	    var ids = ['deb-departement', 'deb-commune', 'deb-arrondissement', 'deb-village'];
    77	    ids.forEach(function(id) {
    78	        var el = document.getElementById(id);
    79	        if (el && el.value) parts.push(el.value);
    80	    });
    81	    return parts.join(' > ') || '';
    82	}
    83	
    84	// Exposer globalement
    85	window.chargerLocalisations = chargerLocalisations;
    86	window.getLocalisationComplete = getLocalisationComplete;

     1	function loadNotificationsPage(c){apiFetch('/notifications').then(function(n){var typeIcon={echeance:'📅',retard:'⚠️',stock:'📦',vente:'💰',paiement:'💳'};c.innerHTML='<div class="fade-in space-y-6"><div class="flex items-center justify-between"><h3 class="text-xl font-bold">Notifications</h3><button onclick="marquerNotifsLues()" class="bg-slate-200 text-slate-700 px-4 py-2 rounded-lg text-sm font-semibold hover:bg-slate-300">Tout marquer lu</button></div><div class="space-y-3">'+(n.length>0?n.map(function(x){return '<div class="panel '+(x.lue?'':'border-l-4 border-accent')+'"><div class="flex items-start justify-between"><div><span class="text-xl mr-2">'+(typeIcon[x.type]||'📌')+'</span><span class="font-semibold">'+x.titre+'</span><p class="text-sm text-muted mt-1">'+x.message+'</p></div><div class="text-xs text-muted">'+new Date(x.created_at).toLocaleDateString('fr-FR')+'</div></div></div>';}).join(''):'<div class="panel text-center py-12"><p class="text-muted">Aucune notification</p></div>')+'</div></div>';}).catch(function(e){c.innerHTML='<div class="panel text-center py-12"><p class="text-muted">Erreur de chargement.</p></div>';});}
     2	function marquerNotifsLues(){apiFetch('/notifications/toutes-lues',{method:'PUT',body:'{}'}).then(function(){loadPage('notifications');});}

    21	function loadParametres(c){
    22	    c.innerHTML='<div class="fade-in space-y-6"><h3 class="text-xl font-bold">Parametrage</h3>'+
    23	        '<div class="panel"><div class="flex items-center justify-between mb-4"><h3>Agences</h3><button onclick="showAddAgenceForm()" class="bg-accent text-white px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg text-xs font-semibold hover:bg-blue-700">+ Ajouter</button></div><div class="table-wrapper"><table><thead><tr><th>Nom</th><th>Adresse</th><th>Telephone</th><th>Statut</th></tr></thead><tbody id="agences-tbody"><tr><td colspan="4" class="text-center text-muted py-4">Chargement...</td></tr></tbody></table></div></div>'+
    24	        '<div class="panel"><div class="flex items-center justify-between mb-4"><h3>Utilisateurs</h3><button onclick="showAddUserForm()" class="bg-accent text-white px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg text-xs font-semibold hover:bg-blue-700">+ Ajouter</button></div><div class="table-wrapper"><table><thead><tr><th>Nom</th><th>Email</th><th>Role</th><th>Actions</th></tr></thead><tbody id="users-tbody"><tr><td colspan="4" class="text-center text-muted py-4">Chargement...</td></tr></tbody></table></div></div>'+
    25	        '<div class="panel"><div class="flex items-center justify-between mb-4"><h3>Magasins</h3><button onclick="showAddMagasinForm()" class="bg-accent text-white px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg text-xs font-semibold hover:bg-blue-700">+ Ajouter</button></div><div class="table-wrapper"><table><thead><tr><th>Nom</th><th>Adresse</th><th>Responsable</th><th>Statut</th></tr></thead><tbody id="magasins-tbody"><tr><td colspan="4" class="text-center text-muted py-4">Chargement...</td></tr></tbody></table></div></div>'+
    26	        '<div class="panel max-w-md"><h3>Mon compte</h3><form onsubmit="changeOwnPassword(event)" class="space-y-3 mt-4"><input type="password" id="own-password" placeholder="Nouveau mot de passe" required class="w-full px-4 py-2.5 border border-slate-200 rounded-xl text-sm"><input type="password" id="own-password-confirm" placeholder="Confirmer le mot de passe" required class="w-full px-4 py-2.5 border border-slate-200 rounded-xl text-sm"><div id="param-msg"></div><button type="submit" class="w-full bg-accent text-white px-4 py-2.5 rounded-xl text-sm font-semibold hover:bg-blue-700">Modifier le mot de passe</button></form></div></div>';
    27	    loadParametresData();
    28	}
    29	
    30	function loadParametresData(){
    31	    apiFetch('/parametres').then(function(d){
    32	        var t=document.getElementById('agences-tbody');
    33	        if(t&&d.agences)t.innerHTML=d.agences.length>0?d.agences.map(function(a){return '<tr><td class="font-medium">'+a.nom+'</td><td>'+(a.adresse||'--')+'</td><td>'+(a.telephone||'--')+'</td><td><span class="badge '+(a.actif?'badge-success':'badge-danger')+'">'+(a.actif?'Actif':'Inactif')+'</span></td></tr>';}).join(''):'<tr><td colspan="4" class="text-center text-muted py-4">Aucune agence</td></tr>';
    34	        
    35	        var u=document.getElementById('users-tbody');
    36	        if(u&&d.utilisateurs)u.innerHTML=d.utilisateurs.map(function(x){
    37	            var actions = '<div class="flex gap-1">';
    38	            actions += '<button onclick="openPermissionsModal(\''+x.uuid+'\',\''+(x.nom||'')+'\','+JSON.stringify(x.permissions||{})+')" class="px-2 py-1 bg-blue-100 text-blue-700 rounded text-xs hover:bg-blue-200">Permissions</button>';
    39	            actions += '<button onclick="openPasswordModal(\''+x.uuid+'\',\''+(x.nom||'')+'\')" class="px-2 py-1 bg-amber-100 text-amber-700 rounded text-xs hover:bg-amber-200">MDP</button>';
    40	            if(x.role!=='gerante') actions += '<button onclick="openSuspendreModal(\''+x.uuid+'\',\''+(x.nom||'')+'\','+x.actif+')" class="px-2 py-1 rounded text-xs font-medium '+(x.actif?'bg-red-100 text-red-700 hover:bg-red-200':'bg-green-100 text-green-700 hover:bg-green-200')+'">'+(x.actif?'Suspendre':'Reactivation')+'</button>';
    41	            actions += '</div>';
    42	            
    43	            return '<tr>'+
    44	                '<td class="font-medium text-xs">'+x.nom+' '+(x.prenom||'')+'</td>'+
    45	                '<td class="text-xs">'+x.email+'</td>'+
    46	                '<td><select onchange="updateUser(\''+x.uuid+'\',\'role\',this.value)" class="px-1 py-0.5 border rounded text-xs"><option value="gerante" '+(x.role==='gerante'?'selected':'')+'>Gerante</option><option value="chef_agence" '+(x.role==='chef_agence'?'selected':'')+'>Chef agence</option><option value="agent" '+(x.role==='agent'?'selected':'')+'>Agent</option></select></td>'+
    47	                '<td>'+actions+'</td>'+
    48	                '</tr>';
    49	        }).join('');
    50	        
    51	        var m=document.getElementById('magasins-tbody');
    52	        if(m&&d.magasins)m.innerHTML=d.magasins.length>0?d.magasins.map(function(x){return '<tr><td class="font-medium">'+x.nom+'</td><td>'+(x.adresse||'--')+'</td><td>'+(x.responsable||'--')+'</td><td><span class="badge '+(x.actif?'badge-success':'badge-danger')+'">'+(x.actif?'Actif':'Inactif')+'</span></td></tr>';}).join(''):'<tr><td colspan="4" class="text-center text-muted py-4">Aucun magasin</td></tr>';
    53	    });
    54	}

    56	// === MODAL PERMISSIONS ===
    57	function openPermissionsModal(uuid, nom, permissions) {
    58	    var perms = permissions || {};
    59	    var html = '<div class="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-center justify-center p-4 bg-black/50" id="perm-modal-overlay" onclick="if(event.target===this)closePermissionsModal()">';
    60	    html += '<div class="bg-white rounded-2xl p-6 w-full max-w-md shadow-2xl fade-in max-h-96 overflow-y-auto">';
    61	    html += '<h3 class="text-lg font-bold mb-4">Permissions de ' + nom + '</h3>';
    62	    html += '<div class="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2">';
    63	    allPages.forEach(function(p) {
    64	        var checked = perms[p.id] !== undefined ? perms[p.id] : true;
    65	        html += '<label class="flex items-center gap-2 p-2 bg-slate-50 rounded-lg cursor-pointer hover:bg-slate-100 text-sm"><input type="checkbox" '+(checked?'checked':'')+' onchange="togglePermission(\''+uuid+'\',\''+p.id+'\',this.checked)" class="w-4 h-4 text-accent">'+p.label+'</label>';
    66	    });
    67	    html += '</div>';
    68	    html += '<div class="flex gap-3 pt-4"><button onclick="closePermissionsModal()" class="flex-1 px-4 py-2.5 bg-accent text-white rounded-xl text-sm font-semibold">Fermer</button></div>';
    69	    html += '</div></div>';
    70	    
    71	    var existing = document.getElementById('perm-modal-overlay');
    72	    if(existing) existing.remove();
    73	    document.body.insertAdjacentHTML('beforeend', html);
    74	}
    75	
    76	function closePermissionsModal() {
    77	    var el = document.getElementById('perm-modal-overlay');
    78	    if(el) el.remove();
    79	}
    80	
    81	function togglePermission(uuid, page, acces) {
    82	    apiFetch('/parametres/permissions', {method:'POST', body:JSON.stringify({user_uuid:uuid, page:page, acces:acces})});
    83	}

    86	function openPasswordModal(uuid, nom) {
    87	    var html = '<div class="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-center justify-center p-4 bg-black/50" id="pwd-modal-overlay" onclick="if(event.target===this)closePasswordModal()">';
    88	    html += '<div class="bg-white rounded-2xl p-6 w-full max-w-sm shadow-2xl fade-in">';
    89	    html += '<h3 class="text-lg font-bold mb-2">Modifier le mot de passe</h3>';
    90	    html += '<p class="text-sm text-muted mb-4">Utilisateur : <strong>' + nom + '</strong></p>';
    91	    html += '<input type="password" id="modal-new-pwd" placeholder="Nouveau mot de passe" class="w-full px-4 py-2.5 border border-slate-200 rounded-xl text-sm mb-3">';
    92	    html += '<div class="flex gap-3"><button onclick="closePasswordModal()" class="flex-1 px-4 py-2.5 border border-slate-200 rounded-xl text-sm">Annuler</button>';
    93	    html += '<button onclick="submitPasswordChange(\''+uuid+'\')" class="flex-1 px-4 py-2.5 bg-accent text-white rounded-xl text-sm font-semibold">Enregistrer</button></div>';
    94	    html += '</div></div>';
    95	    
    96	    var existing = document.getElementById('pwd-modal-overlay');
    97	    if(existing) existing.remove();
    98	    document.body.insertAdjacentHTML('beforeend', html);
    99	}

   102	    var el = document.getElementById('pwd-modal-overlay');
   103	    if(el) el.remove();

   105	
   106	function submitPasswordChange(uuid) {
   107	    var pwd = document.getElementById('modal-new-pwd').value;
   108	    if(!pwd) { showAlert('Veuillez saisir un mot de passe.'); return; }
   109	    apiFetch('/agents/'+uuid, {method:'PUT', body:JSON.stringify({password:pwd})}).then(function(r){
   110	        if(r.success) { closePasswordModal(); showAlert('Mot de passe modifie'); }
   111	    });
   112	}

   114	// === MODAL SUSPENDRE ===
   115	function openSuspendreModal(uuid, nom, actif) {
   116	    var action = actif ? 'Suspendre' : 'Reactivation';
   117	    var html = '<div class="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-center justify-center p-4 bg-black/50" id="susp-modal-overlay" onclick="if(event.target===this)closeSuspendreModal()">';
   118	    html += '<div class="bg-white rounded-2xl p-6 w-full max-w-sm shadow-2xl fade-in text-center">';
   119	    html += '<div class="text-4xl mb-3">'+(actif?'🔒':'🔓')+'</div>';
   120	    html += '<h3 class="text-lg font-bold mb-2">' + action + ' l\'utilisateur ?</h3>';
   121	    html += '<p class="text-sm text-muted mb-6"><strong>' + nom + '</strong> sera ' + (actif ? 'bloque et ne pourra plus se connecter' : 'debloque et pourra se reconnecter') + '.</p>';
   122	    html += '<div class="flex gap-3"><button onclick="closeSuspendreModal()" class="flex-1 px-4 py-2.5 border border-slate-200 rounded-xl text-sm">Annuler</button>';
   123	    html += '<button onclick="submitSuspendre(\''+uuid+'\','+actif+')" class="flex-1 px-4 py-2.5 '+(actif?'bg-red-600':'bg-green-600')+' text-white rounded-xl text-sm font-semibold">Confirmer</button></div>';
   124	    html += '</div></div>';
   125	    
   126	    var existing = document.getElementById('susp-modal-overlay');
   127	    if(existing) existing.remove();
   128	    document.body.insertAdjacentHTML('beforeend', html);
   129	}
   130	
   131	function closeSuspendreModal() {
   132	    var el = document.getElementById('susp-modal-overlay');
   133	    if(el) el.remove();
   134	}

   136	function submitSuspendre(uuid, actif) {
   137	    apiFetch('/agents/'+uuid, {method:'PUT', body:JSON.stringify({actif:!actif})}).then(function(r){
   138	        if(r.success) { closeSuspendreModal(); loadParametresData(); }
   139	    });
   140	}

   142	function updateUser(uuid, field, value) {
   143	    apiFetch('/agents/'+uuid, {method:'PUT', body:JSON.stringify({[field]:value})}).then(function(r){
   144	        if(!r.success) showAlert('Une erreur est survenue. Veuillez réessayer.');
   145	    });
   146	}

   162	    row.innerHTML = '<tr><td colspan="4"><form onsubmit="submitUser(event)" class="flex gap-2 p-2"><input type="text" id="new-user-nom" placeholder="Nom *" required class="w-24 px-2 py-1 border rounded text-xs"><input type="text" id="new-user-prenom" placeholder="Prenom" class="w-20 px-2 py-1 border rounded text-xs"><input type="email" id="new-user-email" placeholder="Email *" required class="w-36 px-2 py-1 border rounded text-xs"><select id="new-user-role" class="px-2 py-1 border rounded text-xs"><option value="agent">Agent</option><option value="chef_agence">Chef agence</option><option value="gerante">Gerante</option></select><input type="password" id="new-user-password" placeholder="MDP" value="agent123" class="w-16 px-2 py-1 border rounded text-xs"><button type="submit" class="bg-green-600 text-white px-2 py-1 rounded text-xs">OK</button><button type="button" onclick="loadParametresData()" class="bg-slate-300 px-2 py-1 rounded text-xs">X</button></form></td></tr>';

   164	function submitUser(e){
   165	    e.preventDefault();

   167	}

===== public/dashboard/js/produits-functions.js =====
     1	function loadProduits(c){
     2	    apiFetch('/produits').then(function(p){
     3	        function f(v){return new Intl.NumberFormat('fr-FR',{style:'currency',currency:'XOF',maximumFractionDigits:0}).format(v||0);}
     4	        
     5	        c.innerHTML='<div class="fade-in space-y-6">'+
     6	            '<div class="flex items-center justify-between">'+
     7	                '<h3 class="text-xl font-bold">Produits ('+p.length+')</h3>'+
     8	                '<button onclick="showAddProduitModal()" class="bg-accent text-white px-4 py-2 rounded-lg text-sm font-semibold hover:bg-blue-700">+ Nouveau Produit</button>'+
     9	            '</div>'+
    10	            '<div class="panel">'+
    11	                '<div class="flex items-center justify-between mb-4">'+
    12	                    '<h3>Liste des produits</h3>'+
    13	                    '<input type="text" id="prod-search" placeholder="Rechercher..." onkeyup="filterProduits()" class="px-4 py-2 border border-slate-200 rounded-xl text-sm w-64">'+
    14	                '</div>'+
    15	                '<div class="table-wrapper">'+
    16	                    '<table><thead><tr><th>Produit</th><th>Prix Achat</th><th>Prix Vente</th><th>Benefice</th><th>Marge</th><th>Stock</th><th>Actions</th></tr></thead>'+
    17	                    '<tbody id="produits-tbody">'+
    18	                        p.map(function(x){
    19	                            var benefice = (x.prix_vente||0) - (x.prix_achat||0);
    20	                            var marge = (x.prix_achat > 0) ? ((benefice / x.prix_achat) * 100).toFixed(1) + '%' : '--';
    21	                            return '<tr>'+
    22	                                '<td class="font-medium">'+x.nom+'</td>'+
    23	                                '<td><input type="number" value="'+(x.prix_achat||0)+'" onchange="updateProduitPrix(\''+x.uuid+'\',\'prix_achat\',this.value)" class="w-24 px-2 py-1 border rounded text-xs text-right"></td>'+
    24	                                '<td><input type="number" value="'+(x.prix_vente||0)+'" onchange="updateProduitPrix(\''+x.uuid+'\',\'prix_vente\',this.value)" class="w-24 px-2 py-1 border rounded text-xs text-right"></td>'+
    25	                                '<td class="font-semibold text-green-600">'+f(benefice)+'</td>'+
    26	                                '<td class="text-xs">'+marge+'</td>'+
    27	                                '<td><span class="badge '+(x.stock<=x.seuil_alerte?'badge-danger':'badge-success')+'">'+(x.stock||0)+'</span></td>'+
    28	                                '<td><div class="flex gap-1">'+
    29	                                    '<button onclick="categoriserProduit(\''+x.uuid+'\',\''+x.nom+'\')" class="text-blue-600 text-xs">Cat.</button>'+
    30	                                    '<button onclick="supprimerProduit(\''+x.uuid+'\',\''+x.nom+'\')" class="text-red-600 text-xs">Suppr.</button>'+
    31	                                '</div></td>'+
    32	                            '</tr>';
    33	                        }).join('')+
    34	                    '</tbody></table>'+
    35	                '</div>'+
    36	            '</div>'+
    37	            '<div class="panel max-w-md">'+
    38	                '<h3>Resume des benefices</h3>'+
    39	                '<div class="grid grid-cols-3 gap-3 mt-4">'+
    40	                    '<div class="text-center"><span class="text-xs text-muted">Valeur achat</span><div class="font-bold">'+f(p.reduce((s,x)=>s+(x.prix_achat||0)*(x.stock||0),0))+'</div></div>'+
    41	                    '<div class="text-center"><span class="text-xs text-muted">Valeur vente</span><div class="font-bold text-green-600">'+f(p.reduce((s,x)=>s+(x.prix_vente||0)*(x.stock||0),0))+'</div></div>'+
    42	                    '<div class="text-center"><span class="text-xs text-muted">Benefice potentiel</span><div class="font-bold text-accent">'+f(p.reduce((s,x)=>s+((x.prix_vente||0)-(x.prix_achat||0))*(x.stock||0),0))+'</div></div>'+
    43	                '</div>'+
    44	            '</div>'+
    45	        '</div>';
    46	    });
    47	}
    48	
    49	function filterProduits(){
    50	    var q = document.getElementById('prod-search').value.toLowerCase();
    51	    document.querySelectorAll('#produits-tbody tr').forEach(function(row){
    52	        var nom = row.cells[0].textContent.toLowerCase();
    53	        row.style.display = nom.includes(q) ? '' : 'none';
    54	    });
    55	}
    56	
    57	function updateProduitPrix(uuid, champ, valeur){
    58	    var data = {};
    59	    data[champ] = parseFloat(valeur) || 0;
    60	    apiFetch('/produits/'+uuid, {method:'PUT', body:JSON.stringify(data)}).then(function(){
    61	        loadPage('produits');
    62	    });
    63	}
    64	
    65	async function categoriserProduit(uuid, nom){
    66	    // Ouvrir un prompt avec les catégories existantes ou créer
    67	    var categories = ['Riz','Sucre','Huile','Tomate','Boisson','Lait','Sardine','Pates','Farine','Savon','Autre'];
    68	    var choix = await showPrompt('Catégorie pour ' + nom + ' :\n' + categories.join(', '), ''); if(!choix) return;
    69	    if(!choix) return;
    70	    apiFetch('/produits/'+uuid, {method:'PUT', body:JSON.stringify({categorie_nom:choix})}).then(function(){
    71	        loadPage('produits');
    72	    });
    73	}
    74	
    75	function supprimerProduit(uuid, nom){
    76	    confirmModal('Supprimer '+nom+' ?').then(function(ok){
    77	        if(!ok) return;
    78	        apiFetch('/produits/'+uuid, {method:'DELETE'}).then(function(){
    79	            loadPage('produits');
    80	        });
    81	    });
    82	}

    84	function showAddProduitModal(){document.getElementById('add-produit-modal').classList.remove('hidden');}
    85	function closeAddProduitModal(){document.getElementById('add-produit-modal').classList.add('hidden');}
    86	function createProduit(e){
    87	    e.preventDefault();
    88	    var data = {
    89	        nom: document.getElementById('prod-nom').value,
    90	        prix_achat: parseFloat(document.getElementById('prod-prix-achat').value)||0,
    91	        prix_vente: parseFloat(document.getElementById('prod-prix-vente').value)||0,
    92	        seuil_alerte: parseInt(document.getElementById('prod-seuil').value)||5
    93	    };
    94	    apiFetch('/produits', {method:'POST', body:JSON.stringify(data)}).then(function(){
    95	        closeAddProduitModal();
    96	        loadPage('produits');
    97	    });
    98	}

     1	function loadRapports(c){c.innerHTML='<div class="fade-in space-y-6"><h3 class="text-xl font-bold">Rapports et Exports</h3><div class="row-grid"><div class="panel text-center py-8"><div class="text-4xl mb-3">📊</div><h3>Rapport Ventes</h3><p class="text-muted text-sm mb-4">Exportez la liste complete des ventes</p><a href="/api/rapports/ventes?format=csv" onclick="this.href=this.href+&#39;&token=&#39;+localStorage.getItem(&#39;sourougnon_token&#39;)" target="_blank" class="inline-block bg-accent text-white px-6 py-3 rounded-xl text-sm font-semibold hover:bg-blue-700">Export CSV Ventes</a></div><div class="panel text-center py-8"><div class="text-4xl mb-3">💳</div><h3>Rapport Paiements</h3><p class="text-muted text-sm mb-4">Exportez l historique des encaissements</p><a href="/api/rapports/recouvrements?format=csv" onclick="this.href=this.href+&#39;&token=&#39;+localStorage.getItem(&#39;sourougnon_token&#39;)" target="_blank" class="inline-block bg-accent text-white px-6 py-3 rounded-xl text-sm font-semibold hover:bg-blue-700">Export CSV Paiements</a></div><div class="panel text-center py-8"><div class="text-4xl mb-3">📋</div><h3>Resume Mensuel</h3><p class="text-muted text-sm mb-4">Synthese des performances du mois</p><a href="/api/rapports/resume" onclick="event.preventDefault();loadResume(this)" class="inline-block bg-accent text-white px-6 py-3 rounded-xl text-sm font-semibold hover:bg-blue-700">Voir le resume</a></div></div><div id="resume-content"></div></div>';}

===== public/dashboard/js/recherche-globale.js =====
     1	// === MOTEUR DE RECHERCHE GLOBALE ===
     2	var rechercheTimeout;
     3	
     4	function rechercheGlobale(query) {
     5	    clearTimeout(rechercheTimeout);
     6	    var resultsDiv = document.getElementById('search-results');
     7	    if (!resultsDiv) return;
     8	    
     9	    if (!query || query.length < 2) {
    10	        resultsDiv.classList.add('hidden');
    11	        return;
    12	    }
    13	    
    14	    rechercheTimeout = setTimeout(function() {
    15	        apiFetch('/recherche?q=' + encodeURIComponent(query)).then(function(data) {
    16	            if (!data || (!data.debiteurs.length && !data.ventes.length && !data.agents.length && !data.produits.length)) {
    17	                resultsDiv.innerHTML = '<div class="p-4 text-sm text-muted text-center">Aucun resultat pour "' + query + '"</div>';
    18	                resultsDiv.classList.remove('hidden');
    19	                return;
    20	            }
    21	            
    22	            var html = '';
    23	            
    24	            if (data.debiteurs && data.debiteurs.length > 0) {
    25	                html += '<div class="p-2 text-xs font-semibold text-muted uppercase">Debiteurs (' + data.debiteurs.length + ')</div>';
    26	                data.debiteurs.forEach(function(d) {
    27	                    html += '<div class="px-4 py-2 hover:bg-slate-50 cursor-pointer text-sm" onclick="loadPage(\'debiteurs\');document.getElementById(\'search-results\').classList.add(\'hidden\')">' +
    28	                        '<span class="font-medium">' + d.nom + ' ' + (d.prenom||'') + '</span> ' +
    29	                        '<span class="text-muted text-xs">' + (d.quartier||'') + ' | ' + (d.telephone||'') + '</span></div>';
    30	                });
    31	            }
    32	            
    33	            if (data.agents && data.agents.length > 0) {
    34	                html += '<div class="p-2 text-xs font-semibold text-muted uppercase border-t">Agents (' + data.agents.length + ')</div>';
    35	                data.agents.forEach(function(a) {
    36	                    html += '<div class="px-4 py-2 hover:bg-slate-50 cursor-pointer text-sm" onclick="loadPage(\'agents\');document.getElementById(\'search-results\').classList.add(\'hidden\')">' +
    37	                        '<span class="font-medium">' + a.nom + ' ' + (a.prenom||'') + '</span> ' +
    38	                        '<span class="text-muted text-xs">' + a.email + ' | ' + a.role + '</span></div>';
    39	                });
    40	            }
    41	            
    42	            if (data.ventes && data.ventes.length > 0) {
    43	                html += '<div class="p-2 text-xs font-semibold text-muted uppercase border-t">Ventes (' + data.ventes.length + ')</div>';
    44	                data.ventes.forEach(function(v) {
    45	                    html += '<div class="px-4 py-2 hover:bg-slate-50 cursor-pointer text-sm" onclick="loadPage(\'ventes\');document.getElementById(\'search-results\').classList.add(\'hidden\')">' +
    46	                        '<span class="font-medium">' + (v.debiteur_nom||'Client') + '</span> ' +
    47	                        '<span class="text-muted text-xs">' + v.type_vente + ' | ' + new Intl.NumberFormat('fr-FR',{style:'currency',currency:'XOF'}).format(v.montant_total||0) + '</span></div>';
    48	                });
    49	            }
    50	            
    51	            if (data.produits && data.produits.length > 0) {
    52	                html += '<div class="p-2 text-xs font-semibold text-muted uppercase border-t">Produits (' + data.produits.length + ')</div>';
    53	                data.produits.forEach(function(p) {
    54	                    html += '<div class="px-4 py-2 hover:bg-slate-50 cursor-pointer text-sm" onclick="loadPage(\'produits\');document.getElementById(\'search-results\').classList.add(\'hidden\')">' +
    55	                        '<span class="font-medium">' + p.nom + '</span> ' +
    56	                        '<span class="text-muted text-xs">Stock: ' + (p.stock||0) + ' | Prix: ' + new Intl.NumberFormat('fr-FR',{style:'currency',currency:'XOF'}).format(p.prix_vente||0) + '</span></div>';
    57	                });
    58	            }
    59	            
    60	            resultsDiv.innerHTML = html;
    61	            resultsDiv.classList.remove('hidden');
    62	        }).catch(function(e) {
    63	            console.error('Erreur recherche:', e);
    64	        });
    65	    }, 300);
    66	}
    67	
    68	// Fermer les resultats si on clique ailleurs
    69	document.addEventListener('click', function(e) {
    70	    var resultsDiv = document.getElementById('search-results');
    71	    var searchInput = document.getElementById('search-input');
    72	    if (resultsDiv && !resultsDiv.contains(e.target) && e.target !== searchInput) {
    73	        resultsDiv.classList.add('hidden');
    74	    }
    75	});
===== public/dashboard/js/remboursement-functions.js =====
     1	function loadRemboursements(c) {
     2	    apiFetch('/remboursements').then(function(d) {
     3	        function f(v) { return new Intl.NumberFormat('fr-FR', {style:'currency', currency:'XOF', maximumFractionDigits:0}).format(v); }
     4	
     5	        var enAttente = d.filter(function(x) { return x.statut === 'en_attente'; });
     6	        var traitees = d.filter(function(x) { return x.statut !== 'en_attente'; });
     7	
     8	        var estGerante = currentUser && (currentUser.role === 'gerante' || currentUser.role === 'gérante');
     9	
    10	        c.innerHTML = '<div class="fade-in space-y-6">' +
    11	            '<div class="flex items-center justify-between"><h3 class="text-xl font-bold">Remboursements</h3></div>' +
    12	
    13	            '<div class="panel"><h3>Demandes en attente</h3><div class="table-wrapper"><table><thead><tr>' +
    14	            '<th>Débiteur</th><th>Vente</th><th>Crédit payé</th><th>Remboursement (50%)</th><th>Épargne (100%)</th><th>Demandeur</th><th>Actions</th>' +
    15	            '</tr></thead><tbody>' +
    16	            (enAttente.length > 0 ? enAttente.map(function(x) {
    17	                var v = x.vente || {};
    18	                var deb = v.debiteur || {};
    19	                var btnValider = estGerante ? '<button onclick="validerRemboursement(\''+x.uuid+'\')" class="text-green-600 text-xs font-semibold px-2 py-1 bg-green-50 rounded">Valider</button>' : '';
    20	                var btnRejeter = estGerante ? '<button onclick="rejeterRemboursement(\''+x.uuid+'\')" class="text-red-600 text-xs font-semibold px-2 py-1 bg-red-50 rounded">Rejeter</button>' : '';
    21	                return '<tr><td class="font-medium">'+(deb.nom || '--')+'</td><td class="font-mono text-xs">'+(v.uuid ? v.uuid.substring(0,8) : '--')+'</td><td>'+f(x.montant_credit_paye)+'</td><td>'+f(x.montant_rembourse)+'</td><td>'+f(x.montant_epargne_rembourse)+'</td><td>'+(x.demandeur ? x.demandeur.nom : '--')+'</td><td><div class="flex gap-1">'+btnValider+' '+btnRejeter+'</div></td></tr>';
    22	            }).join('') : '<tr><td colspan="7" class="text-center text-muted py-4">Aucune demande</td></tr>') +
    23	            '</tbody></table></div></div>' +
    24	
    25	            '<div class="panel"><h3>Historique traité</h3><div class="table-wrapper"><table><thead><tr>' +
    26	            '<th>Débiteur</th><th>Statut</th><th>Commentaire</th><th>Validée le</th>' +
    27	            '</tr></thead><tbody>' +
    28	            (traitees.length > 0 ? traitees.map(function(x) {
    29	                var v = x.vente || {};
    30	                var deb = v.debiteur || {};
    31	                return '<tr><td class="font-medium">'+(deb.nom || '--')+'</td><td><span class="badge '+(x.statut === 'validee' ? 'badge-success' : 'badge-danger')+'">'+x.statut+'</span></td><td>'+(x.commentaire_gerante || '--')+'</td><td>'+(x.date_validation ? x.date_validation.substring(0,10) : '--')+'</td></tr>';
    32	            }).join('') : '<tr><td colspan="4" class="text-center text-muted py-4">Aucun historique</td></tr>') +
    33	            '</tbody></table></div></div>' +
    34	        '</div>';
    35	    }).catch(function() {
    36	        c.innerHTML = '<div class="panel text-center py-12"><p class="text-muted">Erreur chargement.</p></div>';
    37	    });
    38	}
    39	
    40	async function validerRemboursement(uuid) {
    41	    var commentaire = await showPrompt('Commentaire gérante (optionnel) :', '');
    42	    apiFetch('/remboursements/' + uuid + '/valider', {
    43	        method: 'PUT',
    44	        body: JSON.stringify({commentaire: commentaire || ''})
    45	    }).then(function() {
    46	        loadPage('remboursements');
    47	    }).catch(function(e) {
    48	        showAlert(e.message);
    49	    });
    50	}
    51	
    52	async function rejeterRemboursement(uuid) {
    53	    var commentaire = await showPrompt('Motif du rejet :', '');
    54	    apiFetch('/remboursements/' + uuid + '/rejeter', {
    55	        method: 'PUT',
    56	        body: JSON.stringify({commentaire: commentaire || ''})
    57	    }).then(function() {
    58	        loadPage('remboursements');
    59	    }).catch(function(e) {
    60	        showAlert(e.message);
    61	    });
    62	}
